          try {
            startRide(zone, sc);
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('train ride failed', err);
            rideActive = false;
            try {
              sc.scene.resume();
            } catch {}
          }
        }
        function mixColor(a, b, t) {
          const p = (h) => [1, 3, 5].map((i) => parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16)),
            x = p(a),
            y = p(b);
          return (
            '#' +
            x
              .map((v, i) =>
                Math.round(v + (y[i] - v) * t)
                  .toString(16)
                  .padStart(2, '0'),
              )
              .join('')
          );
        }
        function startRide(zone, sc) {
          const toMine = zone.to === 'mining-town',
            dest = toMine ? 'Minerals Town' : 'English Town',
            origin = toMine ? 'English Town' : 'Minerals Town',
            dir = toMine ? 1 : -1;
          const town = {
              sky: '#6fbcff',
              sky2: '#dff3ff',
              far: '#8fb7a8',
              hill: '#79b27e',
              tree: '#3f8a4f',
              ground: '#8cc56d',
            },
            mine = {
              sky: '#8497a8',
              sky2: '#d3dce2',
              far: '#7d8e9b',
              hill: '#6b7f8b',
              tree: '#4a6670',
              ground: '#8b8a80',
            };
          const P0 = toMine ? town : mine,
            P1 = toMine ? mine : town;
          const checked = count('boarding_pass') > 0;
          try {
            sc.scene.pause();
          } catch {}
          const root = document.createElement('div');
          root.id = 'lq-train';
          root.innerHTML =
            '<canvas></canvas><div id="lq-train-hdr"></div><div id="lq-train-box"><b id="lq-train-name"></b><p id="lq-train-line"></p><button type="button" id="lq-train-btn" hidden>[E] Show ticket</button></div>';
          document.body.appendChild(root);
          const cv = root.querySelector('canvas'),
            cx = cv.getContext('2d'),
            hdr = root.querySelector('#lq-train-hdr'),
            box = root.querySelector('#lq-train-box'),
            nameEl = root.querySelector('#lq-train-name'),
            lineEl = root.querySelector('#lq-train-line'),
            btn = root.querySelector('#lq-train-btn');
          requestAnimationFrame(() => root.classList.add('on'));
          const say = (who, en, vi, speak) => {
            nameEl.textContent = who;
            lineEl.innerHTML = esc(en) + (vi ? '<small>' + esc(vi) + '</small>' : '');
            box.classList.add('show');
            if (speak)
              try {
                speechService.speak(en);
              } catch {}
          };
          const size = () => {
            cv.width = innerWidth;
            cv.height = innerHeight;
          };
          size();
          addEventListener('resize', size);
          const stops = [
            { x: 72, l: 'Good day. Tickets, please.' },
            { x: 142, l: 'Thank you, sir. Have a nice trip.' },
            { x: 212, you: true },
            { x: 282, l: 'Tickets, please... Thank you, young man.' },
          ];
          let phase = 'depart',
            pt = 0,
            speed = 0,
            scroll = 0,
            condX = -30,
            si = 0,
            last = performance.now(),
            raf = 0,
            done = false,
            prog = 0;
          const setPhase = (p) => {
            phase = p;
            pt = 0;
          };
          const nextStop = () => {
            si++;
            box.classList.remove('show');
            setPhase(si >= stops.length ? 'exit' : 'walk');
          };
          function inspect() {
            if (phase !== 'ask') return;
            btn.hidden = true;
            if (count('rail_ticket') > 0) {
              moveItem('rail_ticket', -1);
              moveItem('boarding_pass', 1);
              log('travel', 0, 'Ticket checked on the train');
              save();
            }
            railPop('✅', 'Ticket checked');
            say(
              'Conductor',
              'Thank you! Enjoy your trip to ' + dest + '.',
              'Thank you! Have a pleasant trip.',
              true,
            );
            setPhase('check');
          }
          const onKey = (ev) => {
            ev.preventDefault();
            ev.stopImmediatePropagation();
            if ((ev.key === 'e' || ev.key === 'E') && !ev.repeat) inspect();
          };
          addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
          btn.onclick = inspect;
          function finish() {
            if (done) return;
            done = true;
            cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
            removeEventListener('resize', size);
            root.remove();
            if (count('boarding_pass') > 0) {
              moveItem('boarding_pass', -1);
              log('travel', 0, 'Train trip: ' + origin + ' → ' + dest);
              save();
            }
            rideActive = false;
            try {
              sc.scene.resume();
            } catch {}
            sc.switchArea(zone.to, zone.spawn, zone.label);
          }
          function update(dt) {
            pt += dt;
            scroll += speed * dt * 140;
            if (phase !== 'ask') prog = Math.min(1, prog + dt / 14);
            if (phase === 'depart') {
              speed = Math.min(1, pt / 1.2);
              hdr.textContent = '🚆 Train leaving ' + origin + ' station' + '…';
              if (pt > 1.4) {
                hdr.textContent = '🚆 Train heading to ' + dest + ' station' + '…';
                setPhase('walk');
              }
            } else if (phase === 'walk') {
              const st = stops[si];
              condX = Math.min(st.x, condX + 62 * dt);
              if (condX >= st.x) {
                if (st.you && !checked) {
                  setPhase('ask');
                  say(
                    'Conductor',
                    'Tickets, please!',
                    'Ticket check! Press E to show your ticket.',
                    true,
                  );
                  btn.hidden = false;
                } else {
                  if (st.l) say('Conductor', st.l, '', false);
                  setPhase('pause');
                }
              }
            } else if (phase === 'pause') {
              if (pt > 1.1) nextStop();
            } else if (phase === 'check') {
              if (pt > 1.8) nextStop();
            } else if (phase === 'exit') {
              condX += 62 * dt;
              if (condX > 430) setPhase('cruise');
            } else if (phase === 'cruise') {
              hdr.textContent = '🚉 Arriving at ' + dest + ' station' + '…';
              if (pt > 2.4) {
                setPhase('arrive');
                root.classList.remove('on');
              }
            } else if (phase === 'arrive') {
              speed = Math.max(0.15, speed - dt * 0.8);
              if (pt > 0.7) finish();
            }
          }
          const R = (x, y, w, h, c) => {
            cx.fillStyle = c;
            cx.fillRect(Math.round(x), Math.round(y), Math.round(w), Math.round(h));
          };
          const shade = (c, k) => mixColor(c, '#000000', k);
          function seat(sx) {
            R(sx - 17, 126, 8, 40, '#2a6670');
            R(sx - 17, 126, 2, 40, '#3a8590');
            R(sx - 18, 120, 10, 10, '#3a8590');
            R(sx - 18, 120, 10, 2, '#52a3ad');
            R(sx - 14, 160, 30, 7, '#2f7a85');
            R(sx - 14, 160, 30, 2, '#4a9ba5');
            R(sx - 12, 167, 26, 13, '#1f4f58');
            R(sx - 20, 180, 44, 3, '#00000040');
          }
          function sitter(sx, o, now) {
            const k = o.top,
              kd = shade(k, 0.25),
              sk = o.skin;
            R(sx - 6, 156, 20, 8, o.pants);
            R(sx - 6, 156, 20, 2, shade(o.pants, -0.2 > 0 ? 0 : 0.15));
            R(sx + 9, 163, 5, 15, o.pants);
            R(sx + 8, 177, 9, 3, o.shoes);
            R(sx - 9, 132, 11, 26, k);
            R(sx - 9, 132, 3, 26, kd);
            R(sx - 2, 134, 3, 6, shade(k, -0.0));
            R(sx - 5, 138, 5, 12, kd);
            R(sx - 3, 148, 13, 4, kd);
            R(sx + 10, 148, 3, 4, sk);
            R(sx - 5, 128, 5, 4, sk);
            R(sx - 7, 116, 11, 13, sk);
            R(sx - 7, 116, 2, 13, shade(sk, 0.12));
            if (o.long) {
              R(sx - 10, 114, 9, 22, o.hair);
              R(sx - 7, 113, 12, 5, o.hair);
            } else {
              R(sx - 8, 114, 12, 5, o.hair);
              R(sx - 8, 117, 3, 6, o.hair);
            }
            if (o.hat) {
              R(sx - 9, 111, 14, 4, o.hat);
              R(sx + 3, 114, 7, 1, o.hat);
            }
            if (o.shut) {
              R(sx + 1, 122, 3, 1, '#222');
            } else {
              R(sx + 1, 121, 3, 3, '#fff');
              R(sx + 3, 122, 1, 2, o.eye || '#1b1b1b');
            }
            R(sx + 4, 123, 1, 2, shade(sk, 0.15));
            if (o.glasses) {
              R(sx, 120, 5, 1, '#222');
              R(sx, 124, 5, 1, '#222');
            }
            if (o.item === 'book') {
              R(sx + 7, 141, 10, 12, '#f1ead2');
              R(sx + 7, 141, 10, 2, '#b33a3a');
              R(sx + 8, 145, 8, 1, '#999');
              R(sx + 8, 148, 8, 1, '#999');
            } else if (o.item === 'paper') {
              R(sx + 8, 132, 13, 22, '#ece9de');
              R(sx + 10, 135, 9, 2, '#555');
              R(sx + 10, 140, 9, 1, '#999');
              R(sx + 10, 144, 9, 1, '#999');
              R(sx + 10, 148, 9, 1, '#999');
            } else if (o.item === 'phone') {
              R(sx + 9, 142, 3, 6, '#1b1b1b');
              R(sx + 9, 143, 3, 4, '#7fd6ff');
            }
            if (o.bag) R(sx - 20, 146, 8, 14, o.bag);
          }
          function conductor(x, y, step, now) {
            const sw = step && Math.sin(step) > 0,
              l = sw ? -1 : 0,
              r = sw ? 0 : step ? -1 : 0;
            cx.save();
            cx.translate(Math.round(x), y);
            cx.scale(1.4, 1.4);
            R(-5, -1, 6, 2, '#151515');
            R(0, -1, 6, 2, '#151515');
            R(-4, -12 + l, 4, 12 - l, '#18263b');
            R(1, -12 + r, 4, 12 - r, '#18263b');
            R(-6, -25, 13, 14, '#1f3552');
            R(-6, -25, 3, 14, '#162840');
            R(0, -24, 1, 12, '#f3b94d');
            R(-6, -13, 13, 2, '#0e0e0e');
            R(3, -22, 3, 3, '#f3b94d');
            R(-8, -24, 3, 11, '#162840');
            R(-8, -13, 3, 2, '#d9a47c');
            R(6, -24, 3, 11, '#162840');
            R(6, -13, 3, 2, '#d9a47c');
            R(-2, -29, 5, 4, '#d9a47c');
            R(-5, -39, 11, 11, '#d9a47c');
            R(-5, -39, 11, 3, '#2b2b2b');
            R(-6, -43, 13, 5, '#1f3552');
            R(-6, -40, 13, 1, '#f3b94d');
            R(5, -40, 5, 1, '#1f3552');
            R(2, -35, 3, 3, '#fff');
            R(4, -34, 1, 2, '#1b1b1b');
            R(8, -18, 6, 5, '#8c979c');
            R(8, -18, 6, 1, '#c9d1d4');
            cx.restore();
          }
          function scenery(wx, wy, ww, wh, now) {
            cx.save();
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.rect(wx, wy, ww, wh);
            cx.clip();
            const g = cx.createLinearGradient(0, wy, 0, wy + wh);
            g.addColorStop(0, mixColor(P0.sky, P1.sky, prog));
            g.addColorStop(1, mixColor(P0.sky2, P1.sky2, prog));
            cx.fillStyle = g;
            cx.fillRect(wx, wy, ww, wh);
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff6c8';
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.arc(170, wy + 16, 9, 0, 7);
            cx.fill();
            cx.fillStyle = '#fff6c855';
            cx.beginPath();
            cx.arc(170, wy + 16, 15, 0, 7);
            cx.fill();
            for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
              const x = ((((k * 83 - scroll * 0.06) % 498) + 498) % 498) - 40,
                y = wy + 8 + (k % 3) * 9;
              R(x, y, 28, 5, '#ffffffcc');
              R(x + 7, y - 4, 14, 5, '#ffffffcc');
            }
            const far = mixColor(P0.far, P1.far, prog),
              hill = mixColor(P0.hill, P1.hill, prog),
              tc = mixColor(P0.tree, P1.tree, prog),
              gr = mixColor(P0.ground, P1.ground, prog),
              base = wy + wh - 12,
              mf = toMine ? prog : 1 - prog;
            for (let x = wx; x < wx + ww; x += 3) {
              const ax = x + scroll * 0.12,
                h = 16 + 13 * Math.abs(Math.sin(ax / 37)) + 5 * Math.sin(ax / 11);
              R(x, base - h, 3, h + 4, far);
            }
            for (let x = wx; x < wx + ww; x += 3) {
              const ax = x + scroll * 0.3,
                h = 7 + 6 * Math.sin(ax / 26) + 3 * Math.sin(ax / 8);
              R(x, base - h, 3, h + 6, hill);
            }
            R(wx, base, ww, 12, gr);
            R(wx, base, ww, 2, mixColor(gr, '#ffffff', 0.25));
            R(wx, base + 8, ww, 4, shade(gr, 0.2));
            for (let k = 0; k < 14; k++) {
              const x = ((((k * 31 - scroll * 0.7) % 434) + 434) % 434) - 24,
                pine = (k * 0.137) % 1 < mf;
              if (pine) {
                R(x - 1, base - 7, 2, 7, '#3b3b3b');
                R(x - 5, base - 12, 10, 5, tc);
                R(x - 4, base - 17, 8, 5, tc);
                R(x - 2, base - 21, 4, 4, tc);
                R(x - 5, base - 8, 10, 1, shade(tc, 0.25));
                if (k % 3 === 0) R(x + 5, base - 6, 2, 5, '#81f3df');
              } else {
                R(x - 1, base - 8, 2, 8, '#6b4a2e');
                R(x - 5, base - 18, 10, 10, tc);
                R(x - 3, base - 22, 6, 4, tc);
                R(x - 5, base - 10, 10, 2, shade(tc, 0.25));
              }
            }
            R(wx, base + 5, ww, 1, '#5b4630');
            for (let m = 0; m < 20; m++) {
              const x = ((((m * 23 - scroll * 1.3) % 460) + 460) % 460) - 30;
              R(x, base + 2, 2, 9, '#5b4630');
            }
            for (let m = 0; m < 6; m++) {
              const x = ((((m * 83 - scroll) % 498) + 498) % 498) - 30;
              R(x, wy, 2, wh, '#2a2f35');
              R(x - 4, wy + 7, 10, 2, '#2a2f35');
            }
            if (speed > 0.5)
              for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
                const y = base + 3 + ((k * 5) % 9),
                  x = ((((k * 57 - scroll * 2.2) % 420) + 420) % 420) - 20;
                R(x, y, 20, 1, '#ffffff44');
              }
            cx.restore();
            R(wx - 3, wy - 3, ww + 6, 3, '#22343a');
            R(wx - 3, wy + wh, ww + 6, 4, '#22343a');
            R(wx - 3, wy - 3, 3, wh + 7, '#22343a');
            R(wx + ww, wy - 3, 3, wh + 7, '#22343a');
            R(wx + 8, wy, 5, wh, '#ffffff12');
            R(wx + 16, wy, 2, wh, '#ffffff0c');
          }
          const hexColor = (n) => '#' + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
          function playerSitter() {
            const look = game() && game().rpg && game().rpg.look;
            const base = {
              skin: '#f1c6a0',
              hair: '#5a3b2a',
              top: '#3a63c8',
              pants: '#2d3a55',
              shoes: '#222',
              hat: '#2f7de1',
              bag: '#8a4b2a',
            };
            if (!look) return base;
            return {
              skin: hexColor(look.skin),
              hair: hexColor(look.hair),
              top: hexColor(look.outfit),
              pants: hexColor(look.pants),
              shoes: '#222',
              eye: hexColor(look.eye),
              hat: look.hat ? hexColor(look.hat.color) : undefined,
              glasses: !!look.glasses,
              long: ['long', 'bob', 'ponytail', 'longbun', 'flare', 'highpony'].includes(window.LQCharacter.appearance(look).style),
              bag: look.backpack ? hexColor(look.backpack) : undefined,
            };
          }
          const people = [
            {
              sx: 72,
              o: {
                skin: '#f0c8a0',
                hair: '#3a2418',
                top: '#b8433f',
                pants: '#2f3e5a',
                shoes: '#222',
                long: true,
                item: 'book',
              },
            },
            {
              sx: 142,
              o: {
                skin: '#e3b48c',
                hair: '#d8d8d8',
                top: '#6a6f4a',
                pants: '#3b3b3b',
                shoes: '#3a2a1c',
                hat: '#4a4a3a',
                glasses: true,
                item: 'paper',
              },
            },
            { sx: 212, o: playerSitter(), you: true },
            {
              sx: 282,
              o: {
                skin: '#f3cfae',
                hair: '#1f1a16',
                top: '#3f9b5a',
                pants: '#444a5c',
                shoes: '#e8e8e8',
                item: 'phone',
              },
            },
            {
              sx: 352,
              o: {
                skin: '#c98f66',
                hair: '#222',
                top: '#e0a63a',
                pants: '#33384a',
                shoes: '#222',
                shut: true,
              },
            },
          ];
          function draw(now) {
            const W = cv.width,
              H = cv.height,
              s = Math.min(W / 400, H / 225),
              sway = speed > 0.1 ? Math.sin(now / 60) * 0.5 * speed : 0;
            cx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
            cx.fillStyle = '#0b0f14';
            cx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            const ox = (W - 400 * s) / 2,
              oy = (H - 225 * s) / 2 + sway * s;
            if (dir > 0) cx.setTransform(s, 0, 0, s, ox, oy);
            else cx.setTransform(-s, 0, 0, s, ox + 400 * s, oy);
            cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
            R(0, 0, 400, 225, '#5f8187');
            R(0, 0, 400, 16, '#34484e');
            R(0, 16, 400, 3, '#243438');
            for (let x = 24; x < 400; x += 88) {
              R(x, 6, 44, 4, '#fff6c2');
              R(x - 4, 10, 52, 3, '#fff6c218');
            }
            R(0, 30, 400, 2, '#aab6ba');
            R(0, 32, 400, 2, '#6f7c80');
            for (const b of [
              [40, 19, 22, 11, '#8a5a3b'],
              [48, 12, 14, 7, '#c9a15a'],
              [150, 20, 26, 10, '#4a6a8a'],
              [250, 18, 20, 12, '#a64b4b'],
              [320, 21, 24, 9, '#6b5b8a'],
            ])
              R(b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3], b[4]);
            for (const wx of [14, 112, 210, 308]) scenery(wx, 38, 80, 64, now);
            R(0, 102, 400, 63, '#4f7178');
            for (let x = 0; x < 400; x += 10) R(x, 104, 1, 59, '#46666c');
            R(0, 128, 400, 4, '#16b3a5');
            R(0, 132, 400, 1, '#0f857d');
            R(0, 165, 400, 20, '#6d5640');
            R(0, 185, 400, 40, '#2e4f63');
            R(0, 185, 400, 2, '#1f3a4a');
            for (let x = 0; x < 400; x += 16) {
              R(x, 205, 8, 2, '#436b82');
              R(x + 8, 213, 8, 2, '#436b82');
            }
            for (const p of people) {
              seat(p.sx);
              sitter(p.sx, p.o, now);
            }
            for (const x of [100, 190, 280, 370]) {
              const sw = Math.sin(now / 380 + x) * 2;
              R(x, 19, 1, 12, '#cfd6d8');
              R(x - 3 + sw, 31, 7, 2, '#cfd6d8');
              R(x - 3 + sw, 31, 2, 5, '#cfd6d8');
              R(x + 2 + sw, 31, 2, 5, '#cfd6d8');
            }
            const walking = phase === 'walk' || phase === 'exit',
              bob = Math.sin(now / 170) * 1.5;
            if (phase === 'ask' || phase === 'check') {
              R(224, 166 + bob, 10, 6, '#ffd36b');
              R(224, 168 + bob, 10, 1, '#c98a1c');
              R(227, 170 + bob, 5, 1, '#c98a1c');
            }
            if (phase === 'ask') {
              const b = Math.sin(now / 200) * 2;
              R(207, 100 + b, 10, 2, '#ffd36b');
              R(209, 102 + b, 6, 2, '#ffd36b');
              R(211, 104 + b, 2, 2, '#ffd36b');
            }
            if (
              condX > -28 &&
              condX < 420 &&
              phase !== 'cruise' &&
              phase !== 'arrive' &&
              phase !== 'depart'
            )
              conductor(condX, 214, walking ? now / 130 : 0, now);
            const v = cx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 225);
            v.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,.28)');
            v.addColorStop(0.15, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
            v.addColorStop(0.85, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
            v.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)');
            cx.fillStyle = v;
            cx.fillRect(0, 0, 400, 225);
          }
          function frame(now) {
            if (done) return;
            const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000);
            last = now;
            update(dt);
            if (!done) {
              draw(now);
              raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
            }
          }
          raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        }

        function onNpcConversation(id, message) {
          const rail = railFlow(id, message);
          if (rail.length) return rail;
          const s = state(),
            g = game();
          if (
            !s ||
            !g ||
            id !== 'postman' ||
            !canShop('Post Office') ||
            (Number(s.flags.pendingMail) || 0) < 1
          )
            return [];
          const text = norm(message);
          if (!/(letter|parcel|post|mail|stamp|gui thu|gui hang|send)/.test(text)) return [];
          s.flags.pendingMail -= 1;
          s.flags.sentLetters = (Number(s.flags.sentLetters) || 0) + 1;
          log('mail', 0, 'Sent a letter/parcel at the post office');
          save();
          return ['📮 Sam has received your parcel! “Your parcel has been sent.”'];
        }
        function mineAction() {
          const sc = scene(),
            s = state(),
            g = game();
          if (!s || !sc) return 'The game has not finished loading.';
          if (!context().inside?.includes('Great Mine'))
            return 'Go down to the Great Mine to mine.';
          if (count('pickaxe') < 1) return 'Buy a pickaxe at the Smithy or Tool Shop first.';
          const key = `mine:${g.day}`;
          const n = Number(s.jobs[key]) || 0;
          if (n >= 2) return 'You have already mined 2 samples today.';
          if (usedSpace() >= capacity()) return 'Your bag is full.';
          s.jobs[key] = n + 1;
          inventory().ore = (Number(inventory().ore) || 0) + 1;
          log('earn-item', 0, 'Mined 1 ore sample');
          save();
          return '⛏️ You found ore! Take it to the Smithy to trade for tools (it cannot be sold for unlimited cash).';
        }
        function jobAction(english) {
          const c = context(),
            s = state(),
            g = game();
          if (!s || !g) return 'The game has not finished loading.';
          const role = Object.entries(shops).find(([name]) => c.inside === name)?.[0];
          if (
            !role ||
            !['Sunny Cafe', 'Library', 'Post Office', 'Smithy', 'Tool Shop'].includes(role)
          )
            return 'Go to Sunny Cafe, Library, Post Office or Smithy to get a job.';
          const spoken = String(english || '').trim();
          if (
            !/\b(i (?:can|would like to|want to|have|will)|could i|may i|let me|please)\b/i.test(
              spoken,
            ) ||
            spoken.length < 10
          )
            return 'Say a full sentence in English. For example: “Could I help you with your work, please?”';
          const key = `job:${role}:${g.day}`;
          if (s.jobs[key])
            return 'You already worked here today. Come back tomorrow.';
          if (role === 'Smithy' && count('ore') < 1)
            return 'At the Smithy, you need 1 ore sample to finish the job.';
          if (role === 'Smithy') {
            inventory().ore -= 1;
          }
          const payout = {
            'Sunny Cafe': 25,
            Library: 30,
            'Post Office': 35,
            'Smithy': 45,
            'Tool Shop': 28,
          }[role];
          s.jobs[key] = true;
          g.money += payout;
          g.rpg.xp = (Number(g.rpg.xp) || 0) + 10;
          log('job', payout, `Job at ${role}`);
          save();
          return `✅ Finished the job at ${role}! +$${payout}, +10 XP. Practice the sentence “I have finished my work.”`;
        }
        const visualHome = {
          lamp: [1, 1, 0xffdf85],
          home_plant: [2, 2, 0x6ad29d],
          rug: [3, 3, 0xd685b3],
          bookshelf: [4, 1, 0xe8bf73],
          sofa: [5, 3, 0x7e8ddc],
          fancy_bed: [1, 5, 0xf8bfcc],
          study_corner: [7, 2, 0xaacfea],
        };
        function renderHomeDecor() {
          const sc = scene();
          if (decorationGraphics) {
            try {
              decorationGraphics.destroy();
            } catch {}
            decorationGraphics = null;
          }
          if (
            !sc?.inside ||
            sc.inside.b.name !== "Hero's Home" ||
            typeof sc.add?.graphics !== 'function'
          )
            return;
          const s = state();
          const k = sc.inside.k,
            unit = 32 * k;
          const bounds = sc.inside.spec;
          const g = sc.add.graphics().setDepth(2);
          decorationGraphics = g;
          for (const [id, v] of Object.entries(visualHome)) {
            if (count(id) < 1) continue;
            const offset = Number(s.homeSlots[id] || 0),
              x = v[0] + (offset % 3),
              y = v[1] + (offset === 2 ? 1 : 0);
            if (x >= bounds.w - 1 || y >= bounds.h - 1) continue;
            const baseX = sc.inside.objs?.[0]?.x ?? 20000,
              baseY = sc.inside.objs?.[0]?.y ?? 0;
            const X = baseX + x * unit,
              Y = baseY + y * unit;
            g.fillStyle(0x0e2030, 0.19).fillEllipse(
              X + unit / 2,
              Y + unit * 0.8,
              unit * 0.78,
              unit * 0.33,
            );
            g.fillStyle(v[2], 0.92).fillRoundedRect(
              X + unit * 0.14,
              Y + unit * 0.26,
              unit * 0.72,
              unit * 0.52,
              Math.max(2, k * 2),
            );
            g.lineStyle(Math.max(1, k), 0xffffff, 0.35).strokeRoundedRect(
              X + unit * 0.17,
              Y + unit * 0.29,
              unit * 0.65,
              unit * 0.44,
              Math.max(2, k * 2),
            );
            if (id === 'lamp')
              g.fillStyle(0xffe9a4, 0.17).fillCircle(X + unit * 0.5, Y + unit * 0.46, unit * 0.8);
          }
        }
        function show(tab = 'shop', specific = null) {
          const p = $('v10-panel');
          if (!p) return;
          if (specific && shops[specific]) chosenShop = specific;
          openTab = tab;
          p.hidden = false;
          render();
        }
        function close() {
          const p = $('v10-panel');
          if (p) p.hidden = true;
        }
        function render() {
          const root = $('v10-content'),
            p = $('v10-panel');
          if (!root || !p || p.hidden) return;
          const g = game(),
            s = state();
          if (!s) {
            root.innerHTML = '<p>Game is loading, please wait…</p>';
            return;
          }
          const c = context();
          $('v10-balance').textContent =
            `Wallet $${g.money} · Savings $${s.bank} · 🎒 ${usedSpace()}/${capacity()}`;
          document
            .querySelectorAll('[data-v10-tab]')
            .forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.v10Tab === openTab));
          if (openTab === 'shop') {
            const shopsList = Object.keys(shops)
              .map(
                (name) =>
                  `<option value="${esc(name)}" ${chosenShop === name ? 'selected' : ''}>${shops[name].emoji} ${esc(name)}</option>`,
              )
              .join('');
            const located = canShop(chosenShop),
              intro = shops[chosenShop].intro;
            root.innerHTML = `<div class="v10-row"><label>📍 Shop <select id="v10-shop-select">${shopsList}</select></label><b>${located ? '🟢 At the shop' : '⚪ Not at a location'}</b></div><p class="v10-help">💬 ${esc(intro)}<br>🗣️ “How much is it?” · “Could I buy this, please?”</p><div class="v10-grid">${
              shopItems(chosenShop)
                .map(
                  ([id, it]) =>
                    `<article class="v10-item"><strong>${esc(it.name)}</strong><p>${esc(it.desc)}</p><div class="v10-row"><b>$${it.price}</b><button data-v10-buy="${esc(id)}" ${!located || (it.unique && count(id) > 0) ? 'disabled' : ''}>Mua</button></div></article>`,
                )
                .join('') || '<p>No products.</p>'
            }</div><p class="v10-help">You must be at the right building or its entrance to buy. There are no automatic payments.</p>`;
          } else if (openTab === 'bag') {
            const list = Object.entries(inventory()).filter(([id, n]) => catalog[id] && n > 0);
            root.innerHTML = `<h3>🎒 Inventory (${usedSpace()}/${capacity()})</h3><div class="v10-grid">${list.length ? list.map(([id, n]) => `<article class="v10-item"><strong>${esc(catalog[id].name)} ×${n}</strong><p>${esc(catalog[id].desc)}</p><button data-v10-use="${esc(id)}">${catalog[id].effect === 'gift' ? '🎁 Give' : catalog[id].effect === 'decorate' ? '🏠 Place / move' : 'Use / view'}</button></article>`).join('') : '<p>Go to a shop to buy items. Your old quest and RPG items are still in the inventory (R).</p>'}</div><div class="v10-row"><button data-v10-action="mine">⛏️ Mine in the Great Mine</button><button data-v10-action="job">💼 Work here</button><button data-v10-action="rest">😴 Rest at the inn</button></div>`;
          } else if (openTab === 'bank') {
            root.innerHTML = `<h3>🏦 Savings account</h3><div class="v10-summary"><span>Cash <b>$${g.money}</b></span><span>Savings <b>$${s.bank}</b></span><span>Total <b>$${g.money + s.bank}</b></span></div><p>You can only deposit and withdraw while standing at the Bank. There is no fake interest or hidden fees.</p><label>Amount <input id="v10-bank-amount" type="number" inputmode="numeric" min="1" max="1000000" value="20"></label><div class="v10-row"><button data-v10-action="deposit" ${!canShop('Bank') ? 'disabled' : ''}>⬇ Deposit</button><button data-v10-action="withdraw" ${!canShop('Bank') ? 'disabled' : ''}>⬆ Withdraw</button></div><hr><h3>🎯 Savings goal</h3><label>Name <input id="v10-goal-name" maxlength="60" value="${esc(s.goal.name || '')}"></label><label>Goal ($) <input id="v10-goal-amount" type="number" min="1" max="1000000" value="${Number(s.goal.amount) || 200}"></label><button data-v10-action="goal">Save goal</button><p>${Math.min(100, Math.floor((s.bank / Math.max(1, Number(s.goal.amount))) * 100))}% of your goal “${esc(s.goal.name)}” reached</p>`;
          } else if (openTab === 'home') {
            const homeItems = Object.entries(catalog).filter(([id, it]) => it.category === 'home');
            root.innerHTML = `<h3>🏠 Upgrade Hero’s Home</h3><p>Buy items at the Corner Shop or Market, then go home, open your Inventory and press “Place / move”. Items appear on the house graphic.</p><div class="v10-grid">${homeItems.map(([id, it]) => `<article class="v10-item"><strong>${esc(it.name)} ${count(id) ? '✅' : ''}</strong><p>${esc(it.desc)}</p><small>Price $${it.price} · ${count(id) ? 'Owned' : 'Not owned'}</small></article>`).join('')}</div><button data-v10-action="refresh-home">🔁 Refresh furniture</button>`;
          } else if (openTab === 'learning') {
            root.innerHTML = `<h3>🎓 Learn financial English</h3><p>10 short free lessons. Books bought in the game unlock extra vocabulary and advanced material.</p><div class="v10-grid">${financeLessons.map(([id, title, correct, wrong]) => `<article class="v10-item"><b>${s.jobs['lesson-' + id] ? '✅' : '📖'} ${esc(title)}</b><p>${esc(correct)}</p><button data-v10-lesson="${id}">Practice</button></article>`).join('')}</div><h3>📚 Purchased lessons</h3><p>${
              Object.keys(s.unlocked)
                .filter((x) => s.unlocked[x])
                .map((x) => esc(x))
                .join(', ') || 'No advanced lessons unlocked yet.'
            }</p>`;
          } else if (openTab === 'ledger') {
            root.innerHTML = `<h3>🧾 Transaction history</h3><p>The ledger keeps up to ${MAX_HISTORY} recent transactions and does not include your API key.</p><div class="v10-ledger">${s.ledger.length ? s.ledger.map((tx) => `<div><span><b>${esc(tx.label)}</b><small>Day ${tx.day} · ${esc(tx.type)}</small></span><b class="${tx.amount < 0 ? 'expense' : 'income'}">${tx.amount > 0 ? '+' : ''}${tx.amount}$</b></div>`).join('') : '<p>No transactions yet.</p>'}</div><button data-v10-action="export">⬇ Download spending history CSV</button>`;
          }
          $('v10-status').textContent = msg;
        }
        function openLesson(id) {
          const it = financeLessons.find((x) => x[0] === id);
          if (!it) return;
          active = { id, correct: it[2], wrong: it[3], picked: null };
          const pane = $('v10-quiz');
          pane.hidden = false;
          pane.innerHTML = `<h3>📖 ${esc(it[1])}</h3><p>Choose the more natural English sentence:</p>${[
            it[2],
            it[3],
          ]
            .sort((a, b) => (id.length % 2 ? a.localeCompare(b) : b.localeCompare(a)))
            .map((t) => `<button data-v10-answer="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</button>`)
            .join('')}<button data-v10-action="quiz-close">Close</button>`;
        }
        function answerLesson(text) {
          if (!active) return;
          const s = state(),
            g = game();
          const ok = text === active.correct;
          if (ok && !s.jobs['lesson-' + active.id]) {
            s.jobs['lesson-' + active.id] = true;
            g.rpg.xp = (Number(g.rpg.xp) || 0) + 12;
            log('lesson', 0, `Lesson ${active.id}: +12 XP`);
            save();
          }
          notice(
            ok
              ? '✅ Correct! ' + active.correct + ' · +12 XP the first time.'
              : '💡 Try again. A better sentence: ' + active.correct,
          );
          $('v10-quiz').hidden = true;
          active = null;
        }
        function exportCsv() {
          const s = state();
          if (!s) return;
          const csv = [
            ['day', 'minutes', 'type', 'amount', 'label'],
            ...s.ledger.map((t) => [t.day, t.minutes, t.type, t.amount, t.label]),
          ]
            .map((row) => row.map((v) => '"' + String(v ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""') + '"').join(','))
            .join('\r\n');
          const a = document.createElement('a');
          a.href = URL.createObjectURL(
            new Blob(['\ufeff' + csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }),
          );
          a.download = 'LIFEQUEST_V10_CHI_TIEU.csv';
          a.click();
          setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
        }
        function afterSceneChange() {
          if (decorationGraphics) {
            try {
              decorationGraphics.destroy();
            } catch {}
            decorationGraphics = null;
          }
          renderHomeDecor();
        }
        function aiShopping(text) {
          const t = norm(text),
            ctx = context(),
            nearShop = ctx.inside && shops[ctx.inside] ? ctx.inside : null;
          const m = t.match(/(?:mua|buy|order|purchase)\s+(?:cho toi|me|a|an|mot|one)?\s*(.*)/);
          if (m) {
            const id = Object.entries(catalog).find(
              ([key, it]) =>
                norm(it.name).includes(m[1]) ||
                norm(m[1]).includes(norm(it.name).replace(/^[^\p{L}]+/u, '')) ||
                key === norm(m[1]).replace(/\s+/g, '_'),
            )?.[0];
            if (id) {
              const it = catalog[id],
                where = nearShop || it.shops[0];
              chosenShop = where;
              show('shop', where);
              return `🛒 ${it.name} costs $${it.price}, available at ${it.shops.join(', ')}. ${canShop(where) ? 'Pick the item and press Buy to confirm the purchase.' : 'Go to the shop first; the AI never deducts money by itself.'}`;
            }
          }
          if (
            /(toi co|i have|ngan sach|budget|afford|mua duoc gi)/.test(t) &&
            /(\$|\b\d+\b|mua|buy|budget)/.test(t)
          ) {
            const n = Number(t.match(/\d+/)?.[0]) || money();
            const results = Object.values(catalog)
              .filter((x) => x.price <= n)
              .slice(0, 8)
              .map((x) => `${x.name} $${x.price}`)
              .join(' · ');
            return `💰 With $${n} you could consider: ${results}. Cash in your wallet: $${money()}. Open the Shop (B) to check prices and confirm a purchase.`;
          }
          if (/(kiem tien|lam viec|earn money|find a job)/.test(t))
            return '💼 Earn money from NPC quests or a once-a-day job at Sunny Cafe, Library, Post Office or Smithy. Go to the matching building and open the Economy window (B), then Inventory → Work.';
          if (/(tiet kiem|ngan hang|bank|deposit|withdraw|saving)/.test(t)) {
            show('bank');
            return `🏦 You have $${money()} in your wallet and $${state()?.bank || 0} in savings. Go to the Bank to deposit/withdraw. Saving does not create extra virtual money.`;
          }
          if (/(gia|bao nhieu|how much|price|cheaper|discount)/.test(t)) {
            const sample = Object.values(catalog)
              .slice(0, 4)
              .map((x) => `${x.name}: $${x.price}`)
              .join(', ');
            return `🛍️ Prices are fixed in shops, for example: ${sample}. Try asking “How much does it cost?” or “Do you have anything cheaper?”`;
          }
          return null;
        }
        function init() {
          const panel = $('v10-panel');
          if (!panel) return;
          ensureRailUi();
          $('v10-open').addEventListener('click', () => show('shop'));
          $('v10-close').addEventListener('click', close);
          panel.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === panel) {
              close();
              return;
            }
            const b = e.target.closest('button');
            if (!b) return;
            if (b.dataset.v10Tab) {
              openTab = b.dataset.v10Tab;
              render();
              return;
            }
            if (b.dataset.v10Buy) {
              const id = b.dataset.v10Buy;
              if (!confirm(`Buy ${catalog[id].name} for $${catalog[id].price}?`)) return;
              notice(transactBuy(chosenShop, id, 1).message);
              return;
            }
            if (b.dataset.v10Use) {
              notice(consume(b.dataset.v10Use));
              return;
            }
            if (b.dataset.v10Lesson) {
              openLesson(b.dataset.v10Lesson);
              return;
            }
            if (b.dataset.v10Answer) {
              answerLesson(b.dataset.v10Answer);
              return;
            }
            const a = b.dataset.v10Action;
            if (!a) return;
            if (a === 'deposit' || a === 'withdraw') {
              const value = $('v10-bank-amount')?.value;
              notice(bankAction(a, Number(value)));
            } else if (a === 'goal') {
              const n = Number($('v10-goal-amount').value);
              if (!validInt(n, 1, 1e6)) {
                notice('The goal must be a valid amount.');
                return;
              }
              state().goal = { name: $('v10-goal-name').value.slice(0, 60), amount: n };
              save();
              notice('🎯 Savings goal saved.');
            } else if (a === 'mine') notice(mineAction());
            else if (a === 'job') {
              const english = prompt(
                'To get a job, say a sentence in English (for example: Could I help you with your work, please?)',
              );
              if (english !== null) notice(jobAction(english));
            } else if (a === 'rest') notice(restAction());
            else if (a === 'refresh-home') {
              renderHomeDecor();
              notice('Room updated.');
            } else if (a === 'export') exportCsv();
            else if (a === 'quiz-close') $('v10-quiz').hidden = true;
          });
          panel.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.id === 'v10-shop-select') {
              chosenShop = e.target.value;
              render();
            }
          });
          for (const node of [panel, $('v10-quiz')]) {
            node.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                close();
                if ($('v10-quiz')) $('v10-quiz').hidden = true;
              }
            });
            node.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => e.stopPropagation());
          }
          document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (
              ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(e.target?.tagName) ||
              e.altKey ||
              e.ctrlKey ||
              e.metaKey
            )
              return;
            if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'b' && !e.repeat) {
              e.preventDefault();
              panel.hidden ? show('shop') : close();
            } else if (e.key === 'Escape') close();
          });
          const npcButton = $('v10-npc-shop');
          if (npcButton)
            npcButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
              const sc = scene(),
                id = sc?.chat?.currentId,
                link = Object.entries(shops).find(
                  ([, v]) =>
                    v.npc === id &&
                    canShop(
                      Object.entries(shops).find(
                        ([name, s]) => s.npc === id && canShop(name),
                      )?.[0] || '',
                    ),
                )?.[0];
              if (link) show('shop', link);
              else notice('Go to the right shop to buy items.');
            });
          setInterval(() => {
            const sc = scene(),
              btn = $('v10-npc-shop');
            if (btn && sc) {
              const npc = sc.chat?.currentId;
              const possible = Object.entries(shops).find(
                ([name, v]) => v.npc === npc && canShop(name),
              );
              btn.hidden = !possible;
            }
            if (sc?.inside?.b?.name === "Hero's Home" && !decorationGraphics) renderHomeDecor();
            else if (!sc?.inside && decorationGraphics) {
              decorationGraphics.destroy();
              decorationGraphics = null;
            }
            if (!panel.hidden) render();
          }, 1200);
          const oldNpcMessage = adventure.onNpcMessage;
          adventure.onNpcMessage = function (id, message) {
            const original = oldNpcMessage.call(adventure, id, message) || [];
            return [...original, ...onNpcConversation(id, message)];
          };
          const currentAssistantRun = assistantCore.run;
          assistantCore.run = async function (input, ...args) {
            const answer = aiShopping(input);
            if (answer) return { kind: 'text', reply: answer, local: true };
            return currentAssistantRun.call(assistantCore, input, ...args);
          };
          // Hide the old global RPG shop; it cannot take remote purchases anymore.
          window.LQ10_ECONOMY = {
            catalog,
            shops,
            show,
            transactBuy,
            bankAction,
            consume,
            state,
            context,
            mineAction,
            jobAction,
            onNpcConversation,
            boardTrain,
          };
        }
        return {
          init,
          show,
          close,
          render,
          notice,
          renderHomeDecor,
          afterSceneChange,
          state,
          shopItems,
          transactBuy,
          bankAction,
          consume,
          aiShopping,
          context,
          restAction,
          jobAction,
          mineAction,
          onNpcConversation,
          boardTrain,
        };
      })();

      const LQ11 = (() => {
        'use strict';
        const el = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
        const shell = () => el('l11-shell');
        let selected = 'maps',
          lastFocus = null,
          lastHint = '',
          lastHud = '',
          renderTimer = 0;
        const pages = {
          maps: 'Maps',
          world: 'World',
          quests: 'Quests',
          learn: 'English',
          economy: 'Economy',
          character: 'Character',
          achievements: 'Achievements',
          settings: 'Settings',
        };
        const safeHTML = (s) =>
          String(s ?? '').replace(
            /[&<>"']/g,
            (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c],
          );
        const card = (title, desc, action, label) =>
          `<article class="l11-card"><b>${title}</b><p>${desc}</p><button type="button" data-l11-action="${action}">${label || 'Open'} ↗</button></article>`;
        const group = (heading, description, body) =>
          `<h3>${heading}</h3><p class="l11-explain">${description}</p>${body}`;
        const stats = () => {
          const sc = window.__lifequestGetScene?.();
          const g = sc?.store?.game;
          const r = adventure?.state?.();
          const econ = window.LQ10_ECONOMY?.state?.();
          return {
            sc,
            g,
            r,
            econ,
            area: sc?.area?.name || 'English Town',
            inside: sc?.inside?.b?.name || '',
            floor: sc?.inside?.floor || 0,
            money: Number(g?.money || 0),
            bank: Number(econ?.bank || 0),
            level: Number(r?.level || 1),
            xp: Number(r?.xp || 0),
          };
        };
        const mapDetails = {
          'english-town': { icon: '🏘️', label: 'TODAY · CITY', desc: 'Flower plazas, civic pavilions, bright streets, garden walks and calm lakeside corners.' },
          'mining-town': { icon: '⛏️', label: 'TODAY · INDUSTRIAL', desc: 'Minehead towers, ore yards, timber depots, industrial sidings and active workshops.' },
          'art-district': { icon: '🎨', label: 'TODAY · PAINTERS QUARTER', desc: 'Painting studios, galleries, workshops, Dream Gate and a separate music / festival avenue.' },
          'forgotten-valley': { icon: '🔔', label: 'ET · FORGOTTEN HAMLET', desc: 'Restore a hamlet beside the woods, learn from Arven and Lyra, and follow the sound of the bell.' },
          'english-town-past': { icon: '🏰', label: '70 YEARS AGO · VILLAGE', desc: 'Rice paddies, timber cottages, market shelters, watch posts and family stories.' },
          'tech-co': { icon: '🏢', label: 'MAP 5 · B1 · WORKPLACE', desc: 'Neon Circuit technology district, smart transit, holographic terminals, living Innovation Nexus and eco-tech garden. Practise meetings, issue reports and deadline emails.' },
          'old-oriaris': { icon: '👑', label: 'MAP 6 · B1–B2 · ROYAL ISLAND', desc: 'Explore an imperial palace, a grand opera, a royal academy and four noble estates. Practise was/were + -ing with while and when.' },
          'wish-valley': { icon: '🌸', label: 'MAP 7 · B2 · WISHES', desc: 'A lavender valley of choices and reflections. Practise I wish, if I were you, would and because.' },
          'vocalt-greedy-land': { icon: '👑', label: 'MAP 8 · C1 · GREEDY LAND', desc: 'Lord Vocalt’s extravagant rare-goods market. Practise present perfect and past perfect.' },
        };
        function mapsPage(s) {
          const all = window.LQWorldMaps?.list?.() || [];
          if (!all.length) return group('World maps', 'The game world is not ready yet. Close the menu and reopen it after the scene loads.', '');
          const discovered = new Set(Array.isArray(s.g?.worldDiscoveries) ? s.g.worldDiscoveries : []);
          const totalLandmarks = all.reduce((count, map) => count + (window.LQWorldUpgrade?.information?.(map.id)?.landmarks?.length || 0), 0);
          const cards = all.map((map, index) => {
            const info = mapDetails[map.id] || {
              icon: map.theme === 'past' ? '🏕️' : '🗺️',
              label: map.theme === 'past' ? 'HISTORICAL WORLD' : 'EXPLORABLE WORLD',
              desc: 'Explore this area and meet the characters who live here.',
            };
            const current = s.sc?.area?.id === map.id;
            const locked = window.LQCareerWorlds?.lockInfo(map.id);
            const worldArt = window.LQWorldUpgrade?.information?.(map.id);
            const features = worldArt?.landmarks?.slice(0, 3) || [];
            const discoveredHere = (worldArt?.landmarks || []).filter((_, i) => discovered.has(`${map.id}:${i}`)).length;
            const region = map.theme === 'past' ? 'past' : map.theme === 'art' ? 'art' : map.theme === 'mine' ? 'mine' : 'town';
            return `<article class="l11-map-card${current ? ' is-current' : ''}">
                <div class="l11-map-visual l11-map-${region}" aria-hidden="true">
                  <span class="l11-map-symbol">${info.icon}</span>
                  <span class="l11-map-number">${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / ${String(all.length).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="l11-map-content">
                  <span class="l11-map-region">${safeHTML(info.label)}</span>
                  <h4>${safeHTML(map.name)}</h4>
                  <p>${safeHTML(info.desc)}</p>
                  <div class="l11-map-features">${features.map(f => `<span>${safeHTML(f)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
                  <small class="l11-map-tracked">✦ Discovered landmarks: ${discoveredHere}/${worldArt?.landmarks?.length || 0}</small>
                  ${locked ? `<small style="display:block;color:#fbd08e;margin:5px 0">${safeHTML(locked.label)}</small>` : ''}
                  <button type="button" data-l11-action="travel:${safeHTML(map.id)}" ${current || locked ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                    ${current ? '📍 Current location' : locked ? '🔒 Finish preceding map missions' : '➜ Travel to this map'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </article>`;
          }).join('');
          return group(
            '🧭 Choose a map',
            `Explore ${all.length} worlds. All maps are open from the beginning — travel freely and complete missions in any order.`,
            `<div class="l11-statline l11-map-summary"><span>📍 You are in: <b>${safeHTML(s.area)}</b></span><span class="l11-pill">${all.length} MAPS · ${discovered.size}/${totalLandmarks} DISCOVERIES</span></div>
             <div class="l11-map-grid">${cards}</div>
             <p class="l11-map-notice">✅ All maps unlocked! Select any destination to travel instantly. Missions and achievements remain optional and keep their progress.</p>`,
          );
        }
        function page(name) {
          const s = stats();
          const t = el('l11-page');
          if (!t) return;
          let h = '';
          if (name === 'maps') h = mapsPage(s);
          else if (name === 'world')
            h = group(
              'Explore the world',
              'Every existing outdoor map keeps its original layout, interiors and NPC schedules. Use Maps to jump straight to any region.',
              `<div class="l11-statline"><span class="l11-pill">📍 ${safeHTML(s.area)}</span>${s.inside ? ` · In ${safeHTML(s.inside)} · floor ${s.floor}` : ''}<div>${window.LQPast?.dayLabel ? window.LQPast.dayLabel(s.g?.day) : `Day ${s.g?.day || 1}`} ·${Math.floor(
                (s.g?.minutes || 720) / 60,
              )
                .toString()
                .padStart(2, '0')}:${Math.floor((s.g?.minutes || 720) % 60)
                .toString()
                .padStart(2, '0')}</div></div><div class="l11-grid">` +
                card(
                  '🗺️ Minimap & compass',
                  'Toggle the minimap with M; positions and paths come straight from the current world.',
                  'map',
                  'Toggle map',
                ) +
                card(
                  '👥 NPCs & schedules',
                  'See NPC locations, relationships and working hours from V8 data.',
                  'v8:npc',
                  'NPC list',
                ) +
                card(
                  '📍 Map objectives',
                  'Track objectives, area gates and buildings to visit.',
                  'v8:quests',
                  'View objectives',
                ) +
                card(
                  '❔ World controls',
                  'How to enter buildings, change floors, move with WASD, interact, and use shortcuts.',
                  'guide',
                  'View guide',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          else if (name === 'quests')
            h = group(
              'Quest log',
              'Story, AI quests and world quests still use the original reward checks.',
              '<div class="l11-grid">' +
                card(
                  '📜 World quests',
                  'Quests that verify location, floor, NPC and the steps in progress.',
                  'v8:quests',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🏰 RPG story',
                  'Story chapters, decisions and rewards based on your original progress.',
                  'rpg:story',
                ) +
                card(
                  '✨ AI quests',
                  'English conversation scenarios; no reward unless the conditions are really met.',
                  'rpg:quests',
                ) +
                card(
                  '📓 Journal & activity',
                  'See the characters you have met and your activity in the world.',
                  'v8:journal',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          else if (name === 'learn')
            h = group(
              'Learn English',
              'NPC conversations, Gemini AI Coach, grammar fixes, vocabulary, CEFR and flashcards from the existing modules.',
              '<div class="l11-grid">' +
                card(
                  '🤖 AI Coach PRO',
                  'Chat, practise sentences, get explanations, auto-adjust difficulty and control the game with language.',
                  'ai',
                ) +
                card(
                  '📖 Dictionary & sentence fixes',
                  'Look up Vietnamese / English words, save vocabulary and get hints.',
                  'helper',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🃏 Flashcards & Quiz',
                  'Uses the learning flow already in the AI Coach, with no fake achievements.',
                  'ai:quiz',
                  'Practise now',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🎓 Choose CEFR level',
                  'Pick A1, A2, B1, B2 or C1 to adjust the conversation difficulty.',
                  'levels',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🔊 Voice & microphone',
                  'Set NPC speech and speaking speed when the browser supports it.',
                  'voice',
                ) +
                card(
                  '💳 Learn finance',
                  'V10 lessons on prices, saving and budgeting conversations.',
                  'v10:learning',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          else if (name === 'economy')
            h = group(
              'Smart Economy',
              'All prices, purchase conditions and balances come from V10. Trade only at the right NPC or place.',
              `<div class="l11-statline">💰 Wallet: <b>$${s.money}</b> · 🏦 Savings: <b>$${s.bank}</b> <span class="l11-pill">(In-game virtual money)</span></div><div class="l11-grid">` +
                card(
                  '🛒 Shop',
                  'Buy goods and see prices and locations; no remote buying until conditions are met.',
                  'v10:shop',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🎒 Everyday items',
                  'See food, tools, train tickets, books and other items in your V10 bag.',
                  'v10:bag',
                ) +
                card(
                  '📦 RPG / story items',
                  'A separate RPG inventory; quest items are never mixed up or lost.',
                  'rpg:bag',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🏦 Bank',
                  'Deposit or withdraw at the Bank, and see savings goals and the real balance.',
                  'v10:bank',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🏠 Home decoration',
                  "See the furniture you bought and update Hero's Home when you are in the right place.",
                  'v10:home',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🔨 Jobs & mining',
                  'Use the V10 limited jobs and mining system in the Bag tab.',
                  'v10:bag',
                  'Open jobs',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🧾 Receipts & history',
                  'See transactions and export CSV; money data is never duplicated.',
                  'v10:ledger',
                ) +
                card(
                  '📘 Money skills',
                  'Learn budget choices and shopping conversations in English.',
                  'v10:learning',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          else if (name === 'character')
            h = group(
              'Character & relationships',
              'Information comes from the current save, keeping NPC relationships, level and AI memory unchanged.',
              `<div class="l11-statline">⭐ Level: ${s.level} · XP: ${s.xp} · 💰 $${s.money}<div>Location: ${safeHTML(s.area)}${s.inside ? ' / ' + safeHTML(s.inside) : ''}</div></div><div class="l11-grid">` +
                card(
                  '👤 Profile & XP',
                  'Your level, experience and current RPG progress.',
                  'rpg:story',
                ) +
                card(
                  '♥ NPC relationships',
                  'Trust level, number of chats and daily schedules from the RPG data.',
                  'rpg:people',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🧠 AI memory',
                  'Important information the player allows to be saved, with an option to delete it.',
                  'rpg:memory',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🎒 RPG inventory',
                  'Check the quest items and achievements saved in the RPG.',
                  'rpg:bag',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          else if (name === 'achievements')
            h = window.LQAchievements
              ? window.LQAchievements.renderPage()
              : group('Achievements', 'The achievement system could not be loaded.', '');
          else
            h = group(
              'Settings & help',
              'Set your level, graphics, sound and backups using the original features.',
              '<div class="l11-grid">' +
                card(
                  '📱 Device & controls',
                  'Playing on: ' +
                    ((window.LQDevice?.list || []).find((d) => d.id === window.LQDevice?.get())?.title || 'not chosen') +
                    '. Change it to get the right on-screen controls.',
                  'device',
                  'Change',
                ) +
                card('💾 Data backup', 'Export, import and protect your current progress.', 'settings') +
                card(
                  '🎨 Effects & performance',
                  'Low / Normal / High, minimap, compass, weather, sound, contrast.',
                  'v8:settings',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🔊 Voice & speed',
                  'NPC voice, auto-play and a suitable English voice.',
                  'voice',
                ) +
                card('🎓 CEFR', 'Choose a level from A1 to C1.', 'levels') +
                card(
                  '💾 Export / import save',
                  'V8 backup; the Gemini API key is never included in the exported file.',
                  'v8:settings',
                  'Open backup',
                ) +
                card(
                  '❔ Help & shortcuts',
                  'Controls and how to play the game.',
                  'guide',
                ) +
                card(
                  '🎬 Intro & tutorial',
                  'Watch the opening scene again and replay the step-by-step tutorial.',
                  'tutorial',
                  'Replay',
                ) +
                '</div>',
            );
          t.innerHTML = h;
          selected = name;
          el('l11-nav')
            ?.querySelectorAll('button')
            .forEach((b) => {
              b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.l11Page === name);
              b.setAttribute('aria-current', b.dataset.l11Page === name ? 'page' : 'false');
            });
        }
        function close() {
          const x = shell();
          if (!x || x.hidden) return;
          x.hidden = true;
          el('l11-menu-btn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
          (lastFocus?.isConnected ? lastFocus : el('l11-menu-btn'))?.focus?.({
            preventScroll: true,
          });
        }
        function existingIsOpen() {
          return [
            'chat',
            'helper',
            'journal',
            'quests',
            'settings',
            'briefing',
            'v10-panel',
            'rpg-panel',
            'v8-panel',
            'ai-panel',
            'lq-guide',
            'lq-levels',
            'lq-voice',
          ].some((id) => {
            const x = el(id);
            if (!x) return false;
            if (x.hidden) return false;
            if (
              id === 'chat' ||
              id === 'helper' ||
              id === 'journal' ||
              id === 'quests' ||
              id === 'settings' ||
              id === 'briefing'
            )
              return getComputedStyle(x).display !== 'none';
            return true;
          });
        }
        function open(name = 'maps') {
          if (!shell()) return;
          if (existingIsOpen()) return;
          lastFocus = document.activeElement;
          shell().hidden = false;
          el('l11-menu-btn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
          page(name);
          if (name === 'maps') el('l11-nav').scrollLeft = 0;
          el('l11-close')?.focus();
        }
        function toggle() {
          if (!shell().hidden) close();
          else open('maps');
        }
        function adapt(type, tab) {
          close();
          if (type === 'v10') {
            window.LQ10_ECONOMY?.show(tab || 'shop');
            return;
          }
          if (type === 'v8') {
            el('v8-menu-btn')?.click();
            if (tab && tab !== 'quests')
              el('v8-panel')?.querySelector(`[data-v8-tab="${tab}"]`)?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (type === 'rpg') {
            el('rpg-open')?.click();
            if (tab) {
              const node = [...(el('rpg-tabs')?.querySelectorAll('button') || [])].find(
                (b) =>
                  b.dataset.tab === tab ||
                  b.dataset.rpgTab === tab ||
                  b.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(tab),
              );
              node?.click();
            }
            return;
          }
        }
        function action(a) {
          if (!a) return;
          if (a.startsWith('ach:')) return window.LQAchievements?.action(a.slice(4));
          if (a.startsWith('travel:')) {
            const id = a.slice(7);
            const sc = window.__lifequestGetScene?.();
            if(window.LQCareerWorlds && !window.LQCareerWorlds.allowed(id)){window.LQCareerWorlds.feedback(sc,id);return;}
            const destination = window.LQWorldMaps?.destination?.(id);
            if (!sc || !destination || typeof sc.switchArea !== 'function' || sc.switching) return;
            if (sc.area?.id === id) return;
            close();
            sc.switchArea(destination.id, destination.spawn, '🧭 Travelled using the Map Menu');
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'map') {
            close();
            const x = el('v8-map-hide'); // do not mutate prefs without using the original shortcut handler
            document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'm', bubbles: true }));
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'ai' || a === 'ai:quiz') {
            close();
            el('ai-open')?.click();
            if (a === 'ai:quiz') {
              const inp = el('ai-input');
              if (inp) {
                inp.value = 'quiz';
                inp.focus();
              }
            }
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'helper') {
            close();
            el('group-menu')?.querySelector('[data-act="helper"]')?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'settings') {
            close();
            el('group-menu')?.querySelector('[data-act="settings"]')?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'guide') {
            close();
            el('guide-open')?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'device') {
            close();
            window.LQDevice?.change();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'tutorial') {
            close();
            window.LQIntro?.replay();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'voice') {
            close();
            el('voice-open')?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (a === 'levels') {
            close();
            el('level-open')?.click();
            return;
          }
          if (a.startsWith('v10:')) return adapt('v10', a.slice(4));
          if (a.startsWith('rpg:')) return adapt('rpg', a.slice(4));
          if (a.startsWith('v8:')) return adapt('v8', a.slice(3));
        }
        function updateHUD() {
          const sc = window.__lifequestGetScene?.(),
            g = sc?.store?.game;
          if (!sc || !g) return;
          const inside = sc.inside;
          const q = V8?.currentQuest?.();
          const step = V8?.nextStep?.();
          const place = sc.area?.name || 'English Town';
          const loc = inside?.b?.name
            ? ` · ${inside.b.name}${inside.floor ? ' · floor ' + inside.floor : ''}`
            : '';
          const l = `${place}${loc} · ${window.LQPast?.dayLabel ? window.LQPast.dayLabel(g.day) : `Day ${g.day || 1}`} ·${String(Math.floor((g.minutes || 0) / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.floor((g.minutes || 0) % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
          if (el('l11-place')?.textContent !== l) el('l11-place').textContent = l;
          const chatNode = el('chat');
          const talking = !!chatNode && getComputedStyle(chatNode).display !== 'none';
          el('l11-current').hidden = talking;
          el('ai-open').hidden = talking;
          const a = adventure?.state?.() || {};
          const r = a.level || 1,
            xp = a.xp || 0;
          const hud = `Lv. ${r} · $${g.money || 0}`;
          if (hud !== lastHud) {
            lastHud = hud;
            el('l11-stats').textContent = hud;
            el('l11-xp').textContent = `${xp} XP`;
          }
          // V8 objective is authoritative; fallback to original quest log when unavailable.
          let title = q?.title || 'Explore & learn English',
            detail = step?.text || 'Press G to see your quests';
          if (!q?.title) {
            const goal = sc.quests?.hudLine?.();
            if (goal) detail = String(goal).slice(0, 95);
          }
          if (el('l11-objective').textContent !== title) el('l11-objective').textContent = title;
          if (el('l11-objdetail').textContent !== detail) el('l11-objdetail').textContent = detail;
        }
        function init() {
          const menu = el('l11-menu-btn');
          if (!menu) return;
          menu.addEventListener('click', toggle);
          el('l11-close')?.addEventListener('click', close);
          shell()?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === shell()) close();
          });
          el('l11-nav')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const b = e.target.closest('[data-l11-page]');
            if (b) page(b.dataset.l11Page);
          });
          el('l11-page')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const b = e.target.closest('[data-l11-action]');
            if (b) action(b.dataset.l11Action);
          });
          document.addEventListener(
            'keydown',
            (e) => {
              if (
                e.isComposing ||
                ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(e.target?.tagName) ||
                e.target?.isContentEditable
              )
                return;
              if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
              if (!shell().hidden) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                  close();
                  return;
                }
                if (e.key === 'Tab') {
                  const focus = [...shell().querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled])')].filter(
                    (b) => b.offsetParent !== null,
                  );
                  if (focus.length) {
                    const start = focus[0],
                      end = focus.at(-1);
                    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === start) {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      end.focus();
                    } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === end) {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      start.focus();
                    }
                  }
                }
                if (e.key === 'g' || e.key === 'G') e.preventDefault();
                e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                return;
              }
              if ((e.key === 'g' || e.key === 'G') && !e.repeat && !existingIsOpen()) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                open();
              }
            },
            true,
          );
          // Older visible shortcuts route through the one-nav shell; the targets themselves still retain native callbacks.
          el('group-btn')?.addEventListener(
            'click',
            (e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopImmediatePropagation();
              toggle();
            },
            true,
          );
          el('v8-menu-btn')?.addEventListener(
            'click',
            (e) => {
              if (e.isTrusted) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                open('maps');
              }
            },
            true,
          );
          updateHUD();
          setInterval(updateHUD, 650);
          window.LQ11_UI = { open, close, page, action };
        }
        return { init };
      })();

      adventure.init();
      V8.init();
      LQ10.init();
      startGame();
      LQ11.init();
      $('lq-loading').hidden = true;
    

</script>
<script>
/*
 * LifeQuest loader.
 * The game used to be one huge function inside index.html. It is now split into small files under js/game/.
 * The files are plain slices of that function body, so they share one scope: we download them in order,
 * join them and run them as a single function. (This needs http://, i.e. localhost or GitHub Pages — not file://.)
 */
(function () {
  const startupInfo = document.getElementById('lq-loading');

  function displayError(message) {
    startupInfo.hidden = false;
    startupInfo.innerHTML =
      '<strong>Could not load the game</strong><p>' +
      message +
      '</p><small>Reload the page. The Phaser 4.2.1 graphics library is loaded from vendor/phaser.min.js (or a CDN if that file is missing).</small>';
  }

  function loadScript(src) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const s = document.createElement('script');
      s.src = src;
      s.async = true;
      s.onload = () => resolve();
      s.onerror = () => reject(Error(src));
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    });
  }

  async function loadPhaser() {
    // The single-file build already embeds Phaser, so window.Phaser exists and nothing is downloaded.
    if (window.Phaser) return window.Phaser;
    for (const u of [
      // Local copy first (split/ and LIFEQUEST_deploy ship vendor/phaser.min.js), so the game starts without Internet.
      'vendor/phaser.min.js',
      'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/phaser/4.2.1/phaser.min.js',
      'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/phaser@4.2.1/dist/phaser.min.js',
      'https://unpkg.com/phaser@4.2.1/dist/phaser.min.js',
    ]) {
      try {
        await loadScript(u);
        if (window.Phaser) return window.Phaser;
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Could not load Phaser from', u);
      }
    }
    throw Error('Could not load the Phaser library');
  }

  const PARTS = [
    'js/game/01-services.js',
    'js/game/02-core-npcs-and-schedules.js',
    'js/game/03-core-areas-and-map.js',
    'js/game/04-core-quest-helpers.js',
    'js/game/05a-core-npc-dialogue-profiles.js',
    'js/game/05b-core-npc-dialogue-profiles.js',
    'js/game/06-core-npc-chat-engine.js',
    'js/game/07a-core-ai-provider-and-world-art.js',
    'js/game/07b-core-ai-provider-and-world-art.js',
    'js/game/08-core-interiors.js',
    'js/game/09-core-graphics-v9.js',
    'js/game/10-core-characters-and-scene.js',
    'js/game/11-study-lab.js',
    'js/game/12-adventure-rpg.js',
    'js/game/13-assistant-core.js',
    'js/game/14-v8-quests-and-minimap.js',
    'js/game/15-ui-assistant-panel.js',
    'js/game/16-economy.js',
    'js/game/17-train-and-tickets.js',
    'js/game/18-economy-shop-and-npc-hooks.js',
    'js/game/19-menu-v11.js',
    'js/game/20-startup.js',
  ];

  async function loadGameSource() {
    // The single-file build (build-single.ps1) embeds the game source in the page.
    const embedded = document.getElementById('lq-game-src');
    if (embedded) return embedded.textContent + '\n//# sourceURL=lifequest-game.js';
    if (location.protocol === 'file:') {
      throw new Error(
        'Open the game over http (e.g. http://localhost:8080/) or GitHub Pages, not directly via file://.',
      );
    }
    const texts = await Promise.all(
      // 22 files at once: a simple local server (python -m http.server) sometimes drops a connection ("Failed to
      // fetch"), so each file is tried up to 3 times before giving up.
      PARTS.map(async (url) => {
        let last;
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
          try {
            const res = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-cache' });
            if (res.ok) return res.text();
            last = new Error('Could not load ' + url + ' (' + res.status + ')');
          } catch (e) {
            last = e;
          }
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300 * (attempt + 1)));
        }
        throw last;
      }),
    );
    return texts.join('\n') + '\n//# sourceURL=lifequest-game.js';
  }

  async function boot() {
    try {
      // Start downloading Phaser and the game while the title screen is shown.
      const phaserReady = loadPhaser();
      const sourceReady = loadGameSource();
      phaserReady.catch(() => {});
      sourceReady.catch(() => {});
      if (window.LQTitle) await window.LQTitle.show();
      // How the player plays (computer / phone / tablet): asked once, right after Start (device.js).
      if (window.LQDevice) await window.LQDevice.ask();
      if (window.LQAuth && !window.__lqVocaltPreviewMode) await window.LQAuth.start();
      const Phaser = await phaserReady;
      const source = await sourceReady;
      const AsyncFunction = Object.getPrototypeOf(async function () {}).constructor;
      const running = new AsyncFunction('Phaser', source)(Phaser);
      if (window.LQCreatorUI) window.LQCreatorUI.watch();
      await running;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('LifeQuest HTML failed to start:', e);
      displayError('Could not start: ' + (e?.message || e));
    }
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();

</script>

 }

 if(a==='tech-co'&&/(schedule|record|route|north|problem|map)/i.test(m))mark('digital-route','Bản đồ thiếu thông tin','An old northern route is missing from the digital display.','Một tuyến đường phía bắc chưa hiện đủ trên bảng số.','tech-co','Hội thoại về thông tin tuyến đường');

function esc(t){return String(t??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]))}
let panel,tab='story';
function status(c){let visit=!!data.visits[c.id],clues=Object.values(data.clues).filter(x=>x.area===c.id).length;return `${visit?'Đã ghé thăm':'Chưa ghé thăm'} · ${clues} manh mối`}
function render(){if(!panel||panel.hidden)return;const content=panel.querySelector('#ll-body');if(!content)return;
 if(tab==='story')content.innerHTML=`<p class="ll-lead">Một lá thư chưa về nhà. Hãy kết nối các dấu vết bằng những cuộc trò chuyện và hành trình thật.</p><div class="ll-grid">${CHAPTERS.map((c,i)=>`<article class="ll-ch"><small>CHAPTER ${i+1} · ${esc(c.level)} · ${esc(status(c))}</small><h3>${esc(c.title)}</h3><p>${esc(c.aim)}</p><p class="ll-note">${esc(c.hint)}</p><div class="ll-grammar">${esc(c.grammar)}</div></article>`).join('')}</div>`;
 else if(tab==='clues')content.innerHTML=Object.keys(data.clues).length?Object.values(data.clues).map(e=>`<article class="ll-clue"><h3>✉ ${esc(e.title)}</h3><p>${esc(e.english)}</p><p>${esc(e.vietnamese)}</p><small>${esc(e.area)} · ${esc(e.origin||'')}</small></article>`).join(''):'<p>Chưa có manh mối. Hãy tiếp tục tương tác với NPC, The Old Map và các địa điểm trong game.</p>';
 else if(tab==='journey')content.innerHTML=CHAPTERS.map((c,i)=>`<div class="ll-place">${data.visits[c.id]?'✓':'○'} ${i+1}. ${esc(c.title)} <small>${data.visits[c.id]?new Date(data.visits[c.id]).toLocaleDateString('vi-VN'):'Chưa khám phá'}</small></div>`).join('');
 else if(tab==='skills')content.innerHTML=CHAPTERS.map(c=>`<article class="ll-clue"><strong>${esc(c.title)} · ${esc(c.level)}</strong><p>${esc(c.topic)}</p><div class="ll-grammar">${esc(c.grammar)}</div></article>`).join('');
 else content.innerHTML=data.history.length?data.history.map(x=>`<p class="ll-place">${esc(x.message)}<small>${new Date(x.at).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}</small></p>`).join(''):'<p>Chuyến phiêu lưu sắp bắt đầu.</p>';
 panel.querySelectorAll('[data-ll-tab]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.llTab===tab));}
