<script id="lq-physics-checks">
/* V52.2 · precise polygon diagnostics, no sprites or assets changed. */
(function(){'use strict';
  window.LQPhysicsFix=Object.freeze({
    version:'52.2',
    check(x,y){const s=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();
      if(!s?.area||!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))return null;
      return {area:s.area.id, worldX:x,worldY:y,
        tileX:Math.floor(x/32),tileY:Math.floor(y/32),
        solid:s.blocked(x,y), geometry:window.LQPrecisePhysics?.debug(s.area,x,y),
        obstacleTile:s.area.isTileBlocked(Math.floor(x/32),Math.floor(y/32))};
    },
    current(){const s=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();
      return s?.player?this.check(s.player.x,s.player.y):null;
    },
    mapStats(){const s=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();const a=s?.area;
      if(!a?.blocked)return null;
      const count=a.blocked.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.reduce((n,v)=>n+(v?1:0),0),0);
      return {area:a.id,buildings:a.buildings.length,obstacles:a.obstacles.length,solidTiles:count,
        tileCount:a.w*a.h};
    }
  });
})();
</script>

  const cap=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  const dist=(a,b,x,y)=>Math.hypot(a-x,b-y);
  const hash = str => {let h=2166136261; for(const c of String(str))h=Math.imul(h^c.charCodeAt(0),16777619);return h>>>0;};
  let actor=null, root=null, pane=null, widget=null, toastEl=null, keyLock=false;

  const gameReady=sc=>!!(sc?.area&&sc?.player&&sc?.add?.graphics&&sc?.store?.game);
  const area=sc=>String(sc?.area?.id||'');
  const validRoom=sc=>!!sc?.inside?.spec?.items;
  function save(sc){try{sc.store?.save?.();return true;}catch(e){console.warn('[V66] Save not available',e);return false;}}
  function state(sc=scene()){
    if(!sc?.store?.game)return null;
    const g=sc.store.game;
    if(!g.v66||typeof g.v66!=='object'||g.v66.schema!==1) g.v66={schema:1,areas:{},objects:{},history:[],seen:{}};
    const d=g.v66;
    for (const k of ['areas','objects','seen']) if(!d[k]||typeof d[k]!=='object')d[k]={};
    if(!Array.isArray(d.history))d.history=[];
    return d;
  }
  function status(sc,key=area(sc)){
    const d=state(sc); if(!d)return null;
    if(!d.areas[key]) d.areas[key]={step:0,complete:false,visits:0};
    return d.areas[key];
  }
  const timeOf=sc=>{const g=sc?.store?.game||{},m=cap(Number(g.minutes??720),0,1439);
    return {minutes:m,day:Math.max(1,Number(g.day)||1), weather:String(g.weather||'clear').toLowerCase(),
      phase:m<390?'night':m<610?'morning':m<1000?'day':m<1150?'evening':'night'};
  };
  function record(sc,title){const d=state(sc);if(!d)return;
    d.history.unshift({area:area(sc),title:String(title).slice(0,110),day:timeOf(sc).day});d.history=d.history.slice(0,32);
    save(sc);
  }
  function buildings(sc){return Array.isArray(sc?.area?.buildings)?sc.area.buildings:[];}
  function matchBuilding(sc,cfg){
    if(!cfg)return null;
    const bs=buildings(sc),key=cfg.building.toLowerCase();
    return bs.find(b=>String(b.name||'').toLowerCase()===key) ||
      bs.find(b=>String(b.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(key)) || null;
  }
  function safe(sc,x,y){return Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)&&x>24&&y>24&&x<(sc.area.w-1)*32&&y<(sc.area.h-1)*32&&!sc.blocked?.(x,y);}
  function anchorFor(sc,cfg){
    const b=matchBuilding(sc,cfg);
    if(!b)return null;
    const x=(Number(b.door)+.5)*T, y=(Number(b.y)+Number(b.h))*T+23;
    // An interior's blocked() uses local room coordinates. Never feed it outdoor positions.
    if(sc.inside && String(sc.inside.b?.name||'').toLowerCase()===cfg.building.toLowerCase())return{x,y,b};
    if(safe(sc,x,y))return{x,y,b};
    for(const radius of [16,32,48,64,80,96]){
      for(const offset of [0,-16,16,-32,32,-48,48]){
        for(const back of [0,12,24]){
          const xx=x+offset,yy=y+radius+back;
          if(safe(sc,xx,yy))return{x:xx,y:yy,b};
        }
      }
    }
    return null; // Never suggest an unreachable access point.
  }
  function target(sc){const cfg=SCENES[area(sc)];return cfg?anchorFor(sc,cfg):null;}
  function nearTarget(sc){
    if(!gameReady(sc))return false;
    const cfg=SCENES[area(sc)], a=target(sc);
    if(!cfg||!a)return false;
    if(sc.inside)return String(sc.inside.b?.name||'').toLowerCase()===cfg.building.toLowerCase();
    return dist(sc.player.x,sc.player.y,a.x,a.y)<118;
  }
  function realItems(sc){
    if(!validRoom(sc))return [];
    const obj=sc.inside, pic=obj.objs?.[0], x=pic?.x,y=pic?.y,k=obj.k||1;
    if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))return [];
    return obj.spec.items.filter(it=>it&&Number.isFinite(it.x)&&Number.isFinite(it.y)&&Number.isFinite(it.w)&&Number.isFinite(it.h))
      .map((it,i)=>({key:[area(sc),obj.b.name,obj.floor||0,it.k,it.x,it.y,i].join('::'),it,
        x:x+(it.x+it.w*.5)*T*k,y:y+(it.y+it.h*.5)*T*k, k}));
  }
  function nearestItem(sc){return realItems(sc).filter(p=>/table|counter|desk|bed|shelf|stove|forge|machine|screen|loom|herb|plant|clock|book|cabinet|chest|piano|organ|case|furnace|lantern|fireplace|display|workbench/i.test(String(p.it.k||''))).filter(p=>dist(sc.player.x,sc.player.y,p.x,p.y)<75*p.k).sort((a,b)=>dist(sc.player.x,sc.player.y,a.x,a.y)-dist(sc.player.x,sc.player.y,b.x,b.y))[0]||null;}
  function inspectFurniture(sc){
    const p=nearestItem(sc);if(!p)return false;
    const d=state(sc);if(!d)return false;
    const next=!d.objects[p.key];d.objects[p.key]=next;
    const type=String(p.it.k||'object'), label=next?'được sử dụng':'được đặt lại';
    record(sc,`Nội thất ${type}: ${label}`);ping(sc, next?660:420);
    toast(`Vật thể ${type} ${label}.`);
    redrawPanel();return true;
  }
  function attend(sc){
    if(!gameReady(sc)||!SCENES[area(sc)])return {ok:false,reason:'Khu vực này không có hoạt động V66.'};
    if(!nearTarget(sc))return {ok:false,reason:'Hãy đến gần công trình được đánh dấu hoặc vào đúng phòng.'};
    const s=status(sc),cfg=SCENES[area(sc)];
    if(s.complete)return {ok:false,reason:'Bạn đã hoàn thành hoạt động này. Cảnh quan vẫn ghi nhớ kết quả.'};
    s.step=cap(Number(s.step)||0,0,2)+1;
    if(s.step>=3){s.complete=true;s.completedDay=timeOf(sc).day;}
    record(sc,(s.complete?'Hoàn thành: ':'Hoạt động: ')+cfg.steps[s.step-1]);
    ping(sc,740);toast(s.complete?cfg.result:cfg.steps[s.step-1]);redrawPanel();
    return{ok:true,step:s.step,complete:s.complete};
  }
  function updateLocation(sc){
    const aid=area(sc),room=sc.inside?.b?.name||null;
    if(aid!==currentArea){
      currentArea=aid;lastWeather=timeOf(sc).weather;wetUntil=0;
      const bs=buildings(sc),cfg=SCENES[aid];
      const seed=hash(aid);
      windowTiles=[];puddles=[];
      for(let i=0;i<bs.length;i++){
        if(i%3===seed%3){let b=bs[i];if(Number(b.w)>=2&&Number(b.h)>=2)
          windowTiles.push({x:(b.x+.8+((i*7)%Math.max(1,Math.floor(b.w-1))))*T,y:(b.y+b.h*.47)*T,id:i});}
      }
      const a=cfg?anchorFor(sc,cfg):null;
      if(a){for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
        let x=a.x-128+(i*47)%260,y=a.y+48+((i*29)%84);
        if(safe(sc,x,y)&&dist(x,y,a.x,a.y)>34)puddles.push({x,y,sz:4+(i%3)*2});
      }}
    }
    if(room!==currentRoom){currentRoom=room;redrawPanel();}
  }
  function box(g,x,y,w,h,c,a=1){g.fillStyle(c,a);g.fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(1,Math.round(w)),Math.max(1,Math.round(h)));}
  function ring(g,x,y,r,c,a=1,w=1){g.lineStyle(w,c,a);g.strokeCircle(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(1,r));}
  function line(g,x,y,x2,y2,c,a=1,w=1){g.lineStyle(w,c,a);g.lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(x2),Math.round(y2));}
  function shimmer(g,x,y,v,col,t,alpha=1){
    box(g,x,y,11,1,0x18202c,.6);box(g,x+1,y+2,9,1,col,alpha*.55);
    const tick=Math.floor(t/190)%8;box(g,x+tick,y-1,2,2,0xffe6b0,alpha);
    if(v>=2)box(g,x+1,y+5,9,2,0x95dba6,alpha*.7);
  }
  function drawSignature(g,cfg,x,y,step,t,clock,quiet){
    const color=cfg.color, tick=Math.floor(t/310)%3, pulse=(Math.sin(t*.004)+1)/2;
    // Tiny cinematic vignettes tied to real buildings (no collision, no fake NPC sprites).
    switch(cfg.type){
      case 'books':
        box(g,x-24,y-12,50,21,0x493c34,.78);box(g,x-19,y-8,39,12,0xe2d3aa,.55);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const off=(step>i?2:0);box(g,x-17+i*11,y-6-off,9,8,[0xa4e1bd,0xe9b188,0xabc6ef][i]);}
        shimmer(g,x+9,y-18,step,color,t);break;
      case 'rail':

        const wx=x-14+(step>=2?(Math.sin(t*.0012)*8):tick*3);box(g,wx-10,y-18,22,14,0x84684a,.9);box(g,wx-8,y-15,18,4,0xd8ad78,.8);
        for(let k of [-6,7])ring(g,wx+k,y-2,3,0x202b32,1,2);
        box(g,x+18,y-27,5,7,step===3?0x8fe3a0:0xc7a46a,.95);break;
      case 'letters':
        box(g,x-26,y-10,52,15,0x76563b,.8);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const ox=x-22+i*17+(step>i?2:0);
          box(g,ox,y-19-(step>i?3:0),14,10,0xf2debb,.96);line(g,ox,y-19,ox+7,y-13,0xb38d67,.6);}
        break;
      case 'gallery':
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const ox=x-30+i*22;box(g,ox,y-23,19,24,0x372c39,.86);box(g,ox+3,y-20,13,17,[0x7cccb4,0xc7a9d8,0xe7b995][i],.92);
          if(step>=2)box(g,ox+4,y-19,7,3,0xf8e4c5,.4);}
        if(step>=2)box(g,x-29,y-27,62,2,0xffe6be,.65);break;
      case 'terminal':
        box(g,x-31,y-26,62,29,0x132e3f,.9);box(g,x-27,y-22,54,20,0x29636c,.92);
        for(let i=0;i<4;i++)box(g,x-22+i*12,y-17+((i+tick)%3)*2,8,3,step>=2?0x8ee3c8:0x70aaba,.8);
        line(g,x-29,y+5,x+29,y+5,color,.8,2);if(step>=3)shimmer(g,x+13,y-31,step,color,t);break;
      case 'opera':
        box(g,x-32,y-25,65,30,0x5d3346,.86);box(g,x-27,y-20,55,20,0x987b6a,.8);
        const drape=step<2?27:11;
        for(let i=0;i<2;i++){let dx=i?x+31-drape:x-32;box(g,dx,y-25,drape,30,0x8a324d,.95);
          for(let j=1;j<4;j++)line(g,dx+j*drape/4,y-21,dx+j*drape/4,y+5,0xc87c81,.36,1);}
        if(step>=2)for(let i=0;i<3;i++)line(g,x-20+i*20,y-28,x-13+i*14,y+3,0xf8e3b2,.25,2);
        break;
      case 'herbs':
        box(g,x-28,y-11,56,13,0x73543b,.8);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let bx=x-18+i*18;box(g,bx-6,y-19-(step>i?3:0),13,9,0xbd9c69,.95);
          ring(g,bx,y-20-(step>i?3:0),3,[0x85b78a,0xc9c783,0x7fa9a8][i],.9,2);}
        if(!quiet){for(let j=0;j<2;j++){let xx=x-19+j*36+Math.sin(t*.001+j)*1.5;
          line(g,xx,y-29,xx-2,y-37,color,.42,1);}}
        break;
      case 'merchant':
        box(g,x-32,y-26,64,6,0x9a5a47,.94);box(g,x-28,y-18,56,24,0x6c543e,.88);
        for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let ox=x-21+i*14;ring(g,ox,y-11,5,0xd7b77d,.92,2);
          line(g,ox,y-11,ox+Math.cos(t*.0005+i)*3,y-11+Math.sin(t*.0005+i)*3,0xe8e7d1,.9);}
        if(step>=2)for(let i=0;i<3;i++)box(g,x-15+i*15,y+1,7,3,0xf2d48e,.76);
        break;
    }
    if(prefs.focus && step<3 && !quiet)ring(g,x,y-14,37+pulse*3,color,.14,1);
  }
  function visible(sc,x,y,pad=180){
    const cam=sc.cameras?.main;if(!cam)return true;
    const view=cam.worldView;if(view&&Number.isFinite(view.x))return x>view.x-pad&&x<view.right+pad&&y>view.y-pad&&y<view.bottom+pad;
    return true;
  }
  function drawWindows(sc,g,t,clock){
    if(!prefs.windows)return;
    const night=clock.phase==='night'||clock.phase==='evening';
    const max=prefs.quiet?5:10;let drawn=0;
    for(const p of windowTiles){if(drawn>=max)break;if(!visible(sc,p.x,p.y))continue;
      const lit=night||((Math.floor(t/2300)+p.id)%4===0);
      box(g,p.x,p.y,8,9,0x1f3137,.72);box(g,p.x+2,p.y+2,4,5,lit?0xf9d6a0:0x7ca29c,lit?.65:.22);
      if(lit && !prefs.quiet && clock.phase==='night')box(g,p.x-2,p.y-2,11,12,0xe9bd7d,.06);
      drawn++;
    }
  }
  function drawWeather(sc,g,t,clock){
    if(!prefs.weather)return;
    const raining=/rain|storm|mưa|mua|wet/i.test(clock.weather);
    if(lastWeather && /rain|storm|mưa|mua|wet/i.test(lastWeather) && !raining) wetUntil=t+45000;
    lastWeather=clock.weather;
    if(raining)wetUntil=t+6000;
    if(t>wetUntil)return;
    const a=raining?.14:.12*cap((wetUntil-t)/45000,0,1);
    for(const p of puddles){if(!visible(sc,p.x,p.y))continue;
      g.fillStyle(0x9dcace,a);g.fillEllipse(p.x,p.y,p.sz*5,p.sz*2);
      if(!prefs.quiet && Math.floor(t/480)%3===0)ring(g,p.x,p.y,p.sz*1.8,0xd8eff3,a*1.5);
    }
  }
  function drawActorIntent(sc,g,t,a,stage){
    if(!Array.isArray(sc.npcs))return;
    let n=0;
    for(const npc of sc.npcs){if(n>=3)break;
      const s=npc?.sprite;if(!s?.visible||npc.inside||!visible(sc,s.x,s.y,65))continue;
      if(dist(s.x,s.y,a.x,a.y)>145)continue;
      const y=s.y-39+Math.sin(t*.004+n)*2;
      box(g,s.x-9,y-7,18,13,0x1b3540,.78);
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++)box(g,s.x-6+j*5,y-2,3,3,stage>=3?0x9ce2af:0xe1d7ae,.9);
      n++;
    }
  }
  function drawOutdoor(sc,t){
    if(!worldGraphics||!gameReady(sc))return;
    worldGraphics.clear();if(!prefs.enabled||sc.inside)return;
    const cfg=SCENES[area(sc)],a=cfg?anchorFor(sc,cfg):null,clock=timeOf(sc);
    if(a){
      const statusNow=status(sc), rawStep=statusNow?.step||0;
      // Daily scene choreography is visual; quest progress is stored separately.
      const step=statusNow?.complete?3:Math.max(rawStep,clock.phase==='morning'?1:clock.phase==='day'?2:0);
      const bx=(Number(a.b.door)+.5)*T,by=(Number(a.b.y)+Number(a.b.h)*.67)*T;
      if(visible(sc,bx,by)){
        drawSignature(worldGraphics,cfg,bx,by,step,t,clock,prefs.quiet);
        if(!prefs.quiet) drawActorIntent(sc,worldGraphics,t,a,step);
      }
      if(prefs.focus && !statusNow?.complete && visible(sc,a.x,a.y)){
        ring(worldGraphics,a.x,a.y,12+Math.sin(t*.003)*2,cfg.color,.45,2);
        box(worldGraphics,a.x-11,a.y-15,22,2,cfg.color,.65);
      }
    }
    drawWindows(sc,worldGraphics,t,clock);
    if(!prefs.quiet)drawWeather(sc,worldGraphics,t,clock);
  }
  function drawInterior(sc,t){
    indoorGraphics?.clear();
    if(!indoorGraphics||!prefs.enabled||!prefs.interior||!validRoom(sc))return;
    const clock=timeOf(sc),all=realItems(sc),d=state(sc),lim=prefs.quiet?3:5;
    const bx=sc.inside.objs[0].x, by=sc.inside.objs[0].y,k=sc.inside.k||1;
    // Light from actual wall band, never drawn into physical doorway.
    const tone=clock.phase==='evening'||clock.phase==='night'?0xf6d39a:0xe1e5cb;
    for(const i of [0,1]){
      const x=bx+sc.inside.spec.w*T*k*(.21+i*.58),y=by+22*k;
      box(indoorGraphics,x-7*k,y,14*k,3*k,tone,.3);
      if(!prefs.quiet&&prefs.particles)box(indoorGraphics,x-3*k,y+7*k,6*k,2*k,tone,.1);
    }
    let count=0;
    for(const obj of all){
      if(count>=lim)break;
      const kind=String(obj.it.k||'');
      if(!/table|counter|desk|bed|shelf|stove|forge|machine|screen|loom|herb|plant|clock|book|cabinet|chest|piano|organ|case|furnace|lantern|fireplace|display|workbench/i.test(kind))continue;
      if(!visible(sc,obj.x,obj.y,65))continue;
      const on=!!d.objects[obj.key],x=obj.x,y=obj.y;
      const c=on?0xbce8b2:0xe7c99b,alpha=on?.8:.3;
      box(indoorGraphics,x-8*k,y-6*k,16*k,2*k,c,alpha);
      if(on){box(indoorGraphics,x-5*k,y-11*k,10*k,3*k,c,.52);
        if(!prefs.quiet && prefs.particles){let dx=Math.sin(t*.002+count)*2*k;box(indoorGraphics,x+dx,y-15*k,2*k,2*k,c,.65);}}
      count++;
    }
  }
  function attach(sc){
    if(!gameReady(sc)||sc.__lq66Attached)return;
    current=sc;
    // One graphical layer per environment. No fake obstacles or replacement NPC sprites.
    worldGraphics=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(7.15);
    indoorGraphics=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(2.15);
    worldGraphics.__lq66=true;indoorGraphics.__lq66=true;
    const handleUpdate=(time,delta)=>{
      if(!gameReady(sc)||sc!==scene())return;
      const now=Number.isFinite(time)?time:performance.now();
      if(now-lastPulse< (prefs.quiet?190:85))return;
      const stamp=performance.now(); lastPulse=now;
      updateLocation(sc);drawOutdoor(sc,now);drawInterior(sc,now);
      const drawMs=performance.now()-stamp;
      benchmark.drawMs=benchmark.drawMs*.9+drawMs*.1;benchmark.drawCount++;
      if(root){const active=!sc.switching&&!sc.sleeping;
        root.hidden=!active;
        const hint=$('lq66-near');if(hint){const near=nearTarget(sc);
          const furn=validRoom(sc)&&!!nearestItem(sc);
          hint.hidden=!(active&&!sc.chat?.isOpen&&!paneOpen()&&(near||furn));
          hint.textContent=near?'F3 · Hoạt động vùng':furn?'F3 · Nội thất':'V66';
        }
      }
      if(paneOpen() && now-lastUI>950){lastUI=now;refreshStatus();}
    };
    sc.events?.on?.('update',handleUpdate);
    sc.events?.once?.('shutdown',()=>{
      sc.events?.off?.('update',handleUpdate);
      if(worldGraphics)worldGraphics.destroy();if(indoorGraphics)indoorGraphics.destroy();
      worldGraphics=null;indoorGraphics=null;current=null;currentArea='';sc.__lq66Attached=false;
    });
    sc.__lq66Attached=true;
  }
  function ping(sc,frequency){
    if(!prefs.sound)return;
    try{const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return;
      audioCtx=audioCtx||new AC(); if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume();
      let o=audioCtx.createOscillator(),gain=audioCtx.createGain();
      o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=frequency;gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.018,audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,audioCtx.currentTime+.13);
      o.connect(gain);gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);o.start();o.stop(audioCtx.currentTime+.14);
    }catch(_){}
  }
  function toast(str){if(!toastEl)return;toastEl.textContent=String(str);toastEl.hidden=false;
    const token=++toast.token;setTimeout(()=>{if(token===toast.token)toastEl.hidden=true;},3400);
  }
  toast.token=0;

  function focusCamera(sc){
    if(!gameReady(sc)||sc.switching||sc.sleeping||sc.inside)return false;
    const cfg=SCENES[area(sc)],a=cfg?anchorFor(sc,cfg):null,cam=sc.cameras?.main;
    if(!a||!cam||typeof cam.pan!=='function')return false;
    const here=area(sc);close();
    try{cam.stopFollow?.();cam.pan((a.b.x+a.b.w/2)*T,(a.b.y+a.b.h/2)*T,850,'Sine.easeInOut');}
    catch(err){console.warn('[V66] Camera',err);cam.startFollow?.(sc.player);return false;}
    setTimeout(()=>{if(scene()===sc && area(sc)===here && !sc.switching)cam.startFollow?.(sc.player,true,.1,.1);},1600);
    return true;
  }
  function paneOpen(){return !!pane&&!pane.hidden;}
  function close(){if(pane)pane.hidden=true;}
  function open(){if(!pane)return;redrawPanel();pane.hidden=false;}
  function renderStatus(sc){
    if(!gameReady(sc))return '<p>Hãy bắt đầu chơi để xem các hoạt động.</p>';
    const id=area(sc),cfg=SCENES[id],st=cfg?status(sc):null,clock=timeOf(sc),targetNow=cfg?target(sc):null;
    let o=`<div class="lq66-sub"><b>${html(sc.area?.name||id)}</b> · Ngày ${clock.day} · ${String(Math.floor(clock.minutes/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(clock.minutes%60).padStart(2,'0')} · ${html(clock.phase)}</div>`;
    if(cfg){
      o+=`<h3>${html(cfg.name)}</h3><div class="lq66-progress">${[0,1,2].map(i=>`<span class="${i<st.step?'done':''}">${i+1}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
      o+=`<p>${html(st.complete?cfg.result:cfg.steps[st.step])}</p>`;
      o+=`<p class="lq66-sub">${targetNow?(nearTarget(sc)?'Đã ở gần điểm hoạt động.':'Đến gần '+html(cfg.building)+' để tham gia.'):'Chưa tìm thấy vị trí tiếp cận hợp lệ của '+html(cfg.building)+'.'}</p>`;
      o+=`<button data-v66="progress" ${st.complete||!nearTarget(sc)||!targetNow?'disabled':''}>${st.complete?'✓ Đã hoàn thành':'Thực hiện bước '+(st.step+1)}</button>`;
      if(!sc.inside&&targetNow)o+=` <button data-v66="camera">Quan sát địa điểm</button>`;
    }else if(TRAILS[id])o+=`<h3>${html(TRAILS[id].name)}</h3><p>Đoạn đường thám hiểm có chuyển động theo môi trường. Nhiệm vụ gốc vẫn hoạt động bình thường.</p>`;
    else o+='<p>Không có sự kiện V66 cho khu vực này.</p>';
    if(sc.inside){const obj=nearestItem(sc);
      o+=`<div class="lq66-line"><h3>Tương tác nội thất</h3><p>${obj?'Đang ở gần: '+html(obj.it.k):'Hãy đến gần bàn, giường, tủ hoặc máy móc.'}</p><button data-v66="furniture" ${obj?'':'disabled'}>Dùng vật thể gần nhất</button></div>`;
    }
    const done=Object.values(state(sc)?.areas||{}).filter(v=>v.complete).length;
    o+=`<p class="lq66-sub">Tiến độ V66: ${done}/8 cảnh sinh hoạt · Tiến độ được lưu cùng trò chơi.</p>`;
    return o;
  }
  function settingsHTML(){return '<div class="lq66-settings">'+Object.entries({enabled:'Bật V66',quiet:'Giảm chuyển động',windows:'Cửa sổ có sinh hoạt',weather:'Dấu vết sau mưa',interior:'Hoạt cảnh trong nhà',focus:'Nhấn mạnh điểm tương tác',particles:'Hạt chuyển động nhẹ',sound:'Âm báo ngắn'}).map(([k,name])=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-pref66="${k}" ${prefs[k]?'checked':''}/> ${name}</label>`).join('')+'</div>'}
  function redrawPanel(){
    if(!pane)return;
    const el=$('lq66-content');if(el)el.innerHTML=renderStatus(scene());
    const statuses=$('lq66-statuses');
    if(statuses){const sc=scene(),d=state(sc);statuses.innerHTML=Object.entries(SCENES).map(([id,conf])=>{
      const step=d?.areas?.[id]?.step||0,done=!!d?.areas?.[id]?.complete;
      return `<div class="lq66-event"><span>${html(conf.name)}</span><strong>${done?'✓':step+'/3'}</strong></div>`;
    }).join('');}
    refreshStatus();
  }
  function refreshStatus(){
    if(!paneOpen())return;
    const sc=scene(),el=$('lq66-live');if(!el)return;
    const p=gameReady(sc)?timeOf(sc):null;
    el.textContent=p?`${html(area(sc))} · ${p.phase} · ${sc.inside?'Trong nhà':'Ngoài trời'} · Render V66 ~${benchmark.drawMs.toFixed(2)} ms/lượt`:'Chưa bắt đầu game';
  }
  function mount(){
    if(root)return;
    const css=document.createElement('style');css.id='lq66-css';css.textContent=`
      #lq66-root[hidden],#lq66-panel[hidden],#lq66-near[hidden],#lq66-toast[hidden]{display:none!important}
      #lq66-root{font:13px system-ui,sans-serif;color:#e5f4ee;position:fixed;z-index:9600;inset:0;pointer-events:none}
      #lq66-open{pointer-events:auto;position:absolute;right:12px;top:108px;color:#d7f5e6;background:#163b40e8;border:1px solid #75a79d;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 10px;box-shadow:0 3px 14px #0006;cursor:pointer}
      #lq66-near{pointer-events:auto;position:absolute;left:50%;bottom:125px;transform:translateX(-50%);padding:7px 11px;border:1px solid #a4cdad;border-radius:8px;background:#183c3ddd;color:#e9f6e5;cursor:pointer}
      #lq66-toast{position:absolute;top:22%;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);max-width:440px;min-width:170px;text-align:center;background:#19393fed;border:1px solid #b6ccaa;padding:12px 16px;border-radius:9px;box-shadow:0 8px 25px #0008}

      #lq66-panel .lq66-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px}

      #lq66-panel p{line-height:1.5;margin:8px 0 11px}#lq66-panel button{font:inherit;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid #74aa9d;border-radius:8px;background:#28595c;color:#eff8ed;padding:8px 11px}

      #lq66-panel .lq66-settings{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:9px}

      @media(max-width:550px){#lq66-open{right:7px;top:90px;padding:6px 8px}#lq66-panel .lq66-settings{grid-template-columns:1fr}#lq66-near{bottom:96px}}
    `;document.head.appendChild(css);
    root=document.createElement('div');root.id='lq66-root';root.hidden=true;
    root.innerHTML=`<button id="lq66-open" aria-label="Mở các cảnh V66" title="F3 · Living Moments">✦ V66</button><button id="lq66-near" hidden>F3 · Hoạt động</button><div id="lq66-toast" role="status" hidden></div>
    <div id="lq66-panel" hidden><section class="lq66-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="LifeQuest V66"><div class="lq66-head"><h2>V66 · Living Moments</h2><button data-v66="close">✕</button></div><p id="lq66-live" class="lq66-sub"></p>
    <div class="lq66-tabs"><button data-v66="tab:scene">Hoạt động</button><button data-v66="tab:events">Tất cả khu vực</button><button data-v66="tab:settings">Đồ họa</button></div>
    <div id="lq66-content"></div><div id="lq66-statuses" hidden></div><div id="lq66-preferences" hidden>${settingsHTML()}</div>
    <p class="lq66-sub">F3 để mở; E và tất cả điều khiển cũ vẫn hoạt động. V66 không thêm vật cản.</p></section></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(root);pane=$('lq66-panel');widget=$('lq66-open');toastEl=$('lq66-toast');
    widget.addEventListener('click',open);$('lq66-near').addEventListener('click',open);
    root.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target===pane){close();return;}
      const btn=e.target.closest?.('[data-v66]');if(!btn)return;
      const a=btn.dataset.v66,sc=scene();
      if(a==='close'){close();return;}
      if(a==='progress'){let r=attend(sc);if(!r.ok)toast(r.reason);return;}
      if(a==='furniture'){if(!inspectFurniture(sc))toast('Hãy tới gần một đồ nội thất.');return;}
      if(a==='camera'){if(!focusCamera(sc))toast('Chưa thể mở camera tại đây.');return;}
      if(a.startsWith('tab:')){
        for(const [id,tab] of [['lq66-content','scene'],['lq66-statuses','events'],['lq66-preferences','settings']]) $(id).hidden=a!=='tab:'+tab;
        if(a==='tab:scene')redrawPanel();return;
      }
    });
    root.addEventListener('change',e=>{
      const key=e.target?.dataset?.pref66;if(!key||!(key in prefs))return;
      prefs[key]=!!e.target.checked;
      try{localStorage.setItem(CONFIG_KEY,JSON.stringify(prefs));}catch(_){}
      toast('Đã cập nhật thiết lập đồ họa V66.');
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      if(e.key==='Escape'&&paneOpen()){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();close();return;}
      if(e.key!=='F3'||e.repeat||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey||e.defaultPrevented)return;
      if(['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(document.activeElement?.tagName)||document.activeElement?.isContentEditable)return;
      if(!gameReady(scene()))return;
      e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();paneOpen()?close():open();
    },true);
  }
  function init(){mount();
    // Check for scene identity every second, no extra animation timer. Actual effects live in scene.events update.
    setInterval(()=>{const sc=scene();if(gameReady(sc)){if(!sc.__lq66Attached)attach(sc);
      if(root && !sc.switching)root.hidden=false;
    }else if(root){root.hidden=true;close();}},900);
  }
  const test=Object.freeze({
    timeOf, state, status, target, nearTarget, nearestItem, realItems, attend, inspectFurniture, matchBuilding,
    uniqueCount:()=>Object.keys(SCENES).length, stats:sc=>({area:area(sc),progress:state(sc)?.areas,objects:Object.keys(state(sc)?.objects||{}).length,
      anchor:target(sc),renderMs:benchmark.drawMs,renderCount:benchmark.drawCount}),
    emitFrame:(g,cfg,x,y,step,now)=>drawSignature(g,cfg,x,y,step,now,{phase:'day'},false)
  });
  window.LQ66=Object.freeze({version:VERSION,maps:SCENES,trails:TRAILS,open,close,focusCamera,settings:()=>({...prefs}),
    progress:()=>state(),attend:()=>attend(scene()),inspect:()=>inspectFurniture(scene()),test});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>

 const artUnlocked=sc=>ART_TASKS.every(t=>artProgress(sc).done.includes(t.id));
 const nextArtTask=sc=>ART_TASKS.find(t=>!artProgress(sc).done.includes(t.id))||null;
 const nearArt=(sc,at,r=3.75)=>!!(ext(sc)&&at&&Math.hypot(sc.player.x/TILE-(at[0]+.5),sc.player.y/TILE-(at[1]+.5))<=r);
 const artFilmShowing=()=>window.LQArtDistrict?.isFilming?.()||window.LQIntro?.isPlaying?.();
 function finishArtTask(sc,id,option){
  const task=nextArtTask(sc);
  if(!task||task.id!==id||!nearArt(sc,task.at)||artFilmShowing())return false;
  if(option!==task.answer){toast('Chưa chính xác. Hãy quan sát lại rồi chọn câu trả lời phù hợp.');return false;}
  const g=sc?.store?.game;if(!g)return false;
  const p=artProgress(sc);p.done.push(task.id);g.lq72ArtActivities={version:1,done:p.done};
  try{sc.store.save()}catch(e){console.warn('[V72 Art] Activity save:',e)}
  toast(task.done);return true;
 }
const ICONS={star:'★',eye:'◉',wave:'≈',bolt:'ϟ'};
const state=sc=>{
 const root=sc?.store?.game;if(!root)return {version:1,strokes:[]};
 const d=root.lq71CommunityWall;
 if(!d||!Array.isArray(d.strokes))return {version:1,strokes:[]};
 return d;
};
const persist=(sc,strokes)=>{
  if(!sc?.store?.game||!artUnlocked(sc)||!nearArt(sc,[9,32],4.25))return false;
 const clean=strokes.slice(-150).map(s=>({tool:s.tool,color:s.color,size:s.size,stamp:s.stamp||'star',points:(s.points||[]).slice(0,350).map(p=>[Math.round(p[0]),Math.round(p[1])])}));
 sc.store.game.lq71CommunityWall={version:1,strokes:clean};
 try{sc.store.save();return true}catch(e){console.warn('[V71] Save mural:',e);return false}
};
function wallBase(ctx){
 ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.fillStyle='#363844';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 for(let row=0;row<10;row++)for(let col=-1;col<15;col++){
  const x=col*42+(row%2?21:0),y=row*20;
  ctx.fillStyle=['#4b4b5b','#494453','#575160','#464552'][Math.abs(row*7+col*3)%4];ctx.fillRect(x+2,y+2,39,17);
  ctx.fillStyle='#68707e';ctx.fillRect(x+3,y+2,37,1);
 }
 ctx.fillStyle='#0e1a2c';ctx.globalAlpha=.23;ctx.fillRect(7,7,W-14,H-14);ctx.globalAlpha=1;
 // mural frame and guiding symbols from the older neighborhood story
 const colors=['#ffd447','#35c8d0','#ff4f87'];
 for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.strokeStyle=colors[i];ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeRect(7+i*4,7+i*4,W-14-i*8,H-14-i*8);}
 ctx.font='bold 15px monospace';ctx.fillStyle='#dacdbf';ctx.fillText('THE COMMUNITY WALL',30,33);
 ctx.fillStyle='#74c9c6';ctx.font='bold 11px monospace';ctx.fillText('EVERY MARK TELLS A STORY',29,H-21);
}
function drawingLayer(strokes){
 const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W;c.height=H;
 const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
 for(const s of strokes){const pts=Array.isArray(s.points)?s.points:[];if(!pts.length)continue;
  const c=s.tool==='eraser'?'#000000':(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(s.color)?s.color:'#ff4f87');
  ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation=s.tool==='eraser'?'destination-out':'source-over';ctx.strokeStyle=c;ctx.fillStyle=c;
  const size=Math.max(2,Math.min(28,Number(s.size)||7));ctx.lineWidth=size*(s.tool==='marker'?1.4:1);
  ctx.lineCap=s.tool==='marker'?'square':'round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  if(s.tool==='stamp'){
   let [x,y]=pts[0],r=size*2.8;ctx.beginPath();
   if(s.stamp==='eye'){ctx.ellipse(x,y,r,r*.54,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*.26,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
   else if(s.stamp==='wave'){
    ctx.moveTo(x-r,y);for(let i=0;i<=24;i++){const xx=x-r+2*r*i/24;ctx.lineTo(xx,y+Math.sin(i/24*Math.PI*2)*r*.37)}ctx.stroke();
   } else {
    const n=s.stamp==='bolt'?4:5;for(let i=0;i<n*2;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/n,rr=i%2?r*.42:r;const px=x+Math.cos(a)*rr,py=y+Math.sin(a)*rr;if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(px,py);else ctx.lineTo(px,py);}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
  }else if(s.tool==='spray'){
   for(let pi=0;pi<pts.length;pi++){
    const [x,y]=pts[pi];for(let j=0;j<12;j++){const angle=(j*2.399+pi*1.17),rad=(j%7+1)*size*.35;
     ctx.fillRect(Math.round(x+Math.cos(angle)*rad),Math.round(y+Math.sin(angle)*rad),j%4===0?3:2,2);
    }
   }
  }else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(...pts[0]);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(...pts[i]);
   if(pts.length===1){ctx.fillRect(pts[0][0]-size/2,pts[0][1]-size/2,size,size);}else ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.restore();
 }
 return c;
}
function renderMural(canvas, strokes){const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;wallBase(ctx);ctx.drawImage(drawingLayer(strokes),0,0);}
let panel=null,visible=false,mode='wall',painter=null,draw=null,root=null,notice=null,objects=[],linkedScene=null,lastInstall='',route=null,routeGraphic=null,ambient=null,lastActive=false;
let pendingPreview=0;
function makeCanvas(){const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W;c.height=H;return c;}
function displayOnWorld(sc){
 if(!ext(sc))return;
 const key='lq71_community_mural';
 const canvas=makeCanvas();renderMural(canvas,state(sc).strokes);
 if(sc.textures.exists(key))sc.textures.remove(key);
 sc.textures.addCanvas(key,canvas);
 const img=sc.add.image(4*T+18,25*T+108,key).setOrigin(0,0).setDisplaySize(140,50).setDepth(1.2);
 const frame=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.21);
 frame.lineStyle(3,0xe9c986,.98).strokeRect(4*T+16,25*T+106,144,54);
 frame.fillStyle(0x213344,.90).fillRect(4*T+17,25*T+161,142,10);
 const label=sc.add.text(4*T+20,25*T+161,artUnlocked(sc)?'COMMUNITY WALL · F5':'WALL · 3 ART TASKS',{fontFamily:'monospace',fontSize:'8px',color:'#ffe4a2'}).setDepth(1.25);
 objects.push(img,frame,label);
 if(sc.textures.exists('lq71_eye'))sc.textures.remove('lq71_eye');
 // Image is loaded asynchronously in the first frames; the next scene refresh attaches it.
 if(imgEye.complete&&imgEye.naturalWidth){sc.textures.addImage('lq71_eye',imgEye);const eye=sc.add.image(4*T+18,4*T+109,'lq71_eye').setOrigin(0,0).setDisplaySize(68,68).setDepth(1.18);
  const border=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.19);border.lineStyle(3,0xd4b778,.85).strokeRect(4*T+16,4*T+107,72,72);objects.push(eye,border);
 }
 const glow=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.15);objects.push(glow);
 ambient=glow;
 lastInstall=sc.area?.id+':'+((state(sc).strokes||[]).length);
}
function clearWorld(){for(const obj of objects)try{obj.destroy()}catch(_){}objects=[];ambient=null;
 if(routeGraphic){try{routeGraphic.destroy()}catch(_){}routeGraphic=null}
 route=null;linkedScene=null;lastInstall='';}
function toast(msg){if(!notice)return;notice.textContent=msg;notice.hidden=false;clearTimeout(pendingPreview);pendingPreview=setTimeout(()=>{if(notice)notice.hidden=true;},3500)}
function open(){
 const sc=current();if(!ext(sc)||artFilmShowing())return;
 const task=nextArtTask(sc),at=task?.at||[9,32];
 if(!nearArt(sc,at)){toast(task?'Hãy đến gần '+task.place+' để tham gia hoạt động.':'Hãy đến gần Mural Workshop để vẽ.');return;}
 mount();if(visible)return;visible=true;panel.hidden=false;
 document.body.classList.add('lq71-editor-open');drawTab(task?'activity':'wall');
}
function close(){visible=false;if(panel)panel.hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('lq71-editor-open');painter=null;}
function navTarget(sc,to){
 if(!ext(sc)||!sc.area?.isTileBlocked)return false;
 const w=sc.area.w,h=sc.area.h,source=[Math.floor(sc.player.x/TILE),Math.floor(sc.player.y/TILE)],idx=(x,y)=>y*w+x;
 const prev=new Int32Array(w*h).fill(-1),a=[source],origin=idx(...source),target=idx(...to);prev[origin]=origin;
 for(let i=0;i<a.length&&prev[target]===-1;i++){
  const [x,y]=a[i];for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){
   const nx=x+dx,ny=y+dy;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=w||ny>=h||sc.area.isTileBlocked(nx,ny))continue;
   const k=idx(nx,ny);if(prev[k]!==-1)continue;prev[k]=idx(x,y);a.push([nx,ny]);
  }
 }
 if(prev[target]===-1){toast('Không tìm thấy đường đi tới địa điểm này.');return false}
 const points=[];for(let k=target,j=0;j<w*h;j++){points.push([k%w,Math.floor(k/w)]);if(k===origin)break;k=prev[k]}
 points.reverse();route={points,target:to,label:'Art District',built:Date.now()};toast('Đã chỉ đường trên bản đồ · '+(points.length-1)+' ô.');return true;
}
function drawTab(tab){mode=tab;if(!panel)return;const sc=current();const outlet=$('lq71-body');
 const task=nextArtTask(sc),p=artProgress(sc);
 if(task){
  if(!nearArt(sc,task.at)){outlet.innerHTML='<p>Hãy đến đúng điểm sáng tác để tham gia.</p>';return;}
  outlet.innerHTML=`<div class="lq71-heading"><div><span class="lq71-kicker">ART DISTRICT · TRƯỚC KHI VẼ</span><h3>${safe(task.title)}</h3><p><b>${safe(task.place)}</b> · Hoàn thành ${p.done.length}/3 hoạt động.</p><p>${safe(task.intro)}</p></div></div>
   <div class="lq71-activity"><h4>${safe(task.question)}</h4>${task.options.map((o,i)=>`<button type="button" data-art-answer="${i}" data-art-id="${safe(task.id)}">${safe(o)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
   <p class="lq71-note">Sau ba hoạt động, hãy quay lại Mural Workshop để vẽ Community Wall. Không có công cụ vẽ trước khi mở khóa.</p>`;
  return;
 }
 if(tab!=='wall'||!nearArt(sc,[9,32],4.25)){outlet.innerHTML='<p>Đến gần Mural Workshop để vẽ.</p>';return;}
 if(tab==='wall'){
  outlet.innerHTML=`<div class="lq71-heading"><div><span class="lq71-kicker">CREATIVE STUDIO · ART DISTRICT</span><h3>MAKE YOUR MARK</h3><p>Sau khi hoàn thành ba hoạt động sáng tạo, bạn có thể thiết kế tranh riêng và lưu tác phẩm lên tường Mural Workshop.</p></div><img class="lq71-artmark" alt="ART pixel lettering" src="${ART_SRC}" /></div>

   <div class="lq71-row">${[['brush','Bút vẽ'],['marker','Bút bản lớn'],['spray','Phun sơn'],['stamp','Đóng dấu'],['eraser','Tẩy']].map(([k,v])=>`<button type="button" data-tool="${k}">${v}</button>`).join('')}<select id="lq71-stamp" aria-label="Dấu vẽ"><option value="star">★ Ngôi sao</option><option value="eye">◉ Con mắt</option><option value="wave">≈ Làn sóng</option><option value="bolt">ϟ Tia sáng</option></select></div>
   <div class="lq71-row"><button data-action="undo">↶ Hoàn tác</button><button data-action="reset">Xóa bản nháp</button><button class="lq71-primary" data-action="save">Lưu tác phẩm</button></div>
   <p class="lq71-note" id="lq71-editor-status">Đã hoàn thành ba hoạt động sáng tạo. Tranh được lưu khi bạn đứng gần Mural Workshop.</p>`;
  initPainting();
 } else if(tab==='eye'){
  outlet.innerHTML=`<div class="lq71-heading"><img class="lq71-eye" alt="Eye of Creation pixel artwork from the supplied V12.3 source" src="${EYE_SRC}"/><div><span class="lq71-kicker">THE THREE MARKS · ART HISTORY</span><h3>EYE OF CREATION</h3><p>Tác phẩm pixel được lưu trong bản V12.3 của bạn đại ca. Mình đã đưa nó vào mặt tiền Color Gallery của V71.</p><p><b>★ Ngôi sao:</b> tưởng tượng · <b>◉ Con mắt:</b> quan sát · <b>≈ Làn sóng:</b> ý tưởng chuyển động.</p><p><b>English practice:</b> I think the eye represents curiosity because artists learn by looking carefully.</p><button data-action="locateeye">🧭 Chỉ đường tới Color Gallery</button></div></div>`;

 }
}
function initPainting(){
  const sc=current();if(!artUnlocked(sc)||!nearArt(sc,[9,32],4.25))return;draw=$('lq71-draw');if(!draw)return;
 const saved=(state(sc).strokes||[]).map(s=>({...s,points:(s.points||[]).map(p=>p.slice())}));
 let strokes=saved,tool='brush',color='#ff4f87',size=7,stamp='star',working=null,pointer=null;
 const redraw=()=>{renderMural(draw,working?[...strokes,working]:strokes);$('lq71-editor-status').textContent=strokes.length+' nét đã vẽ · Nhấn Lưu tác phẩm để áp dụng vào bản đồ.';};
 redraw();
 const getPoint=e=>{const r=draw.getBoundingClientRect();return [Math.max(0,Math.min(W,Math.round((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*W/3)*3)),Math.max(0,Math.min(H,Math.round((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height*H/3)*3))]};
 draw.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==0&&e.pointerType==='mouse')return;e.preventDefault();pointer=e.pointerId;draw.setPointerCapture(pointer);working={tool,color,size,stamp,points:[getPoint(e)]};redraw();});
 draw.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(e.pointerId!==pointer||!working||working.tool==='stamp')return;const p=getPoint(e),last=working.points[working.points.length-1];if(Math.hypot(p[0]-last[0],p[1]-last[1])>=3&&working.points.length<350){working.points.push(p);redraw();}});
 const done=e=>{if(e.pointerId!==pointer)return;e.preventDefault();if(working){strokes.push(working);if(strokes.length>150)strokes=strokes.slice(-150);working=null;redraw()}pointer=null;};
 draw.addEventListener('pointerup',done);draw.addEventListener('pointercancel',done);
 const pb=panel.querySelector('[data-action=save]');pb.onclick=()=>{if(!ext(sc)||!artUnlocked(sc)||!nearArt(sc,[9,32],4.25))return;if(persist(sc,strokes)){refreshWorld(sc);toast('Đã lưu tranh Community Wall vào tiến trình game.');$('lq71-editor-status').textContent='✓ Đã lưu! Tranh xuất hiện bên mặt tiền Mural Workshop.';}else toast('Chưa lưu được tranh. Hãy kiểm tra bộ nhớ trình duyệt.')};
 panel.querySelector('[data-action=undo]').onclick=()=>{strokes.pop();redraw()};
 panel.querySelector('[data-action=reset]').onclick=()=>{if(confirm('Xóa toàn bộ nét trong bản nháp?')){strokes=[];redraw()}};
 panel.querySelectorAll('[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{color=b.dataset.color;panel.querySelector('#lq71-color').value=color;});
 panel.querySelectorAll('[data-tool]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{tool=b.dataset.tool;panel.querySelectorAll('[data-tool]').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el===b));});
 panel.querySelector('[data-tool=brush]').classList.add('active');
 panel.querySelector('#lq71-color').oninput=e=>{color=e.target.value};
 panel.querySelector('#lq71-size').oninput=e=>{size=Number(e.target.value)};
 panel.querySelector('#lq71-stamp').onchange=e=>{stamp=e.target.value};
 painter={getStrokes:()=>strokes,redraw};
}

 root=document.createElement('div');root.id='lq71-root';root.hidden=true;
 root.innerHTML='<button id="lq71-open" title="F5 · Hoạt động hội họa" hidden>🎨 Hoạt động hội họa</button><div id="lq71-toast" role="status" hidden></div><div id="lq71-panel" hidden role="dialog" aria-label="Art District art activity" aria-modal="true"><section id="lq71-card"><header><h2>✦ ART DISTRICT · HỘI HỌA</h2><button data-action="close">✕</button></header><main id="lq71-body"></main></section></div>';
 document.body.appendChild(root);panel=$('lq71-panel');notice=$('lq71-toast');
 $('lq71-open').onclick=open;
 root.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const btn=e.target.closest('button');if(!btn)return;
  if(btn.dataset.action==='close'){close();return}
   if(btn.dataset.artAnswer!==undefined){const sc=current(),id=btn.dataset.artId,choice=Number(btn.dataset.artAnswer);if(finishArtTask(sc,id,choice)){close();refreshWorld(sc);updateArtPrompt(sc);}return;}
   if(btn.dataset.tab){drawTab(btn.dataset.tab);return}
  if(btn.dataset.action==='locatewall'){if(navTarget(current(),9,32))close();return}
  if(btn.dataset.action==='locateeye'){if(navTarget(current(),9,11))close();return}
  if(btn.dataset.architect!==undefined){const s=STYLES[Number(btn.dataset.architect)];if(s&&navTarget(current(),s.at))close();return}
 });
 document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(visible&&e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();close();return}
  if(visible&&(['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','w','a','s','d','e'].includes(e.key.toLowerCase())||e.key===' ')){
   if(e.target===document.body){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();}return;
  }
  if(e.key!=='F5'||e.repeat||e.defaultPrevented||e.altKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)return;
  if(['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(document.activeElement?.tagName)||document.activeElement?.isContentEditable)return;
  if(!active(current()))return;
   const sc=current(),task=nextArtTask(sc);if(!visible&&!nearArt(sc,task?.at||[9,32]))return;
   e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();visible?close():open();
 },true);
}
function refreshWorld(sc){if(!ext(sc))return;
 for(const o of objects){try{o.destroy()}catch(_){}}objects=[];ambient=null;
 displayOnWorld(sc);
}
function ambience(sc,time){if(!ambient||!ext(sc)||!sc.cameras?.main||visible)return;
 ambient.clear();const v=sc.cameras.main.worldView;
 const reduce=window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')?.matches;
 const hrs=Number(sc.store?.game?.minutes||720)%1440,night=hrs>=1070||hrs<340;
 const sites=[
  {x:9*T,y:33*T,k:'paint',c:0xff8dbb},
  {x:9*T,y:46*T,k:'music',c:0x6de9e3},
  {x:62*T,y:34*T,k:'photo',c:0xd7bcff},
  {x:83*T,y:32*T,k:'paint',c:0xffb879},
  {x:83*T,y:83*T,k:'dance',c:0xff87c9},
  {x:36*T,y:14*T,k:'gate',c:0xffcd8b}
 ];
 for(let i=0;i<sites.length;i++){
  const a=sites[i];if(a.x<v.x-150||a.x>v.right+150||a.y<v.y-150||a.y>v.bottom+150)continue;
  const phase=reduce?0:time*.002+i*1.4;let x=a.x+Math.sin(phase)*7,y=a.y-18-Math.cos(phase*.7)*6;
  if(a.k==='paint'){
   for(let j=0;j<3;j++){ambient.fillStyle([0xffd675,0xf58bb8,0x73ddcb][j],.45);ambient.fillRect(x+j*7,y-j*5,3,5)}
  }else if(a.k==='music'){
   ambient.fillStyle(a.c,.35).fillCircle(x,y,4);ambient.fillRect(x+3,y-15,2,15);ambient.fillRect(x+3,y-15,7,2);
  }else if(a.k==='photo'){
   if(reduce||Math.floor(time/900)%5===0){ambient.fillStyle(a.c,.33).fillCircle(x,y,10);ambient.fillStyle(0xffffff,.6).fillCircle(x,y,2)}
  }else if(a.k==='dance'){
   for(let k=0;k<3;k++){ambient.fillStyle(a.c,.24).fillRect(x+k*8,y+2-(k%2)*5,5,7)}
  }else if(night){
   for(let k=-3;k<=3;k++){ambient.fillStyle(k%2?0xffd18a:0x88e4d2,.08).fillCircle(a.x+k*19,a.y-68,10)}
  }
 }
 if(route?.points?.length){
  const player=sc.player,dx=route.target[0]*TILE+16-player.x,dy=route.target[1]*TILE+16-player.y;
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<56){route=null;toast('Đã tới gần điểm tham quan.');return}
  const pt=route.points;
  for(let i=0;i<pt.length;i+=2){const p=pt[i],x=(p[0]+.5)*TILE,y=(p[1]+.5)*TILE;
   if(x<v.x-20||x>v.right+20||y<v.y-20||y>v.bottom+20)continue;
   ambient.fillStyle(0x4be0dc,.55).fillCircle(x,y,4);ambient.fillStyle(0xffeec9,.38).fillCircle(x,y,1.5);
  }
 }
}
let lastTime=0;
function updateArtPrompt(sc){
 if(!root)return;const btn=$('lq71-open');if(!btn)return;
 const task=nextArtTask(sc),at=task?.at||[9,32],here=nearArt(sc,at);
 const visibleHint=here&&!visible&&!artFilmShowing();
 btn.hidden=!visibleHint;
 if(visibleHint)btn.textContent=task?'🎨 F5 · '+task.title.replace(/^\d+ · /,''):'🎨 F5 · Vẽ Community Wall';
}
function update(sc,time){
 if(!ext(sc)){if(linkedScene===sc&&objects.length)clearWorld();if(root){root.hidden=!active(sc);if(visible)close();updateArtPrompt(sc);}return}
 if(linkedScene!==sc||!objects.length){clearWorld();linkedScene=sc;displayOnWorld(sc);}
 if(root)root.hidden=false;
 if(time-lastTime>140){lastTime=time;ambience(sc,time);updateArtPrompt(sc)}
}
function attach(sc){if(sc.__lq71Attached)return;sc.__lq71Attached=true;
 sc.events.on('update',(time)=>{try{update(sc,time)}catch(e){if(!sc.__lq71Warn){console.warn('[V71 Art]',e);sc.__lq71Warn=true}}});
 sc.events.once('shutdown',()=>{clearWorld();sc.__lq71Attached=false});
}
function start(){mount();
 const refresh=()=>{const sc=current();if(sc?.events){attach(sc);if(active(sc)){root.hidden=false;updateArtPrompt(sc);if(ext(sc)&&linkedScene===sc&&imgEye.complete&&imgEye.naturalWidth&&!sc.textures.exists('lq71_eye'))refreshWorld(sc);}}
  else if(root)root.hidden=true;
 };
 setInterval(refresh,1000);imgEye.addEventListener('load',refresh,{once:true});refresh();
}
function accentFacade(g,b){ // V12.3-inspired ornament, layered ON TOP of the existing V70 authored buildings.
 if(!b||!['Color Gallery','Backbeat Warehouse','Open Canvas Gallery','Mural Workshop','Photo Lab','Independent Studio'].includes(b.name))return;
 const x=b.x*TILE,y=b.y*TILE,w=b.w*TILE,h=b.h*TILE,roof=Math.round(h*.48),fy=y+roof, mid=x+w/2;
 const R=(xx,yy,ww,hh,c,a=.8)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(xx,yy,ww,hh);
 const L=(a,b,c,d,k,alpha=.8,width=2)=>g.lineStyle(width,k,alpha).lineBetween(a,b,c,d);
 if(b.name==='Color Gallery'){
  // leaf-line glass headers, a rosette and copper ribs
  for(let k=0;k<7;k++){const xx=x+16+k*(w-32)/6;L(xx,y+21,mid,y+8,0xd6b7be,.40,2)}
  g.lineStyle(3,0xf4d699,.75).strokeCircle(mid,y+31,13);g.fillStyle(0x6ed8d0,.68).fillCircle(mid,y+31,5);
 }else if(b.name==='Backbeat Warehouse'){
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++){let xx=x+14+i*(w-28)/6;L(xx,fy+7,xx+20,fy+26,0xe29f82,.5,2);R(xx+17,fy+4,4,4,0xffd7a2,.68)}
 }else if(b.name==='Open Canvas Gallery'){
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let xx=x+25+i*66;R(xx,fy+4,4,13,0xf6dbae,.53);R(xx-4,fy+3,12,3,0xe9bf7f,.67)}
 }else if(b.name==='Mural Workshop'){
  L(x+17,fy+2,x+56,fy+36,0xe08b9e,.75,5);L(x+56,fy+2,x+17,fy+36,0x9bbed0,.63,4);
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++)R(x+10+i*21,fy+10,3,3,[0xffd26f,0x74ddd5,0xee92b8][i%3],.55);
 }else if(b.name==='Photo Lab'){
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let xx=x+23+i*63;g.fillStyle(0xbab0cb,.55).fillTriangle(xx-12,fy+19,xx,fy+2,xx+12,fy+19);R(xx-10,fy+19,20,3,0xa794c9,.45)}
 }else if(b.name==='Independent Studio'){
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let xx=x+25+i*60;g.fillStyle(0xecc598,.55).fillCircle(xx,fy+9,4);L(xx-9,fy+12,xx+8,fy+16,0xc5aaae,.4,2)}
 }
}
window.LQArtV71=Object.freeze({version:'72.0-painters-quarter',open,close,styles:STYLES,accentFacade,activities:ART_TASKS,progress:artProgress,unlocked:artUnlocked,
 diagnostics:()=>{const sc=current();return {map:sc?.area?.id,ready:!!(sc?.player),muralStrokes:state(sc).strokes.length,artActivities:artProgress(sc).done.length,paintingUnlocked:artUnlocked(sc),wallObjects:objects.length,eyeLoaded:!!imgEye.naturalWidth,buildings:sc?.area?.buildings?.length||0,routeLength:route?.points?.length||0}}});
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
})();

<script>
(function(){
 const AREA='art-district', TILE=32;
 let installed=null, crowd=[], props=[], lastTick=0;
 const ROLES={watch:'watch',dance:'dance',paint:'paint',perform:'perform',stroll:'stroll'};
 const SITES=[
  {type:'boombox',at:[12,46],crowd:[
   {p:[10,44],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[11,44],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[13,44],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[14,44],r:ROLES.watch},
   {p:[9,46],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[15,46],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[10,48],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[14,48],r:ROLES.watch},
   {p:[12,47],r:ROLES.perform},{p:[11,47],r:ROLES.perform},{p:[13,47],r:ROLES.perform}
  ]},
  {type:'mural',at:[9,32],crowd:[
   {p:[7,31],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[7,33],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[7,35],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[11,31],r:ROLES.paint},
   {p:[12,33],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[13,31],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[13,34],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[12,36],r:ROLES.watch}
  ]},
  {type:'pleinair',at:[83,32],crowd:[
   {p:[80,31],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[81,33],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[84,31],r:ROLES.paint},{p:[86,32],r:ROLES.watch},
   {p:[88,31],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[87,34],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[84,35],r:ROLES.watch}
  ]},
  {type:'dance',at:[83,83],crowd:[
   {p:[82,82],r:ROLES.dance},{p:[84,82],r:ROLES.dance},{p:[83,84],r:ROLES.dance},{p:[85,84],r:ROLES.dance},
   {p:[79,81],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[80,84],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[81,86],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[86,80],r:ROLES.watch},
   {p:[87,83],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[88,85],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[83,79],r:ROLES.perform}
  ]},
  {type:'plaza',at:[36,23],crowd:[
   {p:[33,22],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[34,24],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[36,22],r:ROLES.perform},{p:[38,24],r:ROLES.watch},
   {p:[39,22],r:ROLES.watch},{p:[35,26],r:ROLES.stroll},{p:[37,26],r:ROLES.stroll},{p:[40,24],r:ROLES.watch}
  ]}
 ];
 function scene(){ try{return window.__lifequestGetScene?.()||null;}catch(_){return null;} }
 function active(sc){ return !!(sc&&sc.area&&sc.area.id===AREA&&sc.player&&sc.add&&sc.events); }
 function offscreen(sc,sp,pad=120){ const v=sc?.cameras?.main?.worldView; return !!(v&&(sp.x<v.x-pad||sp.y<v.y-pad||sp.x>v.right+pad||sp.y>v.bottom+pad)); }
 function uniqueTextures(sc){
  const keys=[]; const seen=new Set();
  for(const n of sc.npcs||[]){ const key=n?.sprite?.texture?.key; if(key&&key!=='__MISSING'&&!seen.has(key)){ seen.add(key); keys.push({key,scale:Math.abs(n.sprite.scaleX||1)}); } }
  return keys;
 }
 function drawProps(sc){
  // Ground props and crowd focal points.
  for(const site of SITES){
   const g=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(6.95); props.push(g);
   const px=site.at[0]*TILE, py=site.at[1]*TILE;
   if(site.type==='boombox'){
    g.fillStyle(0x1b2735,.92).fillRoundedRect(px+8,py+10,48,20,4);
    g.fillStyle(0x2dd0cf,.85).fillRect(px+12,py+14,8,8); g.fillStyle(0xf490b7,.85).fillRect(px+44,py+14,8,8);
    g.fillStyle(0x5f6f80,.9).fillCircle(px+24,py+20,7); g.fillStyle(0x5f6f80,.9).fillCircle(px+40,py+20,7);
    g.lineStyle(2,0xffd38d,.55).strokeCircle(px+32,py+20,24);
    g.lineStyle(1,0x6ce3dd,.4).strokeCircle(px+32,py+20,34);
   } else if(site.type==='mural'){
    g.fillStyle(0x394250,.92).fillRect(px-14,py-8,94,58);
    g.fillStyle(0xd5c1a3,.96).fillRect(px-10,py-4,86,50);
    g.lineStyle(3,0xf0c97f,.82).strokeRect(px-10,py-4,86,50);
    const marks=[[8,10,0xffd66f],[20,20,0x6ce3dd],[34,12,0xf58ab8],[48,24,0xa7a0ff],[64,16,0xffb979],[72,32,0x71dccd],[54,38,0xff84c1]];
    for(const [mx,my,c] of marks){ g.fillStyle(c,.9).fillCircle(px-6+mx,py+my,3.5); g.fillStyle(c,.44).fillRect(px-8+mx,py+my+5,6,2); }
    // easels + paint cans
    for(const ex of [px-26,px+90]){ g.lineStyle(2,0xb8885a,.85).lineBetween(ex,py+42,ex+8,py+8).lineBetween(ex+16,py+42,ex+8,py+8).lineBetween(ex+4,py+28,ex+12,py+28); g.fillStyle(0xf0ece1,.95).fillRect(ex+2,py+12,12,14); }
    for(const [dx,c] of [[-18,0xf58ab8],[-10,0x6ce3dd],[-2,0xffd66f]]) g.fillStyle(c,.9).fillRect(px+dx,py+48,5,5);
   } else if(site.type==='pleinair'){
    for(const ox of [0,18,36]){ const ex=px+ox; g.lineStyle(2,0xb98a5d,.8).lineBetween(ex,py+34,ex+8,py+2).lineBetween(ex+16,py+34,ex+8,py+2).lineBetween(ex+4,py+22,ex+12,py+22); g.fillStyle([0xf7d685,0x74ded7,0xf59bc1][(ox/18)%3],.8).fillRect(ex+2,py+8,12,12); }
    g.fillStyle(0x4e5968,.85).fillRoundedRect(px+56,py+8,20,28,4); g.lineStyle(2,0xffd7a2,.48).strokeRect(px+58,py+10,16,24);
   } else if(site.type==='dance'){
    g.lineStyle(3,0xf8ce84,.50).strokeCircle(px+16,py+16,30); g.lineStyle(1,0x72e1da,.35).strokeCircle(px+16,py+16,42);
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){ const a=i*Math.PI/5; g.fillStyle([0xffd66f,0x76e1d9,0xf58ab8][i%3],.75).fillCircle(px+16+Math.cos(a)*30,py+16+Math.sin(a)*30,3); }
    g.fillStyle(0x1b2735,.92).fillRect(px-8,py-14,18,12); g.fillStyle(0x6ce3dd,.9).fillRect(px-5,py-11,4,4); g.fillStyle(0xf58ab8,.9).fillRect(px+3,py-11,4,4);
   } else if(site.type==='plaza'){
    g.lineStyle(2,0xffd7a1,.45).strokeCircle(px+16,py+16,26);
    g.fillStyle(0x243447,.9).fillRoundedRect(px+4,py+6,18,22,4); g.fillStyle(0xffca84,.85).fillRect(px+10,py+2,6,6);
   }
  }
 }
 function spawnCrowd(sc){
  const pool=uniqueTextures(sc); if(!pool.length) return;
  const taken=new Set();
  const valid=(x,y)=>{
    if(x<2||y<2||x>=sc.area.w-2||y>=sc.area.h-2||sc.area.isTileBlocked?.(x,y)||sc.area.warpAt?.(x,y)||taken.has(x+','+y))return false;
    // Keep the main central parade lane and building doors visually accessible.
    if(x>=34&&x<=37&&y>=10&&y<=52)return false;
    if(sc.area.buildings.some(b=>Math.abs(b.door-x)<=1 && Math.abs(b.y+b.h-y)<=1))return false;
    return true;
  };
  const nearest=(x,y)=>{
    if(valid(x,y))return [x,y];
    for(let r=1;r<=5;r++)for(let dy=-r;dy<=r;dy++)for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++){
      if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dy))!==r)continue;
      if(valid(x+dx,y+dy))return [x+dx,y+dy];
    }
    return null;
  };
  let idx=0;
  for(const site of SITES){
   for(const entry of site.crowd){
    const p=nearest(entry.p[0],entry.p[1]), role=entry.r;
    if(!p)continue;
    taken.add(p[0]+','+p[1]);
    const tex=pool[idx%pool.length]; idx++;
    const sp=sc.add.sprite((p[0]+.5)*TILE,(p[1]+.5)*TILE,tex.key).setDepth(7.35).setScale(tex.scale||1);
    sp.setVisible(true);
    crowd.push({sprite:sp,home:p,role,seed:idx,baseY:(p[1]+.5)*TILE,baseX:(p[0]+.5)*TILE,focus:site.at});
   }
  }
 }
 function clearAll(){ for(const a of crowd) try{a.sprite.destroy()}catch(_){} crowd=[]; for(const p of props) try{p.destroy()}catch(_){} props=[]; installed=null; }
 function install(sc){ clearAll(); installed=sc; drawProps(sc); spawnCrowd(sc); sc.events.once('shutdown',clearAll); }
 function animateActor(a,time){
   const sp=a.sprite; const t=time*0.001 + a.seed*0.37;
   let x=a.baseX, y=a.baseY, flip=false;
   if(a.role===ROLES.watch){ x+=Math.sin(t*.8)*2.2; y+=Math.cos(t*1.1)*1.4; flip=Math.sin(t) < 0; }
   else if(a.role===ROLES.paint){ x+=Math.sin(t*.4)*1.2; y+=Math.cos(t*.6)*.9; flip=true; }
   else if(a.role===ROLES.perform){ x+=Math.sin(t*1.7)*4.5; y+=Math.cos(t*1.5)*2.8; flip=Math.sin(t*1.7)<0; }
   else if(a.role===ROLES.dance){ x+=Math.sin(t*2.2)*7.5; y+=Math.cos(t*1.8)*4.2; flip=Math.sin(t*2.2)<0; }
   else { x+=Math.sin(t*.9)*5; y+=Math.cos(t*.7)*3; flip=Math.cos(t)<0; }
   sp.x=x; sp.y=y; sp.setFlipX(flip); sp.setVisible(!offscreen(installed,sp));
 }
 function update(time){
   const sc=scene();
   if(!active(sc)){ if(installed) clearAll(); return; }
   if(installed!==sc||!crowd.length) install(sc);
   if(sc.inside){ for(const a of crowd) a.sprite.setVisible(false); for(const p of props) p.setVisible(false); return; }
   for(const p of props) p.setVisible(true);
   if(time-lastTick<90) return; lastTick=time;
   for(const a of crowd) animateActor(a,time);
  }
 function boot(){ setInterval(()=>{ try{ update(performance.now()); }catch(e){ if(!window.__lq73Warn){ console.warn('[LQ V73 Crowd]',e); window.__lq73Warn=true; } } },110); }
 if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true}); else boot();
 window.LQArtV73={version:'74.0-crowd-art-district-fixed',diagnostics:()=>({count:crowd.length,sites:SITES.length,artists:crowd.filter(c=>c.role==='paint').length,dancers:crowd.filter(c=>c.role==='dance').length,watchers:crowd.filter(c=>c.role==='watch').length})};
})();
</script>

<script>
/* LIFEQUEST AI V77 — BEYOND CONVERSATION (Phase 1)
   Adds: NPC mood chips (AI-reported), Conversation Replay + phrase notebook,
   Cultural Passport (stamps earned from real world state), The LifeQuest Herald
   (newspaper compiled from recorded events). Reads existing saves; never changes
   quests, money, unlocks or NPC memory. All data lives under store.game.v77. */
(() => {
'use strict';
const VERSION = '77.0-beyond-conversation';
const getScene = () => (typeof window.__lifequestGetScene === 'function' ? window.__lifequestGetScene() : null);
const valid = sc => !!(sc && sc.store && sc.store.game && sc.area);
const services = () => window.__lifequestServices || null;
const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const MOOD_ICON = {"neutral":"◆","happy":"✦","sad":"◒","confused":"?","amused":"✧","curious":"?","disagreeing":"◇","worried":"!","formal":"♜","calm":"◈","friendly":"✦","surprised":"!","angry":"!","shy":"◒","embarrassed":"◒","thinking":"…","doubtful":"?","relieved":"◈","excited":"✧","apologetic":"◒","encouraging":"✦","impressed":"✧","disappointed":"◒","proud":"♜","focused":"◆","urgent":"!","nostalgic":"◈","grateful":"✦","frustrated":"!","determined":"◆","suspicious":"?","playful":"✧","confident":"◆","contemptuous":"◇","tired":"…","inspired":"✧","patient":"◈","solemn":"♜","alert":"!","persuasive":"✦"};
const MOODS = new Set(Object.keys(MOOD_ICON));

function data(sc = getScene()) {
  if (!valid(sc)) return null;
  const g = sc.store.game;
  if (!g.v77 || typeof g.v77 !== 'object' || Array.isArray(g.v77)) g.v77 = { schema: 1 };
  const d = g.v77;
  d.schema = 1;
  if (!d.passport || typeof d.passport !== 'object') d.passport = {};
  if (!Array.isArray(d.favorites)) d.favorites = [];
  if (!d.names || typeof d.names !== 'object') d.names = {};
  if (!d.helped || typeof d.helped !== 'object') d.helped = {};
  if (!d.news || typeof d.news !== 'object') d.news = { lastReadAt: 0 };
  return d;
}
function persist(sc = getScene()) { try { sc?.store?.save?.(); } catch {} }
function level() { try { return services()?.cefr?.getLevel?.() || 'B1'; } catch { return 'B1'; } }
function speak(text) {
  const s = services()?.speech;
  if (s && typeof s.speak === 'function') { s.speak(String(text)); return; }
  try {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(String(text));
    u.lang = 'en-US'; u.rate = 0.95;
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
  } catch {}
}
function timeLabel(minutes) {
  const m = Math.max(0, Math.min(1439, Number(minutes) || 0));
  return String(Math.floor(m / 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(m % 60).padStart(2, '0');
}
function affinityLabel(a) { return a < 0 ? 'Unfriendly' : a < 10 ? 'Stranger' : a < 30 ? 'Acquaintance' : a < 60 ? 'Friend' : 'Close friend'; }

/* ---------- names: learn NPC display names from the live scene ---------- */
function refreshNames(sc = getScene()) {
  const d = data(sc); if (!d) return;
  let changed = false;
  const add = (id, name) => { if (id && name && d.names[id] !== name) { d.names[id] = name; changed = true; } };
  try {
    for (const n of sc.npcs || []) add(n?.def?.id || n?.id, n?.def?.name || n?.name);
    const c = sc.chat?.npc; if (c) add(c.id, c.name);
  } catch {}
  if (changed) persist(sc);
}
const npcName = (d, id) => d?.names?.[id] || String(id || 'someone').replace(/[-_]/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());

/* ---------- helped: remember, per region, that the player fulfilled a resident's wish ----------
   The base game marks a resident's wish done in store.mem[id].goalDone, but it does not tie that
   to a region in the V60 world log. While the player is in a region we observe its residents and
   record the evidence under v77.helped[regionId], so the passport stamp survives leaving/reloading. */
function refreshHelped(sc = getScene()) {
  const d = data(sc); if (!d) return;
  const area = sc.area?.id; if (!area) return;
  if (d.helped[area]) return;
  let done = null;
  try {
    for (const n of sc.npcs || []) {
      const id = n?.def?.id || n?.id;
      if (id && sc.store.mem?.[id]?.goalDone) { done = id; break; }
    }
    const c = sc.chat?.npc;
    if (!done && c?.id && sc.store.mem?.[c.id]?.goalDone) done = c.id;
  } catch {}
  if (done) {
    d.helped[area] = { npc: done, day: sc.store.game.day || 1, at: Date.now() };
    persist(sc);
  }
}
