const toastEl = document.createElement('div');
toastEl.id = 'lq77-toast';
document.body.appendChild(toastEl);
let toastTimer = 0;
function toast(msg) {
  toastEl.textContent = msg;
  toastEl.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => toastEl.classList.remove('show'), 4200);
}

const panels = {};
function makePanel(id, paper) {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.id = id;
  el.className = 'lq77-panel' + (paper ? ' lq77-paper' : '');
  el.style.display = 'none';
  el.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  panels[id] = el;
  return el;
}
function closePanels() { for (const el of Object.values(panels)) el.style.display = 'none'; }
function anyPanelOpen() { return Object.values(panels).some(el => el.style.display === 'block'); }
function baseOverlayOpen() {
  return ['chat', 'settings', 'briefing', 'helper', 'journal', 'quests'].some(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    return el && el.style.display === 'block';
  });
}
function openPanel(el) { closePanels(); el.style.display = 'block'; }
function closeButton(el, extra) {
  const b = document.createElement('button');
  b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'lq77-close'; b.textContent = '✕ Close';
  b.onclick = () => { el.style.display = 'none'; extra?.(); };
  el.appendChild(b);
  return b;
}
function openHelperWith(word) {
  const helper = document.getElementById('helper');
  const input = helper?.querySelector('input');
  const ask = document.getElementById('helper-ask');
  if (!helper || !input || !ask) { toast('Word helper is not ready yet.'); return; }
  closePanels();
  input.value = word;
  helper.style.display = 'block';
  ask.click();
}
function wordSpans(container, text) {
  const parts = String(text).split(/(\s+)/);
  for (const p of parts) {
    if (/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'-]{1,}$/.test(p)) {
      const s = document.createElement('span');
      s.className = 'lq77-word'; s.textContent = p; s.title = 'Look up "' + p + '" in the Word helper';
      s.onclick = () => openHelperWith(p.replace(/^['-]+|['-]+$/g, ''));
      container.appendChild(s);
    } else container.appendChild(document.createTextNode(p));
  }
}
function moodChip(mood) {
  if (!mood || !MOODS.has(mood) || mood === 'neutral') return null;
  const chip = document.createElement('span');
  chip.className = 'lq77-mood lq77-mood-' + mood;
  chip.textContent = (MOOD_ICON[mood] || '') + ' ' + mood;
  return chip;
}

/* ================= Conversation Replay + Notebook ================= */
const replayEl = makePanel('lq77-replay');
let replayNpc = '', replayTab = 'talk';
function openReplay(npcId) {
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  if (!d) { toast('Enter the town first, then open the replay.'); return; }
  refreshNames(sc);
  if (npcId) replayNpc = npcId;
  renderReplay();
  openPanel(replayEl);
}
function renderReplay() {
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  replayEl.innerHTML = '';
  closeButton(replayEl);
  const h = document.createElement('h2'); h.textContent = '🕮 Conversation Replay'; replayEl.appendChild(h);
  const sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'lq77-sub';
  sub.textContent = 'Re-read past talks, listen again, click any word to look it up, ★ save a line to your notebook.';
  replayEl.appendChild(sub);
  if (!d) { replayEl.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement('div'), { className: 'lq77-empty', textContent: 'The town is still loading.' })); return; }

  const tabs = document.createElement('div'); tabs.className = 'lq77-tabs';
  for (const [key, label] of [['talk', 'Past talks'], ['book', '★ Notebook (' + d.favorites.length + ')']]) {
    const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button';
    b.className = 'lq77-tab' + (replayTab === key ? ' on' : ''); b.textContent = label;
    b.onclick = () => { replayTab = key; renderReplay(); };
    tabs.appendChild(b);
  }
  replayEl.appendChild(tabs);

  if (replayTab === 'book') { renderFavorites(d); return; }

  const mem = sc.store.all().filter(([, m]) => Array.isArray(m.transcript) && m.transcript.length > 0);
  if (!mem.length) {
    replayEl.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement('div'), { className: 'lq77-empty', textContent: 'No conversations yet. Talk to a villager (E) and your history will appear here.' }));
    return;
  }
  if (!replayNpc || !mem.some(([id]) => id === replayNpc)) replayNpc = mem[mem.length - 1][0];
  const sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'lq77-select';
  for (const [id, m] of mem) {
    const o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = id; o.textContent = npcName(d, id) + ' — ' + m.transcript.length + ' messages · ' + affinityLabel(m.affinity || 0);
    if (id === replayNpc) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
  sel.onchange = () => { replayNpc = sel.value; renderReplay(); };
  replayEl.appendChild(sel);

  const m = sc.store.get(replayNpc);
  const list = document.createElement('div');
  for (const t of m.transcript) {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'lq77-row' + (t.role === 'user' ? ' me' : '');
    const who = document.createElement('div'); who.className = 'who';
    who.textContent = (t.role === 'user' ? 'You' : npcName(d, replayNpc).split(' (')[0]) + '\nDay ' + (t.day ?? '?') + ' ' + timeLabel(t.minutes);
    who.style.whiteSpace = 'pre-line';
    const txt = document.createElement('div'); txt.className = 'txt';
    const chip = t.role !== 'user' ? moodChip(t.mood) : null;
    if (chip) { txt.appendChild(chip); txt.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' ')); }
    wordSpans(txt, t.text || '');
    row.append(who, txt);
    const ops = document.createElement('div'); ops.className = 'ops';
    if (t.role !== 'user') {
      const spk = document.createElement('button'); spk.type = 'button'; spk.className = 'lq77-mini'; spk.textContent = '🔊'; spk.title = 'Listen again';
      spk.onclick = () => speak(t.text);
      ops.appendChild(spk);
      const fav = document.createElement('button'); fav.type = 'button'; fav.className = 'lq77-mini'; fav.textContent = '★'; fav.title = 'Save this line to your notebook';
      fav.onclick = () => {
        const d2 = data(); if (!d2) return;
        if (d2.favorites.some(f => f.npc === replayNpc && f.text === t.text)) { toast('Already in your notebook.'); return; }
        d2.favorites.push({ npc: replayNpc, text: t.text, day: t.day ?? sc.store.game.day, at: Date.now() });
        if (d2.favorites.length > 60) d2.favorites.shift();
        persist(); toast('★ Saved to your notebook.'); renderReplay();
      };
      ops.appendChild(fav);
    }
    row.appendChild(ops);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  replayEl.appendChild(list);
  const note = document.createElement('div'); note.className = 'lq77-sub';
  note.textContent = 'The town remembers the most recent 80 messages per person. Older talks are kept as a short memory summary in your Journal (J).';
  replayEl.appendChild(note);
}
function renderFavorites(d) {
  if (!d.favorites.length) {
    replayEl.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement('div'), { className: 'lq77-empty', textContent: 'Your notebook is empty. In "Past talks", press ★ on a line you love.' }));
    return;
  }
  for (const f of [...d.favorites].reverse()) {
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'lq77-row';
    const who = document.createElement('div'); who.className = 'who';
    who.textContent = npcName(d, f.npc).split(' (')[0] + '\nDay ' + f.day;
    who.style.whiteSpace = 'pre-line';
    const txt = document.createElement('div'); txt.className = 'txt';
    wordSpans(txt, f.text);
    const ops = document.createElement('div'); ops.className = 'ops';
    const spk = document.createElement('button'); spk.type = 'button'; spk.className = 'lq77-mini'; spk.textContent = '🔊';
    spk.onclick = () => speak(f.text);
    const del = document.createElement('button'); del.type = 'button'; del.className = 'lq77-mini'; del.textContent = '🗑'; del.title = 'Remove from notebook';
    del.onclick = () => { const d2 = data(); if (!d2) return; d2.favorites = d2.favorites.filter(x => x !== f && !(x.npc === f.npc && x.text === f.text && x.at === f.at)); persist(); renderReplay(); };
    ops.append(spk, del);
    row.append(who, txt, ops);
    replayEl.appendChild(row);
  }
}

/* ================= Cultural Passport ================= */
const passportEl = makePanel('lq77-passport');
const CORE_META = {
  'english-town': { name: 'English Town', icon: '🏘️', role: 'Home Town', level: 'A1–B1', building: 'Town Square', tone: '#ffd98a' },
  'mining-town': { name: 'Mining Town', icon: '⛏️', role: 'The Echoes Beneath', level: 'A2–B1', building: 'Crystal Cavern', tone: '#b9a7f0' },
};
const REGION_ICON = { 'art-district': '🎨', 'tech-co': '💡', 'old-oriaris': '👑', 'wish-valley': '🌿', 'vocalt-greedy-land': '⚖️' };
function passportRegions() {
  const out = [];
  const maps = window.LQWorldV60?.MAPS || {};
  const list = window.LQWorldMaps?.list?.() || [];
  const ids = new Set([...Object.keys(CORE_META), ...Object.keys(maps)]);
  for (const id of ids) {
    const area = list.find(a => a.id === id);
    if (!area && !CORE_META[id] && !maps[id]) continue;
    const m = maps[id] || {};
    out.push({
      id,
      name: CORE_META[id]?.name || m.name || area?.name || id,
      icon: CORE_META[id]?.icon || REGION_ICON[id] || '📍',
      role: CORE_META[id]?.role || m.role || 'Region',
      level: CORE_META[id]?.level || m.level || '',
      building: CORE_META[id]?.building || m.building || '',
      tone: CORE_META[id]?.tone || (typeof m.tone === 'number' ? '#' + m.tone.toString(16).padStart(6, '0') : '#c69a54'),
    });
  }
  return out;
}
function stampEvidence(sc, id) {
  const g = sc.store.game, v60 = g.v60;
  if (!v60) return null;
  const reg = v60.regions?.[id];
  const evs = (v60.events || []).filter(e => e.area === id);
  const first = list => list.length ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
  const quest = first(evs.filter(e => e.kind === 'quest' || e.kind === 'world-change'));
  if (reg?.complete || (reg?.progress || 0) > 0 || quest) return { reason: 'Completed a local mission', day: quest?.day || g.day };
  const echo = first(evs.filter(e => e.kind === 'echo-complete'));
  if (echo) return { reason: 'Restored a Time Echo memory', day: echo.day || g.day };
  const fest = first(evs.filter(e => e.kind === 'pulse' && /bắt đầu/.test(e.title || '')));
  if (fest || reg?.festival) return { reason: 'Joined a local festival', day: fest?.day || g.day };
  const helpedHere = g.v77?.helped?.[id];
  if (helpedHere) return { reason: 'Helped a resident with their wish', day: helpedHere.day || g.day };
  const helped = Object.keys(v60.people || {}).some(k => k.startsWith(id + '::') && sc.store.mem?.[k.split('::')[1]]?.goalDone);
  if (helped) return { reason: 'Helped a resident with their wish', day: g.day };
  const art = first(evs.filter(e => e.kind === 'companion' || e.kind === 'echo'));
  if (art) return { reason: 'Discovered a piece of local history', day: art.day || g.day };
  return null;
}
function checkStamps(quiet) {
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  if (!d) return;
  let earned = null;
  for (const r of passportRegions()) {
    if (d.passport[r.id]) continue;
    const ev = stampEvidence(sc, r.id);
    if (ev) {
      d.passport[r.id] = { day: ev.day, reason: ev.reason, at: Date.now() };
      earned = r;
    }
  }
  if (earned) {
    persist(sc);
    if (!quiet) toast('📔 Passport stamped: ' + earned.name + '!');
    if (passportEl.style.display === 'block') renderPassport();
  }
}
function openPassport() {
  const d = data();
  if (!d) { toast('Enter the town first, then open your passport.'); return; }
  refreshHelped();
  checkStamps(true);
  renderPassport();
  openPanel(passportEl);
}
function renderPassport() {
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  passportEl.innerHTML = '';
  closeButton(passportEl);
  const h = document.createElement('h2'); h.textContent = '📔 Cultural Passport'; passportEl.appendChild(h);
  const regions = passportRegions();
  const count = regions.filter(r => d?.passport?.[r.id]).length;
  const sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'lq77-sub';
  sub.textContent = count + ' / ' + regions.length + ' stamps collected. A stamp is earned by living the region\'s story — not by walking in.';
  passportEl.appendChild(sub);
  if (!d) return;
  for (const r of regions) {
    const got = d.passport[r.id];
    const page = document.createElement('div');
    page.className = 'lq77-page' + (got ? '' : ' locked');
    const stamp = document.createElement('div'); stamp.className = 'lq77-stamp';
    stamp.style.borderColor = r.tone;
    stamp.innerHTML = '<span class="ic">' + esc(r.icon) + '</span><span>' + esc(got ? 'STAMPED' : 'SEALED') + '</span>';
    const info = document.createElement('div'); info.className = 'info';
    const b = document.createElement('b'); b.textContent = r.name; info.appendChild(b);
    const meta = document.createElement('div'); meta.className = 'meta';
    meta.textContent = r.role + (r.level ? ' · English ' + r.level : '') + (r.building ? ' · Landmark: ' + r.building : '');
    info.appendChild(meta);
    if (got) {
      const e = document.createElement('div'); e.className = 'lq77-earned';
      e.textContent = '✔ ' + got.reason + ' — Day ' + got.day;
      info.appendChild(e);
    } else {
      const hint = document.createElement('div'); hint.className = 'lq77-hint';
      hint.textContent = 'To earn this stamp: complete a local mission, help a resident, join a festival, or restore a Time Echo here.';
      info.appendChild(hint);
    }
    page.append(stamp, info);
    passportEl.appendChild(page);
  }
}

/* ================= The LifeQuest Herald (world newspaper) ================= */
const newsEl = makePanel('lq77-news', true);
function regionName(id) {
  const maps = window.LQWorldV60?.MAPS || {};
  return CORE_META[id]?.name || maps[id]?.name || String(id || 'the town').replace(/[-_]/g, ' ');
}
function compileNews(sc) {
  const g = sc.store.game, d = data(sc);
  const v60 = g.v60 || { events: [], regions: {} };
  const evs = v60.events || [];
  const simple = /^A/.test(level());
  const day = g.day || 1;
  const arts = [];
  const recent = evs.filter(e => day - (e.day || 1) <= 3);
  const big = recent.find(e => ['quest', 'world-change', 'echo-complete', 'region-restored'].includes(e.kind));
  if (big) {
    const where = regionName(big.area);
    const what = big.kind === 'echo-complete' ? 'an old memory was pieced together'
      : big.kind === 'region-restored' || big.kind === 'world-change' ? 'the area changed for the better'
      : 'a local quest was completed';
    arts.push({
      title: simple ? 'Good news in ' + where : 'Progress in ' + where,
      body: simple
        ? 'A visitor helped in ' + where + '. Yesterday, ' + what + '. People are happy.'
        : 'Word from ' + where + ': ' + what + ', thanks to the efforts of our visiting friend. Residents say the town feels a little brighter.',
    });
  } else {
    arts.push({
      title: simple ? 'A quiet day' : 'A calm day across the regions',
      body: simple
        ? 'It is day ' + day + '. The towns are quiet. People work and talk. You can visit a new place today.'
        : 'Day ' + day + ' passes peacefully. Shops are open, neighbours chat, and travellers are welcome. Perhaps today is a good day to explore somewhere new.',
    });
  }
  const festRegions = Object.entries(v60.regions || {}).filter(([, r]) => r?.festival).map(([id]) => regionName(id));
  if (festRegions.length) {
    arts.push({
      title: simple ? 'Festival time!' : 'Festivities under way',
      body: simple
        ? 'There is a festival in ' + festRegions.join(' and ') + '. Go and join the fun. Talk to people there.'
        : 'Celebrations continue in ' + festRegions.join(' and ') + '. Visitors are invited to join the activities and meet the locals — a fine chance to practise English in a lively crowd.',
    });
  }
  const friends = sc.store.all().filter(([, m]) => (m.affinity || 0) >= 30).sort((a, b) => b[1].affinity - a[1].affinity).slice(0, 3);
  if (friends.length) {
    const names = friends.map(([id, m]) => npcName(d, id).split(' (')[0] + ' (' + affinityLabel(m.affinity).toLowerCase() + ')');
    arts.push({
      title: simple ? 'Our visitor makes friends' : 'The visitor builds friendships',
      body: simple
        ? 'People like the visitor. ' + names.join(', ') + ' are friends now. Say hello to them!'
        : 'This paper can report that ' + names.join(', ') + ' now count the visitor among their friends. Regular, polite conversation — not gifts — earned this trust.',
    });
  }
  const stats = g.stats || {};
  const bookN = Array.isArray(stats.book) ? stats.book.length : 0;
  arts.push({
    title: simple ? 'English corner' : 'From the learning desk',
    body: simple
      ? 'The visitor sent ' + (stats.messages || 0) + ' English messages. ' + bookN + ' words are in the word book. Keep going!'
      : 'Our correspondent notes ' + (stats.messages || 0) + ' English messages exchanged, ' + (stats.corrections || 0) + ' gentle corrections received, and ' + bookN + ' words collected in the personal word book. Small steps, every day.',
  });
  const learned = Object.keys(v60.learning?.words || {}).length;
  if (learned > 0) {
    arts.push({
      title: simple ? 'Words in the wild' : 'Vocabulary spotted in the wild',
      body: simple
        ? 'The visitor used ' + learned + ' different words in regional activities. Well done!'
        : 'Regional activities have recorded ' + learned + ' distinct words used by the visitor in real tasks — proof that English here is a tool, not a test.',
    });
  }
  return { day, arts };
}
function openNews() {
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  if (!d) { toast('Enter the town first, then read the Herald.'); return; }
  refreshNames(sc);
  renderNews();
  d.news.lastReadAt = Date.now();
  persist(sc);
  openPanel(newsEl);
  updateNewsBadge();
}
function renderNews() {
  const sc = getScene();
  newsEl.innerHTML = '';
  closeButton(newsEl);
  const h = document.createElement('h2'); h.textContent = '📰 THE LIFEQUEST HERALD'; newsEl.appendChild(h);
  if (!valid(sc)) { newsEl.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement('div'), { className: 'lq77-empty', textContent: 'The town is still loading.' })); return; }
  const { day, arts } = compileNews(sc);
  const sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'lq77-sub';
  sub.textContent = 'Day ' + day + ' · ' + regionName(sc.area.id) + ' edition · Level ' + level();
  newsEl.appendChild(sub);
  for (const a of arts) {
    const art = document.createElement('div'); art.className = 'lq77-article';
    const t = document.createElement('h3'); t.textContent = a.title; art.appendChild(t);
    const p = document.createElement('p'); wordSpans(p, a.body); art.appendChild(p);
    newsEl.appendChild(art);
  }
  const note = document.createElement('div'); note.className = 'lq77-note';
  note.textContent = 'Every story is compiled from your saved world state (events, friendships, learning). Nothing is invented. Click any word to look it up.';
  newsEl.appendChild(note);
}

/* ================= Menu, keys, chat button, timers ================= */
let newsBtn = null;
function updateNewsBadge() {
  if (!newsBtn) return;
  const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
  const v60 = sc?.store?.game?.v60;
  const fresh = d && (v60?.events || []).some(e => (e.at || 0) > (d.news.lastReadAt || 0) && ['quest', 'world-change', 'echo-complete', 'festival', 'region-restored'].includes(e.kind));
  newsBtn.querySelector('span').textContent = (fresh ? '📰 Herald •' : '📰 Herald');
}
function installMenu() {
  // V11 "one-nav shell" hides the legacy #group-menu (display:none!important), so the
  // discoverable home for V77 features is the active hub nav #l11-nav. These buttons use
  // data-lq77-nav (not data-l11-page) so the shell's own page router ignores them.
  const nav = document.getElementById('l11-nav');
  if (nav && !nav.querySelector('[data-lq77-nav]')) {
    const closeShell = () => {
      document.getElementById('l11-close')?.click();
      const sh = document.getElementById('l11-shell');
      if (sh) sh.hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('l11-menu-btn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    };
    const mk = (label, kbd, fn) => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button'; b.setAttribute('data-lq77-nav', '1');
      b.title = label + ' (shortcut: ' + kbd + ')';
      const s = document.createElement('span'); s.textContent = label;
      const k = document.createElement('kbd'); k.textContent = kbd;
      k.style.cssText = 'margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;opacity:.7;border:1px solid #6f87b866;border-radius:5px;padding:1px 6px';
      b.append(s, k);
      b.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); closeShell(); fn(); });
      nav.appendChild(b);
      return b;
    };
    mk('📔 Passport', 'P', openPassport);
    newsBtn = mk('📰 Herald', 'T', openNews);
    mk('🕮 Replay talks', 'L', () => openReplay(getScene()?.chat?.npc?.id || ''));
  }
  const hint = document.querySelector('#chat .hint');
  if (hint && !hint.querySelector('[data-lq77-replay]')) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.id = 'lq77-chat-replay'; b.setAttribute('data-lq77-replay', '1');
    b.title = 'Re-read your past conversations with this person';
    b.textContent = 'Journal';
    b.onclick = () => openReplay(getScene()?.chat?.npc?.id || '');
    hint.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
    hint.appendChild(b);
  }
}
window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.defaultPrevented || e.repeat) return;
  const t = e.target;
  if (t && (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.isContentEditable)) return;
  if (anyPanelOpen()) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { closePanels(); e.preventDefault(); }
    return;
  }
  if (baseOverlayOpen()) return;
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  if (k === 'p') { e.preventDefault(); openPassport(); }
  else if (k === 't') { e.preventDefault(); openNews(); }
  else if (k === 'l') { e.preventDefault(); openReplay(); }
});
function tick() {
  const sc = getScene();
  if (!valid(sc)) return;
  refreshNames(sc);
  refreshHelped(sc);
  checkStamps(false);
  updateNewsBadge();
}
function init() {
  installMenu();
  setInterval(tick, 12000);
  setTimeout(tick, 4000);
  const w = window.LQWorldV60;
  if (w?.subscribe) {
    for (const kind of ['festival', 'echo-complete', 'region-restored']) {
      try { w.subscribe(kind, () => setTimeout(() => checkStamps(false), 300)); } catch {}
    }
  }
}
if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init, { once: true });
else init();

window.LQBeyondV77 = Object.freeze({
  version: VERSION,
  openReplay, openPassport, openNews,
  data: () => data(),
  diagnostics: () => {
    const sc = getScene(); const d = data(sc);
    return {
      version: VERSION,
      sceneReady: valid(sc),
      stamps: d ? Object.keys(d.passport).length : 0,
      favorites: d ? d.favorites.length : 0,
      namesKnown: d ? Object.keys(d.names).length : 0,
      moodSchema: true,
    };
  },
});
})();
</script>
<!-- LQ77-MODULE-END -->

<script id="lq81-script">
/* V81 — Field Atlas. Separate save namespace; reads only actual scene coordinates.
   No rewards, teleportation, NPC mutation, route or weather invention. */
(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;else {root.LQ81=api;api.mount(root);}})(typeof window!=='undefined'?window:globalThis,function(){
'use strict';
const VERSION='V81.1 — Cartographer’s Journal', TILE=32, LIMIT=180, NEAR=100;
const num=Number.isFinite, clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const text=(v,n=100)=>String(v??'').trim().slice(0,n);
const record=v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
const dict=v=>record(v)?v:{};
const day=sc=>Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(sc?.store?.game?.day)||1));
const areaId=sc=>text(sc?.area?.id||'',80);
const roomId=sc=>sc?.inside?JSON.stringify([areaId(sc),text(sc.inside.b?.name),sc.inside.floor??0,sc.inside.b?.x??null,sc.inside.b?.y??null]):JSON.stringify([areaId(sc),'outdoor']);
function ready(sc){return !!(areaId(sc)&&record(sc?.store?.game)&&num(sc?.player?.x)&&num(sc?.player?.y)&&num(sc.area?.w)&&num(sc.area?.h)&&!sc.switching&&!sc.sleeping&&!sc.store.frozen);}
function rect(o,unit,offset=0){if(![o?.x,o?.y,o?.w,o?.h].every(num)||o.w<=0||o.h<=0)return null;return {x:offset+o.x*unit,y:o.y*unit,w:o.w*unit,h:o.h*unit};}
const WORDS={shelf:['shelf','kệ','A shelf holds objects or books.'],bookshelf:['bookshelf','giá sách','A bookshelf holds books.'],bookcase:['bookcase','tủ sách','A bookcase stores books.'],desk:['desk','bàn làm việc','A desk is used for studying or working.'],table:['table','bàn','A table has a flat top.'],counter:['counter','quầy','A counter is used for serving customers.'],window:['window','cửa sổ','A window lets light into a room.'],chair:['chair','ghế','A chair is used for sitting.'],clock:['clock','đồng hồ','A clock tells the time.'],plant:['plant','cây','A plant needs light and water.'],crate:['crate','thùng hàng','A crate holds goods.'],computer:['computer','máy tính','A computer processes information.'],board:['board','bảng','A board can display written information.'],lamp:['lamp','đèn','A lamp provides light.'],lantern:['lantern','đèn lồng','A lantern provides light.'],bed:['bed','giường','A bed is used for sleeping.'],tree:['tree','cây thân gỗ','A tree has a trunk and branches.'],pine:['pine','cây thông','A pine is a type of tree.'],fountain:['fountain','đài phun nước','A fountain sends water into the air.'],statue:['statue','tượng','A statue is a sculpture.'],rock:['rock','đá','A rock is a solid natural material.'],boulder:['boulder','tảng đá','A boulder is a large rock.'],sign:['sign','biển chỉ dẫn','A sign displays information.'],barrel:['barrel','thùng tròn','A barrel is a round container.'],stall:['stall','sạp hàng','A stall is a small place for selling goods.'],bridge:['bridge','cầu','A bridge connects two sides.'],fireplace:['fireplace','lò sưởi','A fireplace provides heat.'],cabinet:['cabinet','tủ','A cabinet stores things.'],anvil:['anvil','đe','An anvil is a heavy metal work surface.'],minecart:['minecart','xe goòng','A minecart carries materials on rails.'],altar:['altar','bàn thờ','An altar is a place for ceremonial use.']};
const BUILD=[[/post office|postal/i,['post office','bưu điện','A post office handles letters and parcels.']],[/cafe|café|coffee/i,['café','quán cà phê','A café serves drinks and often food.']],[/library/i,['library','thư viện','A library provides books for reading.']],[/school|academy/i,['school','trường học','A school is a place to learn.']],[/gallery|museum/i,['gallery','phòng trưng bày','A gallery displays works of art.']],[/market|bazaar/i,['market','chợ','A market is a place to buy and sell goods.']],[/shop|store/i,['shop','cửa hàng','A shop sells goods.']],[/office/i,['office','văn phòng','An office is a place for working.']],[/house|cottage|home/i,['house','ngôi nhà','A house is a place to live.']],[/hall/i,['hall','hội trường','A hall is a large room or building.']],[/studio/i,['studio','xưởng sáng tạo','A studio is a workspace for creative work.']],[/station/i,['station','nhà ga','A station is a place where transport services stop.']]];
function word(kind,name){if(kind==='building')return BUILD.find(([r])=>r.test(name))?.[1]||['building','công trình','A building is a structure with walls and a roof.'];return WORDS[kind]||null;}
function objects(sc){if(!ready(sc))return [];const a=areaId(sc), room=roomId(sc), ins=sc.inside, unit=ins?TILE*(num(ins.k)&&ins.k>0?ins.k:1):TILE;const out=[];const used=new Set();
  function add(o,kind,name,source){const r=rect(o,unit,ins?20000:0);if(!r||!name)return;const bx=ins?20000:0,bw=(ins?ins.spec?.w:sc.area.w)*unit,bh=(ins?ins.spec?.h:sc.area.h)*unit;if(!num(bw)||!num(bh)||r.x<bx||r.y<0||r.x+r.w>bx+bw||r.y+r.h>bh)return;const id=JSON.stringify([room,kind,name,o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h]);if(used.has(id))return;used.add(id);let anchor={x:r.x+r.w/2,y:r.y+r.h/2};if(kind==='building'&&num(o.door)&&o.door>=o.x&&o.door<o.x+o.w){anchor={x:(o.door+.5)*TILE,y:(o.y+o.h+.5)*TILE};}out.push({id,area:a,region:text(sc.area.name||a),room,location:text(ins?.b?.name||sc.area.name||a),kind,name,r,anchor,source,vocab:word(kind,name)});}
  if(ins){if(!num(ins.spec?.w)||!num(ins.spec?.h))return [];for(const o of ins.spec.items||[]){let k=text(o.k,40).toLowerCase();if(WORDS[k])add(o,k,text(o.label||k), 'Room items');}}
  else {for(const b of sc.area.buildings||[]){if(text(b.name))add(b,'building',text(b.name),'Map buildings');}for(const o of sc.area.obstacles||[]){let k=text(o.kind||o.k||o.type,40).toLowerCase();if(WORDS[k])add(o,k,text(o.label||k),'Map objects');}}
  return out;
}
function distance(p,r){return Math.hypot(p.x-clamp(p.x,r.x,r.x+r.w),p.y-clamp(p.y,r.y,r.y+r.h));}
function nearby(sc,items=objects(sc)){return items.map(o=>({...o,d:distance(sc.player,o.r)})).filter(o=>o.d<=NEAR).sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));}
function state(sc){const game=sc?.store?.game;if(!record(game))return null;if(!record(game.lq81))game.lq81={};const s=game.lq81;s.version=81;s.discovered=dict(s.discovered);s.deck=dict(s.deck);s.notes=dict(s.notes);s.stats=dict(s.stats);return s;}
function persist(sc){try{sc.store.save?.();}catch{} }
function discover(sc,items=null){if(!ready(sc))return 0;const actual=nearby(sc);const allowed=items?new Set(items.map(o=>o.id)):null;const s=state(sc);let n=0;for(const o of actual){if(allowed&&!allowed.has(o.id))continue;if(o.room!==roomId(sc)||s.discovered[o.id]||Object.keys(s.discovered).length>=LIMIT)continue;s.discovered[o.id]={id:o.id,name:o.name,region:o.region,area:o.area,room:o.room,location:o.location,kind:o.kind,source:o.source,firstDay:day(sc),vocab:o.vocab};n++;}if(n)persist(sc);return n;}
function addCard(sc,id){if(!ready(sc))return false;const s=state(sc),o=s?.discovered[id];if(!Array.isArray(o?.vocab)||o.vocab.length!==3)return false;const [en,vi,example]=o.vocab;const key='word:'+en;if(!s.deck[key]){s.deck[key]={en,vi,example,origin:o.name,region:o.region,due:day(sc),streak:0,attempts:0,correct:0};persist(sc);}return key;}
function optionsFor(card){const entries=[...Object.values(WORDS),...BUILD.map(x=>x[1]),['building','công trình','']];const others=[...new Set(entries.map(x=>x[1]))].filter(v=>v!==card.vi);let h=0;for(const c of card.en)h=(Math.imul(h,31)+c.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;const opts=[card.vi];for(let i=0;opts.length<4&&i<others.length;i++){const v=others[(h+i*7)%others.length];if(!opts.includes(v))opts.push(v);}const j=h%opts.length;[opts[0],opts[j]]=[opts[j],opts[0]];return opts;}
function answer(sc,key,value){if(!ready(sc))return null;const s=state(sc),c=s?.deck[key];if(!record(c)||Number(c.due)>day(sc)||!optionsFor(c).includes(value))return null;const correct=c.vi===value;c.attempts=(Number(c.attempts)||0)+1;c.correct=(Number(c.correct)||0)+(correct?1:0);c.streak=correct?Math.min(5,(Number(c.streak)||0)+1):0;c.due=day(sc)+(correct?[0,1,2,4,7,14][c.streak]:0);persist(sc);return {correct,answer:c.vi,example:c.example,due:c.due};}
function due(sc){const s=state(sc);return Object.entries(s?.deck||{}).filter(([,v])=>record(v)&&v.due<=day(sc)).sort((a,b)=>a[1].due-b[1].due||a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));}
function note(sc,id,value){if(!ready(sc))return false;const s=state(sc);if(!s?.discovered[id])return false;const v=text(value,280);if(!v){delete s.notes[id];}else s.notes[id]={text:v,day:day(sc)};persist(sc);return true;}
function bearing(sc,o){if(!ready(sc)||o?.room!==roomId(sc))return null;const dx=o.anchor.x-sc.player.x,dy=o.anchor.y-sc.player.y;const d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);return {d:Math.round(d/TILE*10)/10,direction:['N','NE','E','SE','S','SW','W','NW'][(Math.round(Math.atan2(dx,-dy)/(Math.PI/4))+8)%8],angle:Math.atan2(dx,-dy),near:distance(sc.player,o.r)<=NEAR};}
function question(sc,o){if(!ready(sc)||o?.room!==roomId(sc)||distance(sc.player,o.r)>NEAR||!o.vocab)return null;const fresh=objects(sc).find(x=>x.id===o.id);if(!fresh)return null;const direction=distance(sc.player,fresh.r)<=TILE?'next to':bearing(sc,fresh).direction;return {word:fresh.vocab[0],vi:fresh.vocab[1],example:fresh.vocab[2],name:fresh.name,direction};}
function fieldChallenge(sc,id){if(!ready(sc))return null;const o=nearby(sc).find(x=>x.id===id);if(!o?.vocab)return null;const [en,vi,example]=o.vocab;const distractors=optionsFor({en,vi}).map(x=>x===vi?en:([...Object.values(WORDS),...BUILD.map(y=>y[1]),['building','công trình','']].find(y=>y[1]===x)?.[0]||x));return {id,room:o.room,name:o.name,prompt:`Which English word matches this nearby object: ${vi}?`,correct:en,options:[...new Set(distractors)],example};}
function fieldAnswer(sc,id,value){const q=fieldChallenge(sc,id);if(!q||!q.options.includes(value))return null;const st=state(sc),ok=value===q.correct;st.stats.fieldAttempts=(Number(st.stats.fieldAttempts)||0)+1;st.stats.fieldCorrect=(Number(st.stats.fieldCorrect)||0)+(ok?1:0);if(ok){discover(sc,[{id}]);addCard(sc,id);}persist(sc);return {ok,answer:q.correct,example:q.example};}
// Atlas projection: world space, room offset, zoom and bounds are explicit.
function mapFrame(sc,W,H,zoom=1,focus=null){
 if(!ready(sc)||!num(W)||!num(H)||W<=40||H<=40)return null;
 const ins=sc.inside,u=ins?TILE*(num(ins.k)&&ins.k>0?ins.k:1):TILE,offset=ins?20000:0;
 const bw=(ins?ins.spec?.w:sc.area.w)*u,bh=(ins?ins.spec?.h:sc.area.h)*u;
 if(!num(bw)||!num(bh)||bw<=0||bh<=0)return null;
 const pad=22,z=clamp(Number(zoom)||1,1,3),scale=Math.min((W-2*pad)/bw,(H-2*pad)/bh)*z;
 const fw=bw*scale,fh=bh*scale,fx=clamp((focus?.x??sc.player.x)-offset,0,bw),fy=clamp(focus?.y??sc.player.y,0,bh);
 const ox=fw<=W-2*pad?(W-fw)/2:clamp(W/2-fx*scale,W-pad-fw,pad);
 const oy=fh<=H-2*pad?(H-fh)/2:clamp(H/2-fy*scale,H-pad-fh,pad);
 return {offset,u,bw,bh,scale,ox,oy,W,H,x:v=>ox+(v-offset)*scale,y:v=>oy+v*scale,world:(x,y)=>({x:(x-ox)/scale+offset,y:(y-oy)/scale})};
}
function mapHit(frame,items,x,y){if(!frame||!num(x)||!num(y)||x<0||y<0||x>frame.W||y>frame.H)return null;const p=frame.world(x,y);return items.filter(o=>o?.r&&distance(p,o.r)*frame.scale<=9).sort((a,b)=>distance(p,a.r)-distance(p,b.r)||(a.r.w*a.r.h-b.r.w*b.r.h)||a.id.localeCompare(b.id))[0]||null;}
function progress(sc,items=objects(sc)){const st=state(sc);const cards=Object.values(st?.deck||{}).filter(record);let attempts=0,correct=0;for(const c of cards){attempts+=Math.max(0,Number(c.attempts)||0);correct+=Math.max(0,Number(c.correct)||0);}const local=items.filter(o=>st?.discovered[o.id]);return {mapped:items.length,local:local.length,total:Object.keys(st?.discovered||{}).length,words:cards.length,due:due(sc).length,practised:cards.filter(c=>c.attempts>0).length,established:cards.filter(c=>c.streak>=3).length,attempts,correct,notes:Object.keys(st?.notes||{}).length};}
function filteredRecords(rows,query='',category='all',room=null){const q=text(query,100).normalize('NFD').replace(/\p{Diacritic}/gu,'').toLowerCase().replace(/đ/g,'d');return rows.filter(o=>(category==='all'||(category==='building'?o.kind==='building':category==='object'?o.kind!=='building':category==='local'?o.room===room:false))&&[o.name,o.region,o.location,...(o.vocab||[])].join(' ').normalize('NFD').replace(/\p{Diacritic}/gu,'').toLowerCase().replace(/đ/g,'d').includes(q)).sort((a,b)=>(b.room===room)-(a.room===room)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));}
function recall(sc,key,value){if(!ready(sc))return null;const c=state(sc)?.deck[key];if(!record(c)||Number(c.due)>day(sc)||typeof value!=='string'||!value.trim())return null;const clean=x=>text(x,100).normalize('NFKC').toLocaleLowerCase('en').replace(/\s+/g,' ');const ok=clean(value)===clean(c.en);const chosen=ok?c.vi:optionsFor(c).find(x=>x!==c.vi);const r=answer(sc,key,chosen);return r?{...r,word:c.en}:null;}
function mount(w){const doc=w.document,$=id=>doc.getElementById(id);const el=(tag,cls,txt)=>{const e=doc.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(txt!==undefined)e.textContent=txt;return e;};const sc=()=>{try{return w.__lifequestGetScene?.();}catch{return null;}};let modal,view,nav,closeBtn,toast,compass,button,tab='near',pin=null,uiScene=null,keyboard=null,priorKeyboard=true,prevFocus=null,questionKey=null,fieldId=null,fieldFeedback='',feedback='',atlasFilter='',lastRoom=null,openDay=0,zoom=1,selected=null,category='all',reviewMode='choice',noteTarget=null;
const visible=e=>!!(e&&e.getClientRects().length&&w.getComputedStyle(e).visibility!=='hidden'&&w.getComputedStyle(e).display!=='none');
function busy(s){return !ready(s)||s.chat?.isOpen||s.journal?.isOpen||s.settings?.isOpen||s.quests?.isOpen||s.helper?.isOpen||s.briefing?.isOpen||w.LQCareerWorlds?.isDialogOpen?.()||w.LQOrigins?.isPlaying?.(s)||w.LQMineIntro?.isPlaying?.(s)||w.LQPast?.isFilming?.()||['chat','l11-shell','lq51-dialog','ll-panel','rpg-panel','v10-panel','lq-title','lq-device','lq-past','lq-art','lq-intro','lq-mine-choice','ai-panel','v8-panel','lq-guide','lq-levels','lq-voice','lq77-replay','lq77-passport','lq77-news'].some(id=>visible($(id)));}
function btn(parent,label,fn,style=''){const b=el('button','lq81-btn '+style,label);b.type='button';b.onclick=fn;parent.append(b);return b;}
function card(parent,title,detail){const c=el('article','lq81-card');c.append(el('h3','',title),el('p','lq81-muted',detail));parent.append(c);return c;}
function title(parent,head,detail){parent.append(el('h2','',head),el('p','lq81-muted',detail));}
function notify(msg){toast.textContent=msg;toast.hidden=false;clearTimeout(notify.t);notify.t=setTimeout(()=>toast.hidden=true,2700);}
function close(){if(!modal||modal.hidden)return;modal.hidden=true;try{if(keyboard){keyboard.resetKeys?.();keyboard.enabled=priorKeyboard;}}catch{}keyboard=null;uiScene=null;try{if(visible(prevFocus))prevFocus.focus({preventScroll:true});else $('l11-menu-btn')?.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch{} }
function show(t='near'){const s=sc();if(!ready(s))return false;if(visible($('l11-shell')))$('l11-close')?.click();if(busy(s))return false;if(!modal.hidden){tab=t;render();return true;}prevFocus=doc.activeElement;uiScene=s;lastRoom=roomId(s);openDay=day(s);zoom=1;selected=null;keyboard=s.input?.keyboard;priorKeyboard=keyboard?.enabled;try{keyboard?.resetKeys?.();if(keyboard)keyboard.enabled=false;const c=w.__townControls;if(c){c.up=c.down=c.left=c.right=false;c.actionRequested=false;}}catch{}tab=t;feedback='';questionKey=null;fieldId=null;fieldFeedback='';discover(s);modal.hidden=false;render();closeBtn.focus();return true;}
function select(t){tab=t;feedback='';questionKey=null;fieldId=null;fieldFeedback='';render();view.scrollTop=0;nav.querySelector(`[data-tab="${t}"]`)?.focus();}
function drawMap(canvas,s,items){
 const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return null;
 const pick=items.find(o=>o.id===selected),f=mapFrame(s,canvas.width,canvas.height,zoom,pick?.anchor);if(!f)return null;
 const {W,H,scale,ox,oy,bw,bh,offset,u:unit,x,y}=f,ins=s.inside,st=state(s);
 const fill=(a,b,c,d,col)=>{ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillRect(a,b,c,d);};
 const line=(a,b,c,d,col,width=1)=>{ctx.strokeStyle=col;ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a,b);ctx.lineTo(c,d);ctx.stroke();};
 fill(0,0,W,H,'#101e24');ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(ox,oy,bw*scale,bh*scale);ctx.clip();
 fill(ox,oy,bw*scale,bh*scale,ins?'#544e42':'#354d44');
 // Fine cartographic coordinate grid, not physical terrain.
 const step=Math.max(16,unit*scale*2);for(let a=ox+step;a<Math.min(W,ox+bw*scale);a+=step)line(a,Math.max(0,oy),a,Math.min(H,oy+bh*scale),'#b6c5a20c');for(let b=oy+step;b<Math.min(H,oy+bh*scale);b+=step)line(Math.max(0,ox),b,Math.min(W,ox+bw*scale),b,'#b6c5a20c');
 function areaRect(v,color,edge){const q=rect(v,unit,offset);if(!q)return;const X=x(q.x),Y=y(q.y),A=q.w*scale,B=q.h*scale;fill(X,Y,A,B,color);if(edge){ctx.strokeStyle=edge;ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(X+.5,Y+.5,A-1,B-1);}}
 if(!ins){const a=s.area;areaRect(a.park,'#48684d','#78936b');areaRect(a.garden,'#48684d','#78936b');areaRect(a.campus,'#77735b','#a49b77');areaRect(a.lake,'#31566e','#70a3b0');for(const v of a.ponds||[])areaRect(v,'#31566e','#70a3b0');for(const r of a.roadsH||[])areaRect({x:r.x0??0,y:r.y,w:(r.x1??a.w)-(r.x0??0),h:r.h},'#777b70','#93998a');for(const r of a.roadsV||[])areaRect({x:r.x,y:r.y0??0,w:r.w,h:(r.y1??a.h)-(r.y0??0)},'#777b70','#93998a');}
 else for(const q of ins.spec.items||[]){if(['rug','carpet','mat'].includes(q.k))areaRect(q,'#775953','#9c7667');else if(['pool','pond','water'].includes(q.k))areaRect(q,'#31566e','#70a3b0');}
 let labels=[];for(const o of items){const found=!!st.discovered[o.id],active=o.id===selected||o.id===pin;const X=x(o.r.x),Y=y(o.r.y),A=Math.max(2,o.r.w*scale),B=Math.max(2,o.r.h*scale);if(X+A<0||Y+B<0||X>W||Y>H)continue;
 const color=active?'#e4b76d':found?'#a0bc98':o.kind==='building'?'#637d79':'#4d6e56';
 fill(X+2,Y+3,A,B,'#10262666');fill(X,Y,A,B,color);
 if(o.kind==='building'){fill(X+1,Y+1,Math.max(1,A-2),Math.max(1,B*.38),'#ffffff16');ctx.strokeStyle=active?'#ffdc91':'#d3dfc15e';ctx.lineWidth=active?2:1;ctx.strokeRect(X+.5,Y+.5,Math.max(0,A-1),Math.max(0,B-1));if(A>18&&B>12)line(X+A/2,Y+3,X+A/2,Y+B-3,'#263b3c65');}
 else if(A>=6&&B>=6){ctx.fillStyle='#d1e3c6a0';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X+A/2,Y+B/2,Math.min(A,B)/4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
 if(active||found&&o.kind==='building')labels.push({name:o.name,x:X+A/2,y:Y-7,active});
 }
 ctx.restore();ctx.strokeStyle='#b5c8a66b';ctx.strokeRect(ox+.5,oy+.5,bw*scale,bh*scale);
 if(pick){ctx.setLineDash([4,5]);line(x(s.player.x),y(s.player.y),x(pick.anchor.x),y(pick.anchor.y),'#f3cc85a0',1.5);ctx.setLineDash([]);}
 ctx.font='600 13px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';let boxes=[];for(const l of labels.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.active)-Number(a.active))){let ww=Math.min(200,ctx.measureText(l.name).width+12),xx=clamp(l.x-ww/2,4,W-ww-4),yy=clamp(l.y-15,4,H-25);if(!l.active&&boxes.some(q=>xx<q.x+q.w&&xx+ww>q.x&&yy<q.y+20&&yy+20>q.y))continue;boxes.push({x:xx,y:yy,w:ww});fill(xx,yy,ww,21,l.active?'#e4b76d':'#14292ce8');ctx.fillStyle=l.active?'#18292b':'#e4ead7';ctx.fillText(l.name,xx+ww/2,yy+15,ww-8);}
 let px=x(s.player.x),py=y(s.player.y);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,10,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='#f9ead143';ctx.fill();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='#fff1bc';ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle='#172c32';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.stroke();
 // Compass is map north, not player facing.
 fill(W-40,8,32,49,'#10232be8');ctx.fillStyle='#f5d6a0';ctx.font='700 12px system-ui';ctx.fillText('N',W-24,20);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W-24,25);ctx.lineTo(W-30,43);ctx.lineTo(W-24,39);ctx.lineTo(W-18,43);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 return f;
}
function overview(parent,p){const row=el('div','lq811-metrics');for(const [label,value,small] of [['Discovered',p.total,`${p.local}/${p.mapped} here`],['Vocabulary',p.words,`${p.due} due today`],['Field notes',p.notes,`${p.practised} words practised`]]){const box=el('div','lq811-metric');box.append(el('span','',label),el('strong','',String(value)),el('small','',small));row.append(box);}parent.append(row);}
function render(){const s=sc();if(!modal||modal.hidden||s!==uiScene||roomId(s)!==lastRoom||day(s)!==openDay){close();return;}const st=state(s),items=objects(s),near=nearby(s,items),saved=Object.values(st.discovered);view.replaceChildren();for(const b of nav.querySelectorAll('button'))b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b.dataset.tab===tab));const status=$('lq81-location');status.textContent=(s.inside?.b?.name||s.area.name||s.area.id)+' · Day '+day(s);$('lq81-count').textContent=`${saved.length} places & objects · ${due(s).length} words due`;
if(tab==='near'){title(view,'Observe your surroundings','Walk close to a real object, learn its English name and add it to your journal. Only objects within 100 world pixels are shown.');overview(view,progress(s,items));if(fieldFeedback)view.append(el('div','lq81-feedback',fieldFeedback));if(!near.length)card(view,'Nothing nearby','Close the atlas and walk closer to a building or an object.');for(const o of near.slice(0,8)){const c=card(view,o.name,`${o.kind==='building'?'Building':o.kind} · ${Math.round(o.d/TILE*10)/10} tiles from object · ${o.source}`);if(o.vocab){const q=question(s,o);if(q){if(fieldId===o.id){const challenge=fieldChallenge(s,o.id);if(challenge){c.append(el('p','lq81-word',challenge.prompt));for(const choice of challenge.options)btn(c,choice,()=>{const result=fieldAnswer(sc(),o.id,choice);fieldFeedback=result?(result.ok?'Correct! ':'Not quite. ')+result.answer+' · '+result.example:'This object is no longer nearby.';if(result?.ok)fieldId=null;render();},'choice');}}else {const l=el('div','lq81-word',`${q.word} — ${q.vi}`);c.append(l,el('p','',q.example));btn(c,'Field challenge',()=>{fieldId=o.id;fieldFeedback='';render();},'primary');}btn(c,'Save word & practise',()=>{if(!question(sc(),o)){feedback='This object is no longer nearby.';render();return;}discover(s,[o]);questionKey=addCard(s,o.id);tab='review';render();},'primary');}}if(o.kind==='building')btn(c,'Set guide marker',()=>{pin=o.id;notify('Guide marker set');close();});}
 }else if(tab==='atlas'){
 title(view,'Cartographer’s atlas','Inspect the current area. The map is a coordinate-based schematic; the dotted line is a bearing, not a walking route.');overview(view,progress(s,items));
 const layout=el('div','lq811-maplayout'),panel=el('section','lq811-mappanel'),inspector=el('aside','lq811-inspector');layout.append(panel,inspector);view.append(layout);
 const tools=el('div','lq811-toolbar');tools.append(el('span','lq811-caption','CURRENT AREA'));btn(tools,'−',()=>{zoom=Math.max(1,zoom-.5);update();}).setAttribute('aria-label','Zoom out');const zoomLabel=el('span','lq811-zoom');tools.append(zoomLabel);btn(tools,'+',()=>{zoom=Math.min(3,zoom+.5);update();}).setAttribute('aria-label','Zoom in');btn(tools,'Reset',()=>{zoom=1;selected=null;update();});panel.append(tools);
 const map=el('canvas','lq81-map');map.width=900;map.height=350;map.setAttribute('role','img');map.setAttribute('aria-label','Current map schematic showing player and known object coordinates');panel.append(map);
 const legend=el('div','lq811-legend');for(const [col,label] of [['#fff1bc','You'],['#e4b76d','Selected'],['#a0bc98','Discovered'],['#637d79','Mapped'],['#31566e','Water']]){const item=el('span','');const dot=el('i','');dot.style.background=col;item.append(dot,doc.createTextNode(label));legend.append(item);}panel.append(legend);
 let frame;
 function update(){frame=drawMap(map,s,items);zoomLabel.textContent=zoom.toFixed(1)+'×';inspector.replaceChildren();const o=items.find(x=>x.id===selected);inspector.append(el('span','lq811-caption','PLACE INSPECTOR'));if(!o){inspector.append(el('h3','','Choose a place'),el('p','lq81-muted','Click the map or select a location below. Zoom centers on the selected place or your position.'));return;}
 const found=st.discovered[o.id],b=bearing(s,o);inspector.append(el('h3','',o.name),el('p','lq811-type',found?'Discovered':'Mapped · not yet approached'),el('p','lq81-muted',o.location+' · '+o.source),el('p','lq81-word',b?`${b.direction} · ${b.d} tiles to marker`:'Direction unavailable'));if(found&&o.vocab){inspector.append(el('p','',`${o.vocab[0]} — ${o.vocab[1]}`),el('p','lq81-muted',o.vocab[2]));btn(inspector,'Save vocabulary',()=>{addCard(s,o.id);notify('Saved to review deck');});}if(found)btn(inspector,'Guide to here',()=>{pin=o.id;notify('Guide marker set');close();},'primary');else inspector.append(el('p','lq81-muted','Approach this place in the world to save it to your atlas.'));}
 map.style.cursor='crosshair';map.onclick=e=>{const r=map.getBoundingClientRect();const o=mapHit(frame,items,(e.clientX-r.left)*map.width/r.width,(e.clientY-r.top)*map.height/r.height);if(o){selected=o.id;update();}};update();
 const filters=el('div','lq811-filters');const input=el('input','lq81-input');input.type='search';input.placeholder='Search discovered places…';input.setAttribute('aria-label','Search discovered places');input.value=atlasFilter;filters.append(input);const sel=el('select','lq81-input');sel.setAttribute('aria-label','Filter discoveries');for(const [id,label] of [['all','All discoveries'],['local','This area / room'],['building','Buildings'],['object','Objects']]){const opt=el('option','',label);opt.value=id;sel.append(opt);}sel.value=category;filters.append(sel);view.append(filters);
 const list=el('div','lq81-list');view.append(list);const fill=()=>{list.replaceChildren();const rows=filteredRecords(saved,atlasFilter,category,lastRoom);if(!rows.length)card(list,'No matching discoveries','Walk closer to a place, or change the search filter.');for(const o of rows.slice(0,200)){const c=card(list,o.name,`${o.region} · ${o.location} · first seen day ${o.firstDay}`);const live=items.find(t=>t.id===o.id);if(live){btn(c,'Inspect on map',()=>{selected=o.id;update();layout.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});});btn(c,'Guide to here',()=>{pin=o.id;notify('Guide marker set');close();},'primary');}if(o.vocab)btn(c,'Save vocabulary',()=>{addCard(s,o.id);notify('Saved to review deck');});btn(c,'Field note',()=>{noteTarget=o.id;tab='notes';render();const field=[...view.querySelectorAll('[data-note-id]')].find(e=>e.dataset.noteId===o.id);field?.focus();});}};input.oninput=()=>{atlasFilter=input.value;fill();};sel.onchange=()=>{category=sel.value;fill();};fill();
}else if(tab==='review'){title(view,'Review deck','Recall words discovered on the map. Correct answers schedule the next review in game days; mistakes stay due today.');overview(view,progress(s,items));const modes=el('div','lq811-toolbar');modes.append(el('span','lq811-caption','PRACTICE MODE'));for(const [id,label] of [['choice','Recognition'],['recall','Write in English']]){const m=btn(modes,label,()=>{reviewMode=id;feedback='';render();});m.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(reviewMode===id));}view.append(modes);const dueRows=due(s);const stats=Object.values(st.deck);view.append(el('p','lq81-muted',`${stats.length} words saved · ${dueRows.length} due · ${stats.reduce((a,c)=>a+(Number(c.correct)||0),0)} correct reviews · ${st.stats.fieldCorrect||0}/${st.stats.fieldAttempts||0} field challenges correct`));if(feedback)view.append(el('div','lq81-feedback',feedback));const active=dueRows.find(([key])=>key===questionKey)||dueRows[0];if(active){const [key,c]=active;const box=card(view,reviewMode==='recall'?`Write the English word for “${c.vi}”`:`What does “${c.en}” mean?`,reviewMode==='recall'?c.region:`${c.origin} · ${c.region}`);function done(r){if(!r)return;feedback=(r.correct?'Correct! ':'Not quite. ')+`${c.en} = ${r.answer}. ${r.example} Next review: day ${r.due}.`;questionKey=null;render();}if(reviewMode==='recall'){const inp=el('input','lq81-input');inp.placeholder='Type the English word';inp.setAttribute('aria-label','English answer');inp.autocomplete='off';inp.spellcheck=false;box.append(inp);const check=()=>{if(inp.value.trim())done(recall(s,key,inp.value));};btn(box,'Check answer',check,'primary');inp.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();check();}});}else for(const opt of optionsFor(c))btn(box,opt,()=>done(answer(s,key,opt)),'choice');}else card(view,'You’re caught up','Find more objects in the world or return on a later in-game day.');for(const [key,c] of Object.entries(st.deck).slice(0,40)){const x=card(view,c.en,`Review day ${c.due} · ${c.correct||0}/${c.attempts||0} correct`);if(c.due<=day(s))btn(x,'Practise this word',()=>{questionKey=key;feedback='';render();view.scrollTop=0;});}
}else if(tab==='notes'){title(view,'Field notebook','Write your own observations in English. These notes are personal statements, not verified world facts or AI-graded answers.');if(!saved.length)card(view,'No observations yet','Approach objects to unlock notebook entries.');for(const o of saved.slice().reverse().sort((a,b)=>Number(b.id===noteTarget)-Number(a.id===noteTarget)).slice(0,180)){const c=card(view,o.name,`${o.location} · ${o.region}`);const a=el('textarea','lq81-input');a.rows=2;a.maxLength=280;a.dataset.noteId=o.id;a.placeholder='I noticed… / I can see…';a.value=st.notes[o.id]?.text||'';a.setAttribute('aria-label','Note about '+o.name);const meter=el('span','lq811-caption',`${a.value.length}/280 characters`);a.oninput=()=>meter.textContent=`${a.value.length}/280 characters`;c.append(a,meter);btn(c,'Save note',()=>{note(s,o.id,a.value);notify('Note saved');});}}
}
function tick(){const s=sc();if(!modal)return;const available=ready(s)&&!busy(s);const opened=!modal.hidden;if(opened&&(s!==uiScene||!ready(s)||roomId(s)!==lastRoom||day(s)!==openDay))close();button.hidden=!available||opened;const menu=$('l11-nav');if(menu&&!menu.querySelector('#lq81-nav')){const b=btn(menu,'✦ Field Atlas',()=>show('near'));b.id='lq81-nav';}if(available&&!opened){discover(s);if(pin){const items=objects(s);const o=items.find(x=>x.id===pin);const b=o&&bearing(s,o);if(!b){pin=null;compass.hidden=true;}else{compass.hidden=false;compass.querySelector('.lq81-arrow').style.transform=`rotate(${b.angle}rad)`;compass.querySelector('.lq81-pin-text').textContent=b.near?`${o.name} · Nearby`:`${o.name} · ${b.direction} · ${b.d} tiles to marker`;}}else compass.hidden=true;}else compass.hidden=true;}
function init(){if($('lq81-modal'))return;modal=el('section','lq81-modal');modal.id='lq81-modal';modal.hidden=true;modal.setAttribute('role','dialog');modal.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');modal.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','lq81-title');const shell=el('div','lq81-shell');const header=el('header','lq81-header');const heading=el('div','');heading.append(el('div','lq81-kicker','LIFEQUEST AI / V81.1'),el('h1','','Cartographer’s Journal'));heading.lastChild.id='lq81-title';const loc=el('p','lq81-muted','');loc.id='lq81-location';heading.append(loc);header.append(heading);closeBtn=btn(header,'Close ×',close);const count=el('div','lq81-count');count.id='lq81-count';nav=el('nav','lq81-nav');nav.setAttribute('aria-label','Field explorer sections');for(const [k,label] of [['near','Nearby'],['atlas','Atlas'],['review','Review'],['notes','Notebook']]){const b=btn(nav,label,()=>select(k));b.dataset.tab=k;}view=el('main','lq81-view');shell.append(header,count,nav,view);modal.append(shell);doc.body.append(modal);button=el('button','lq81-launch','✦ Explore');button.id='lq81-launch';button.type='button';button.hidden=true;button.title='Open Field Explorer';button.onclick=()=>show();const dock=$('l11-brand');if(dock){button.dataset.docked='true';dock.append(button);}else doc.body.append(button);compass=el('div','lq81-compass');compass.hidden=true;compass.append(el('span','lq81-arrow','↑'),el('span','lq81-pin-text',''));btn(compass,'×',()=>{pin=null;compass.hidden=true;});if(dock){compass.dataset.docked='true';dock.append(compass);}else doc.body.append(compass);toast=el('div','lq81-toast');toast.hidden=true;toast.setAttribute('role','status');doc.body.append(toast);w.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(modal.hidden)return;if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();close();return;}if(e.key==='Tab'){const f=[...modal.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),input,textarea,select')].filter(visible);if(!f.length)return;const i=f.indexOf(doc.activeElement);if((e.shiftKey&&i<=0)||(!e.shiftKey&&(i<0||i===f.length-1))){e.preventDefault();f[e.shiftKey?f.length-1:0].focus();}}if(e.key==='Enter'&&doc.activeElement?.getAttribute('aria-label')==='English answer'){e.preventDefault();view.querySelector('button.primary')?.click();}e.stopImmediatePropagation();},true);w.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{if(!modal.hidden)e.stopImmediatePropagation();},true);w.setInterval(()=>{if(!doc.hidden)try{tick();}catch(e){console.warn('Field Atlas:',e.message);}},1000);tick();}
if(doc.readyState==='loading')doc.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();return {show,close,tick};}
return {VERSION,ready,roomId,objects,distance,nearby,state,discover,addCard,answer,due,optionsFor,note,bearing,question,fieldChallenge,fieldAnswer,mapFrame,mapHit,progress,filteredRecords,recall,mount};
});

(function (root, create) {

  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = content;
  else root.LQValleyContent = content;
})(globalThis, function () {
  'use strict';

  const ID = 'forgotten-valley';
  const TILE = 32;
  const W = 264;
  const OLD_W=168;
  const H = 138;
  const WorldConfig = Object.freeze({width:W,height:H,tileSize:TILE,chunkSize:16,baseWidth:56,baseHeight:46,seed:8501,generatorVersion:1});
  const legacy=(x,y)=>x<WorldConfig.baseWidth&&y<WorldConfig.baseHeight;
  const hash=(x,y,salt=0)=>{let n=Math.imul(x+WorldConfig.seed,374761393)^Math.imul(y+salt,668265263);n=Math.imul(n^(n>>>13),1274126177);return ((n^(n>>>16))>>>0)/4294967296;};
  const worldToTile=(x,y)=>tile(Math.floor(x/TILE),Math.floor(y/TILE));
  const tileToChunk=(x,y)=>tile(Math.floor(x/WorldConfig.chunkSize),Math.floor(y/WorldConfig.chunkSize));
